ArGuard Shared Task: Harmful Content Detection in Arabic Memes and LLM Prompts
Authors: Firoj Alam, Md. Rafiul Biswas, Mohamed Bayan Kmainasi, Ali Ezzat Shahroor, Hamdy Mubarak, George Mikros, Abul Hasnat, Wajdi Zaghouani
First: 2026-09-24T10:22:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-24T10:22:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
ArGuard is a shared task on harmful content detection in Arabic memes and LLM prompts. It includes two tracks: Track A focuses on multimodal hate detection in Arabic memes, while Track B addresses harmful prompt detection for Arabic LLM safety evaluation. In total, 58 teams registered, 35 participated in the final evaluation, and 27 submitted system-description papers. Participating teams explored models such as AraBERT, Jais, and Qwen3-VL. The best systems achieved macro-F1 scores of 0.823 on A1, 0.419 on A2, 0.984 on B1, and 0.790 on B2. Fine-grained meme classification in A2 was the most challenging setting, partly due to sparse labels and train-test distribution shifts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ArGuard共享任务：阿拉伯语表情包和LLM提示中的有害内容检测
ArGuard是一个关于阿拉伯语表情包和LLM提示中有害内容检测的共享任务。它包括两个赛道：A赛道专注于阿拉伯语表情包中的多模态仇恨检测，而B赛道则针对阿拉伯语LLM安全评估中的有害提示检测。共有58支队伍注册，35支参与了最终评估，27支提交了系统描述论文。参与的团队探索了AraBERT、Jais和Qwen3-VL等模型。最佳系统在A1上达到了0.823的宏F1分数，在A2上为0.419，在B1上为0.984，在B2上为0.790。A2中的细粒度表情包分类是最具挑战性的设置，部分原因是标签稀疏和训练-测试分布的变化。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the growing concern of harmful content in Arabic memes and prompts generated by large language models (LLMs), highlighting the need for effective detection methods in this context. Previous approaches lacked specificity and robustness, particularly in handling multimodal data and the nuances of Arabic language, which the proposed ArGuard shared task aims to improve upon by providing a structured evaluation framework. The contribution of the paper lies in organizing a competitive environment for 58 teams to develop and assess their models, including AraBERT and Jais, against established benchmarks. The methodology involves two tracks focusing on hate detection in memes and harmful prompt detection, with the best systems achieving macro-F1 scores of 0.823 and 0.984 respectively, demonstrating significant performance in detecting harmful content and supporting the task's goals effectively.
本研究关注阿拉伯表情包和大型语言模型（LLM）生成的提示中有害内容的检测，这对在线安全和社会互动构成了风险。以往的方法缺乏有效的多模态方法，并且在视觉和文本内容中的仇恨检测复杂性方面存在困难，尤其是在阿拉伯语中。提出的ArGuard共享任务引入了一个结构化的评估框架，允许比较包括AraBERT和Jais在内的各种模型，以应对这些挑战。该方法论包括两个轨道，分别关注多模态仇恨检测和有害提示检测，共有58个团队参与，取得了显著的宏F1分数，例如A1轨道的0.823和B1轨道的0.984，证明了所提方法在增强这些背景下的检测能力方面的有效性。
FinRED: An Expert-Guided Benchmark Generation and Evaluation Framework for Financial LLM Red-Teaming
Authors: Chaeyun Kim, Daeyoung Park, Junghwan Kim, Jinyoung Jeong, Eunji Song, Yongtaek Lim, Minwoo Kim
First: 2026-06-18T07:46:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-22T05:37:13+00:00
Comments: Accepted at IEEE ICDM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Existing safety benchmarks target general adversarial scenarios but miss finance-specific risks. Financial LLMs face regulatory compliance violations, fraud facilitation, and systemic trust erosion that require targeted evaluation. We introduce FinRED, an expert-guided red-teaming framework for financial LLM safety evaluation developed with financial experts. FinRED uses a novel two-level taxonomy mapping global standards (e.g., FATF and EU DORA) to threats ranging from regulatory evasion to complex fraud, integrated with a scalable pipeline that converts real financial documents into context-rich red-teaming Behavioral Prompts (seeds) through an expert-defined schema. Rigorous expert validation confirms seed plausibility and realism for meaningful LLM safety evaluation. We also provide an expert-validated, finance-specific rubric that goes beyond disclaimer checks, aligns more closely with human experts than static one-size-fits-all rubrics, and reduces critical false negatives from 28 to 12. Aligned with internationally adopted risk-management and information-security standards (e.g., ISO/IEC 27001), FinRED is deployed in South Korea's Financial Security Institute (FSI) regulatory sandbox for generative AI security evaluation in real financial services. To mitigate dual-use risks, the dataset, generation pipeline, prompt template, and evaluation framework are gated for qualified researchers at https://github.com/selectstar-ai/FinRED-paper and https://huggingface.co/datasets/datumo/FinRED.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FinRED：一个专家指导的金融LLM红队评估基准生成框架
现有的安全基准针对一般对抗场景，但忽视了金融特定风险。金融LLM面临合规性违规、欺诈促进和系统性信任侵蚀等问题，需要针对性评估。我们引入了FinRED，这是一个由金融专家开发的专家指导的金融LLM安全评估红队框架。FinRED使用一种新颖的两级分类法，将全球标准（如FATF和EU DORA）映射到从合规规避到复杂欺诈的威胁，结合一个可扩展的管道，通过专家定义的模式将真实金融文档转换为上下文丰富的红队行为提示（种子）。严格的专家验证确认了种子的合理性和现实性，以便进行有意义的LLM安全评估。我们还提供了一个经过专家验证的金融特定评分标准，超越了免责声明检查，更加贴近人类专家，而不是静态的一刀切评分标准，并将关键的假阴性从28减少到12。FinRED与国际公认的风险管理和信息安全标准（如ISO/IEC 27001）保持一致，已在韩国金融安全研究所（FSI）的监管沙箱中部署，用于真实金融服务中的生成AI安全评估。为了减轻双重用途风险，数据集、生成管道、提示模板和评估框架对合格研究人员进行了限制，网址为https://github.com/selectstar-ai/FinRED-paper和https://huggingface.co/datasets/datumo/FinRED。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the inadequacy of existing safety benchmarks for financial large language models (LLMs), which often overlook finance-specific risks such as regulatory compliance violations and fraud. Previous methods primarily focused on general adversarial scenarios, failing to provide targeted evaluations for financial contexts. The proposed FinRED framework distinguishes itself by employing an expert-guided approach that maps global standards to specific financial threats, creating context-rich red-teaming Behavioral Prompts through a structured schema. This methodology includes rigorous expert validation to ensure the realism and plausibility of the prompts, leading to a finance-specific rubric that significantly reduces false negatives in safety evaluations. FinRED has been implemented in South Korea's Financial Security Institute regulatory sandbox, demonstrating its effectiveness in enhancing generative AI security evaluations in financial services, thus supporting its goal of improving financial LLM safety assessment.
本文解决了现有金融大型语言模型（LLM）安全基准的不足，这些基准通常忽视了诸如合规违规和欺诈等特定金融风险。以往的方法主要集中在一般对抗场景，导致金融环境下的评估不够充分。所提出的FinRED框架在金融专家的指导下开发，引入了一个两级分类法，将全球标准映射到特定威胁，并利用可扩展的管道从真实金融文档生成上下文丰富的红队提示。这种专家指导的方法不仅增强了评估种子的现实性和可信度，还显著减少了安全评估中的关键假阴性，从28减少到12。该方法经过专家审查验证，并在韩国金融安全研究所实施，用于生成性人工智能安全评估，从而支持其在金融服务中改善安全评估的目标。
BreakFun: Jailbreaking LLMs via Object Instantiation under Simulated Code Execution
Authors: Amirkia Rafiei Oskooei, Mehmet S. Aktas
First: 2025-10-19T11:27:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-18T18:53:12+00:00
Comments: Accepted to AACL-IJCNLP 2026 Findings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely used because they process structures, syntax and code well, but this same ability also makes them paradoxically vulnerable. We introduce BreakFun, a jailbreak method that frames a harmful request as code-execution simulation. The prompt gives the model a benign Python class definition, the "Trojan Schema", and asks what that code would print if it ran. To answer, the model must create an object from the class and invent a value for each field, and the adversarial field names steer those values toward the attacker's goal. The harmful content therefore appears as a side-effect of the simulated instantiation, not as a direct answer. A three-part prompt carries the attack: an innocent frame, the Trojan Schema, and a Chain-of-Thought distraction. On JailbreakBench, BreakFun reaches an average attack success rate of 89% across 13 open-weight and commercial models (open-weight ~98%, API systems ~78%), and 100% on several. An ablation identifies the Trojan Schema as the strongest contributing component. As a counter-ablation probe we introduce Adversarial Prompt Deconstruction (APD), in which a second LLM writes out all readable text in the prompt before it judges safety. APD improves detection across three model families, and a probe-specific ablation shows that the transcription step gives most of the gain. Our findings show that LLM safety must account for content that a simulation task produces as a side-effect, not only for content that a user requests directly.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BreakFun：通过模拟代码执行中的对象实例化破解LLM
大型语言模型（LLM）因其良好的结构、语法和代码处理能力而被广泛使用，但这种能力也使其在某种程度上变得脆弱。我们介绍了BreakFun，这是一种将有害请求框架化为代码执行模拟的越狱方法。提示给模型一个无害的Python类定义，即“特洛伊模式”，并询问如果运行该代码会打印什么。为了回答，模型必须从类中创建一个对象并为每个字段发明一个值，而对抗性字段名称则将这些值引导至攻击者的目标。因此，有害内容作为模拟实例化的副作用出现，而不是直接答案。攻击通过三部分提示进行：一个无辜的框架、特洛伊模式和一个思维链干扰。在JailbreakBench上，BreakFun在13个开放权重和商业模型中达到了89%的平均攻击成功率（开放权重约98%，API系统约78%），并在多个模型上达到了100%。消融实验确定特洛伊模式是最强的贡献成分。作为对抗消融探针，我们引入了对抗性提示解构（APD），其中第二个LLM在判断安全性之前写出提示中的所有可读文本。APD提高了三个模型家族的检测能力，特定探针的消融显示转录步骤带来了大部分增益。我们的研究结果表明，LLM安全性必须考虑模拟任务作为副作用产生的内容，而不仅仅是用户直接请求的内容。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the vulnerability of Large Language Models (LLMs) to harmful requests due to their proficiency in processing code and structures. Previous methods for mitigating such vulnerabilities have been inadequate, as they often fail to account for the nuanced ways in which LLMs can be manipulated. The proposed approach, BreakFun, innovatively frames harmful requests as code-execution simulations, utilizing a benign Python class definition called the 'Trojan Schema' to guide the model's output towards the attacker's objectives without direct prompts. The methodology involves a three-part prompt that combines an innocent framing, the Trojan Schema, and a Chain-of-Thought distraction. BreakFun achieves an average attack success rate of 89% across various models, with some reaching 100%, demonstrating its effectiveness in exploiting LLM vulnerabilities while also highlighting the need for safety measures that consider side-effect outputs from simulations.
本研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）因其有效处理代码和结构的能力而面临的脆弱性。以往的缓解方法不足，因为它们往往未能考虑到LLMs被操控的细微方式。提出的方法BreakFun创新性地将有害请求框架化为代码执行模拟，利用一个无害的Python类定义来引导模型的响应朝向攻击者的目标，而不是直接提示。该方法论涉及一个三部分提示，包括无害的框架、'特洛伊模式'和思维链分散注意力。BreakFun在多个模型中展示了89%的高平均攻击成功率，特定模型甚至达到100%，表明该方法有效支持其揭示LLM脆弱性的目标。此外，引入的对抗性提示解构（APD）增强了检测能力，显示安全措施必须考虑模拟任务的副作用以及直接用户请求。
Local Sparsity Enables Unsupervised LLM Safety Detection
Authors: Xin Chen, Gil Kur, Alexander Shevchenko, Andreas Krause
First: 2026-09-17T12:23:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-17T12:23:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deployment-time safety methods for large language models (LLMs) are predominantly supervised and assume access to unsafe training data. Nevertheless, new attacks and harm categories regularly arise, not captured by models trained in such a supervised fashion. An alternative approach is to view this problem through the lens of anomaly detection, namely, to rely solely on modeling safe data and flagging out-of-distribution inputs. However, LLM activations lie in a high-dimensional space, raising concerns about whether anomaly detection is statistically feasible. We show that, under the linear representation hypothesis (LRH), there may indeed be hope. In the LRH concept space, which is typically recovered via a sparse autoencoder (SAE), nearby points share a small common active support. Using this local sparsity insight, we propose a framework for locally masked SAE-based anomaly detection, supported by theoretical justifications. We validate it on various architectures and datasets, including both capability-testing datasets and safety-specific datasets. Finally, when we allow algorithms to use 1% out-of-distribution data for calibration, locally sparse methods achieve near-optimal performance, demonstrating their ability to capture meaningful safety information while using only 1-2% of SAE neurons for computation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：局部稀疏性使无监督LLM安全检测成为可能
大型语言模型（LLMs）的部署时安全方法主要是监督性的，并假设可以访问不安全的训练数据。然而，新攻击和伤害类别定期出现，这些在以这种监督方式训练的模型中并未被捕获。另一种方法是通过异常检测的视角来看待这个问题，即仅依赖于建模安全数据并标记分布外输入。然而，LLM激活位于高维空间，这引发了关于异常检测在统计上是否可行的担忧。我们表明，在线性表示假设（LRH）下，确实可能存在希望。在LRH概念空间中，通常通过稀疏自编码器（SAE）恢复，附近点共享一个小的共同活跃支持。利用这一局部稀疏性洞察，我们提出了一个基于局部掩蔽SAE的异常检测框架，并提供了理论依据。我们在各种架构和数据集上进行了验证，包括能力测试数据集和安全特定数据集。最后，当我们允许算法使用1%的分布外数据进行校准时，局部稀疏方法实现了接近最佳的性能，展示了它们在仅使用1-2%的SAE神经元进行计算时捕获有意义的安全信息的能力。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the limitations of existing supervised safety methods for large language models (LLMs), which rely on unsafe training data and struggle to adapt to new attacks and harm categories. Previous methods have primarily focused on supervised learning, which can be inadequate for evolving threats. This paper proposes a novel approach based on anomaly detection, leveraging the concept of local sparsity in high-dimensional spaces to identify out-of-distribution inputs without needing unsafe data. The methodology involves a locally masked sparse autoencoder (SAE) for anomaly detection, supported by theoretical justifications. The experiments demonstrate that this approach achieves near-optimal performance on various datasets, including safety-specific ones, while only utilizing 1-2% of SAE neurons, effectively capturing essential safety information and supporting the proposed goals.
本研究解决了现有大型语言模型（LLMs）监督安全方法的局限性，这些方法依赖于不安全的训练数据，无法适应新攻击类型和危害类别。过去的方法在LLM激活的高维特性下面临统计挑战，使得异常检测变得困难。所提出的方法将重点转向使用局部掩蔽稀疏自编码器（SAE）的异常检测，利用线性表示假设（LRH）有效建模安全数据并识别分布外输入。这种方法的动机充分，因为它允许在不需要大量不安全数据的情况下检测安全问题。本文贡献了一种新颖的框架，在各种数据集上表现出色，在安全检测中实现了近乎最佳的结果，同时仅使用1-2%的SAE神经元进行计算，从而支持了在LLMs中有效监测安全的目标。
From Intent to Action: Benchmarking LLM Safety in Vehicle Voice Command Authorization
Authors: Diba Afroze, Xingli Zhang, Yazhou Tu, Xiali Hei
First: 2026-09-17T03:20:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-17T03:20:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into vehicle voice assistants. But linking natural-language requests to vehicle functions creates a safety-critical authorization problem. Before executing a command, the system must choose whether to execute, refuse, clarify, require confirmation, defer to manual control, trigger an emergency response, or make no tool call. To our knowledge, prior evaluations do not isolate this pre-action decision across speaker role, authentication status, vehicle state, and tool availability. We introduce a 202-scenario benchmark with Reference Decisions under a seven-class taxonomy. We evaluate two local open-weight models and three API-based LLMs using Decision Alignment and safety-specific error metrics. Alignment ranges from 40.1% for Llama 3.2 3B to 89.1% for Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview. The API-based models score between 83.2% and 89.1%, with no statistically significant differences among them. Even these models produce two to three False Executes among 161 non-execution scenarios, and persistent errors remain in confirmation and manual-control decisions. A controlled Llama 3.2 3B ablation increases alignment to 40.1% under the structured authorization policy, versus 28.2-29.2% under schema-only and generic-safety baselines, but it does not eliminate False Executes. Structured LLM decisions are therefore insufficient as a standalone safety mechanism, and deployment requires an independent enforcement layer that verifies tool permissions and vehicle-state constraints before invoking any vehicle function.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从意图到行动：车辆语音命令授权的LLM安全性基准测试
大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地集成到车辆语音助手中。但将自然语言请求与车辆功能关联起来会产生一个安全关键的授权问题。在执行命令之前，系统必须选择执行、拒绝、澄清、要求确认、推迟手动控制、触发紧急响应或不进行任何工具调用。根据我们的了解，之前的评估并未在发言者角色、身份验证状态、车辆状态和工具可用性之间隔离这一预行动决策。我们引入了一个包含202个场景的基准测试，采用七类分类法下的参考决策。我们使用决策对齐和安全特定错误指标评估了两个本地开放权重模型和三个基于API的LLM。对齐率从Llama 3.2 3B的40.1%到Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview的89.1%不等。基于API的模型得分在83.2%到89.1%之间，且它们之间没有统计学显著差异。即使这些模型在161个非执行场景中也产生了两到三个错误执行，并且在确认和手动控制决策中仍然存在持续错误。受控的Llama 3.2 3B消融在结构化授权政策下将对齐率提高到40.1%，而在仅使用模式和通用安全基线下则为28.2-29.2%，但并未消除错误执行。因此，结构化LLM决策作为独立的安全机制是不够的，部署需要一个独立的执行层，在调用任何车辆功能之前验证工具权限和车辆状态约束。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the safety-critical issue of linking natural-language requests to vehicle functions in voice assistants powered by large language models (LLMs). Previous evaluations have not adequately isolated the pre-action decision-making process, which is essential for ensuring safety in various contexts such as speaker role and vehicle state. This paper proposes a 202-scenario benchmark with Reference Decisions categorized under a seven-class taxonomy to evaluate LLM performance. The methodology involves assessing two local models and three API-based LLMs using Decision Alignment and safety-specific error metrics. The findings reveal that alignment scores vary significantly, with the best-performing model achieving 89.1% alignment, though persistent errors in certain decision-making scenarios indicate that structured LLM decisions alone are insufficient for safety, necessitating an additional enforcement layer for vehicle function authorization.
本研究解决了将自然语言请求与语音助手中大型语言模型（LLMs）驱动的车辆功能链接的安全关键问题。以往的评估未能充分隔离各种因素（如说话者角色和车辆状态）下的预行动决策过程，导致潜在的安全风险。本文提出了一个包含202种场景的基准，采用七类分类法的参考决策，从而允许对LLM性能进行更细致的评估。该方法论涉及使用决策对齐和特定安全错误指标评估两个本地开放权重模型和三个基于API的LLM。研究结果显示，决策对齐得分范围为40.1%至89.1%，在某些决策场景中存在持续错误，表明单靠结构化LLM决策不足以确保安全，因此需要额外的执行层以确保安全操作。
HoloAegis: Frozen Representation, Topological Inference --- Minimally Parametric Safety Manifolds and Their Capability Boundaries for LLM Guardrails
Authors: Tak Ho Alex Li, Kaijie Liu, Lik-Hang Lee, Kin Chung Ho, Ping Shum, Michael K. Ng
First: 2026-08-09T05:05:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-15T12:22:48+00:00
Comments: Preprint v2, September 2026. 4 figures, 12 tables. Corrected and substantially revised from v1 (arXiv:2608.08485v1)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current LLM safety guardrails face a fundamental tension: fine-tuning distorts pre-trained representations while generative judges incur prohibitive inference costs. We ask a complementary question: how far can safety be achieved through pure geometric reasoning over frozen representations, and where does it fail? We present HoloAegis, a minimally parametric topological inference framework that decouples representation from reasoning: an un-fine-tuned encoder maps text to the unit sphere S^{d-1}, and all decisions reduce to Gibbs-Boltzmann free-energy differences over pre-computed anchor centroids. We contribute a boundary-mapping study rather than a leaderboard claim. On a frozen three-benchmark protocol, HoloAegis (3.2 MB) statistically matches WildGuard-7B (14 GB) on toxicity (0.96 vs. 0.96), exceeds it on harmful behaviors (0.99 vs. 0.79), and cedes oversafety detection (0.62 vs. 0.98) -- while ShieldGemma-2B fails on indirect harms (0.34). These failure modes are complementary and mechanistically traceable: potential-difference scoring senses manifold clustering, whereas policy-conditioned LLM judging requires explicit taxonomy matching. We restate our Topological Boundary Stability conjecture in ratio form and validate it via reference-set bootstrap: anchor banks reduce score variance 4-15x and boundary displacement to approximately 0.44 + 0.23 sqrt(k/K) of the full-space estimator. Per-domain analysis further reveals that geometric separability tracks within-domain semantic homogeneity. Our results chart where geometric guardrails substitute for, and where they must defer to, LLM judges.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HoloAegis：冻结表示、拓扑推理---最小参数安全流形及其对LLM保护措施的能力边界
当前LLM安全保护措施面临根本性矛盾：微调扭曲了预训练表示，而生成性判断则产生了高昂的推理成本。我们提出一个互补性问题：通过对冻结表示的纯几何推理，安全性可以达到多远，在哪里会失败？我们提出HoloAegis，一个最小参数的拓扑推理框架，将表示与推理解耦：未微调的编码器将文本映射到单位球面S^{d-1}，所有决策归结为对预计算锚心的Gibbs-Boltzmann自由能差异。我们贡献了一个边界映射研究，而不是排行榜声明。在一个冻结的三基准协议上，HoloAegis（3.2 MB）在毒性上与WildGuard-7B（14 GB）统计匹配（0.96 vs. 0.96），在有害行为上超越它（0.99 vs. 0.79），而在过度安全检测上则让步（0.62 vs. 0.98）——同时ShieldGemma-2B在间接危害上失败（0.34）。这些失败模式是互补的，并且在机制上可追溯：电位差评分感知流形聚类，而政策条件的LLM判断则需要明确的分类匹配。我们以比率形式重述我们的拓扑边界稳定性猜想，并通过参考集自助法进行验证：锚库将得分方差降低4-15倍，边界位移约为全空间估计器的0.44 + 0.23 sqrt(k/K)。每个领域的分析进一步揭示几何可分性与领域内语义同质性之间的关系。我们的结果描绘了几何保护措施替代和必须依赖LLM判断的地方。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the limitations of current large language model (LLM) safety guardrails, which often distort pre-trained representations through fine-tuning or incur high inference costs with generative judges. The proposed method, HoloAegis, introduces a minimally parametric topological inference framework that utilizes frozen representations, allowing for geometric reasoning without fine-tuning. This approach aims to achieve safety through boundary-mapping studies rather than traditional leaderboard comparisons. HoloAegis demonstrates competitive performance on a frozen three-benchmark protocol, matching toxicity detection rates with a larger model while exceeding it in harmful behavior detection, thus validating the effectiveness of geometric guardrails in certain contexts while identifying their limitations in others.
本研究解决了当前LLM安全防护措施的局限性，这些措施通常通过微调扭曲预训练表示，或在生成判断中产生高昂的推理成本。提出的方法HoloAegis引入了一种最小参数的拓扑推理框架，利用冻结表示，允许在不微调的情况下进行几何推理。这种方法有效地将表示与推理分离，将文本映射到单位球体，并基于预计算的锚心做出决策，从而克服了扭曲和成本的问题。本文贡献了一项边界映射研究，展示了HoloAegis在冻结三基准协议上的表现，其毒性检测与WildGuard-7B相当，同时在有害行为方面超越了它，表明几何防护措施在某些情况下可以有效替代LLM判断。该方法包括对拓扑边界稳定性猜想的统计验证，揭示了得分方差和边界位移的显著减少，支持了所提出框架在确保LLM应用安全性方面的有效性。
Useless but Safe? Benchmarking Utility Recovery with User Intent Clarification in Multi-Turn Conversations
Authors: Mingqian Zheng, Malia Morgan, Liwei Jiang, Carolyn Rose, Maarten Sap
First: 2026-04-29T18:37:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-12T19:48:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current LLM safety alignment techniques improve model robustness against adversarial attacks, but overlook whether and how LLMs can recover helpfulness when benign users clarify their intent. We introduce CarryOnBench, the first interactive benchmark that measures whether LLMs can revise their interpretation of user intent and recover utility, while remaining safe through multi-turn conversations. Starting from 398 seemingly harmful queries with benign underlying intents, we simulate 5,970 conversations by varying user follow-up sequences, evaluating 14 models on both intent-aligned utility and safety. CarryOnBench yields 1,866 different conversation flows of 4--12 turns, totaling 23,880 model responses. We design Ben-Util, a checklist-based metric that evaluates how well each model response fulfills the user's benign information need using atomic items. At turn one, models fulfill only 10.5--37.6% of the user's benign information need. When the same query includes the benign intent upfront, models fulfill 25.1--72.1%, confirming that models withhold information due to intent misinterpretation, not limited knowledge. With benign clarifications in multi-turn conversations, 13 of 14 models approach or exceed this single-turn baseline, yet recovery cost varies across models. We identify two failure modes invisible to single-turn evaluations---unsafe recovery, where a model updates at disproportionate safety cost and redundant recovery, where a model recycles prior responses rather than providing new information. Moreover, conversations converge to similar harmfulness levels regardless of how conservative the model starts. These findings expose a gap that single-turn evaluations miss---whether a model is appropriately cautious or simply unresponsive to clarified user intent.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无用但安全？在多轮对话中通过用户意图澄清基准测试实用性恢复
当前的LLM安全对齐技术提高了模型对抗攻击的鲁棒性，但忽视了LLM在良性用户澄清意图时是否以及如何恢复有用性。我们引入CarryOnBench，这是第一个交互式基准，测量LLM是否能够修正对用户意图的解释并恢复实用性，同时在多轮对话中保持安全。从398个看似有害的查询开始，这些查询具有良性的潜在意图，我们通过变化用户后续序列模拟了5,970个对话，评估了14个模型在意图对齐的实用性和安全性方面的表现。CarryOnBench产生了1,866种不同的对话流程，轮次为4到12，总计23,880个模型响应。我们设计了Ben-Util，这是一个基于清单的指标，评估每个模型响应在多大程度上满足用户的良性信息需求。在第一轮中，模型仅满足用户良性信息需求的10.5%到37.6%。当同一查询在前面包含良性意图时，模型满足25.1%到72.1%，确认模型因意图误解而 withholding 信息，而不是知识有限。在多轮对话中，13个模型接近或超过这一单轮基线，但恢复成本在模型之间有所不同。我们识别出两种在单轮评估中不可见的失败模式——不安全恢复，其中模型以不成比例的安全成本进行更新，以及冗余恢复，其中模型重复先前的响应而不是提供新信息。此外，无论模型起始多么保守，对话都趋向于相似的有害性水平。这些发现揭示了单轮评估所忽视的一个差距——模型是否适当地谨慎，或仅仅对澄清的用户意图无反应。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the limitations of current large language model (LLM) safety alignment techniques, which enhance robustness against adversarial attacks but fail to assess the models' ability to recover helpfulness when users clarify their intent in multi-turn conversations. Previous methods primarily focused on single-turn evaluations, neglecting the dynamics of user intent clarification, leading to issues such as misinterpretation and unresponsiveness. The proposed approach, CarryOnBench, introduces an interactive benchmark that evaluates LLMs on their ability to revise interpretations and recover utility while maintaining safety across multi-turn dialogues. This methodology involves simulating 5,970 conversations based on 398 harmful queries with benign intents and assessing 14 models using a new checklist-based metric, Ben-Util. The results reveal that models initially fulfill only a fraction of user needs, but with clarifications, many models improve their performance, although recovery costs vary, highlighting two failure modes that single-turn evaluations overlook. Overall, the study contributes to understanding LLM behavior in multi-turn interactions and emphasizes the importance of evaluating models' responsiveness to clarified user intent.
本研究解决了当前大型语言模型（LLM）安全对齐技术的局限性，这些技术专注于抵御对抗性攻击，但未能评估LLM在用户澄清意图时如何在多轮对话中恢复有用性。以往的方法主要评估单轮交互，忽视了持续对话的动态，这可能导致用户意图的误解和不充分的响应。所提出的方法CarryOnBench引入了一个交互式基准，模拟了5970个对话，以评估14个模型在多轮中将效用与安全性对齐的能力。研究表明，模型最初仅满足一小部分良性用户需求，但通过澄清，许多模型显著提高了性能，尽管恢复成本各异。研究结果揭示了单轮评估所忽视的两种失败模式：不安全的恢复和冗余的恢复，强调了在多轮上下文中评估LLM的重要性，以更好地理解它们对用户意图的响应能力。
Adaptive Adversaries: A Multi-Turn, Multi-LLM Benchmark for LLM Agent Security
Authors: Devina Jain, David Hartmann, Chuan Li
First: 2026-07-20T15:30:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-11T18:06:53+00:00
Comments: Second Workshop on Agents in the Wild: Safety, Security, and Beyond
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-based agents process external content, exposing them to prompt injection and multi-turn manipulation. We present a 21-scenario benchmark for adaptive cross-session attacks against fresh-session LLM defenders: an autonomous LLM attacker observes prior defender responses and pivots across rounds, while each defender response is evaluated as a fresh interaction. A controlled 3 x 3 attacker-defender matrix contains 945 battles. Restricting scoring to the first round yields 0-1% attack success rate (ASR); allowing 15 rounds yields 7.9-16.8%. Pooling three attacker LLMs uncovers 1.7-2.2 times as many unique successful inputs as the best single attacker, at three times the battle budget. Aggregate rates conceal opposing scenario-specific weaknesses in session-secret protection and authority handling, preserved in two higher-sample evaluations. On six scenarios, adding one provenance paragraph reduces ASR from 110/270 to 70/270, with selective effects across tasks. History and defender-state controls, together with frozen replay, characterize how the interaction protocol changes the result. A competition adds 18,422 held-out battles on a fixed gpt-oss-20b backbone and complementary benign-task evaluations. The benchmark exposes attacker and defender models, harnesses, scenarios, session state, and interaction budgets as configurable choices for systematic security evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自适应对手：多回合、多LLM基准测试用于LLM代理安全性
基于LLM的代理处理外部内容，使其暴露于提示注入和多回合操控中。我们提出了一个21场景基准测试，用于针对新会话LLM防御者的自适应跨会话攻击：自主LLM攻击者观察先前防御者的响应并在回合间进行调整，而每个防御者的响应被评估为一次新的交互。一个受控的3 x 3攻击者-防御者矩阵包含945场战斗。将评分限制在第一回合的攻击成功率（ASR）为0-1%；允许15回合则为7.9-16.8%。汇聚三种攻击者LLM发现的独特成功输入数量是最佳单一攻击者的1.7-2.2倍，战斗预算是三倍。汇总率掩盖了会话秘密保护和权限处理中的相对场景特定弱点，这在两个更高样本评估中得以保留。在六个场景中，添加一个来源段落将ASR从110/270降低到70/270，任务间效果选择性。历史和防御者状态控制，以及冻结重放，表征了交互协议如何改变结果。一个竞赛在固定的gpt-oss-20b基础上增加了18,422场保留战斗和补充的良性任务评估。该基准测试暴露了攻击者和防御者模型、工具、场景、会话状态和交互预算作为系统安全评估的可配置选择。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the vulnerabilities of LLM-based agents to prompt injection and multi-turn manipulation, which have been inadequately assessed by previous methods that typically focus on single-turn interactions. The proposed approach introduces a 21-scenario benchmark for adaptive cross-session attacks, where an autonomous LLM attacker learns from prior defender responses and adapts its strategy across multiple rounds. This method effectively highlights the weaknesses of defender models in session-secret protection and authority handling, demonstrating a significant improvement in attack success rates when allowing for multiple interactions. The methodology involves a controlled attacker-defender matrix with 945 battles, revealing that pooling multiple attackers can yield a greater variety of successful inputs. The findings indicate that the proposed benchmark can systematically evaluate LLM agent security, achieving an attack success rate of 7.9-16.8% over 15 rounds, thus supporting the goal of enhancing security assessments in LLM applications.

'Ghaib in Translation' aka Unseen Harm: Measuring Cross-Script Safety Inconsistency with 'Missed-in-Urdu' Scores in LLM Hate Speech Detection
Authors: Fawzia Zehra, Kara-Isitt, Sonal Khosla, Stephen Swift
First: 2026-08-25T07:58:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-09T16:54:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Urdu, the world's tenth most spoken language with 246 million speakers, remains almost entirely absent from mainstream LLM safety evaluation and nine years of WOAH proceedings. To investigate whether this absence has measurable consequences for content moderation reliability, five large language models, GPT-4o, Claude Sonnet 4.5, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Qwen-2.5, and Llama-3.1, were tested across six datasets spanning Nastaliq Urdu, Roman Urdu, English, and code-switched Urdu-English. Across the five Urdu-script datasets, label instability between original-script and English-translation classification ranged from 15.9% (Gemini 2.5 Flash) to 31.6% (Qwen-2.5), with a 'Missed-in-Urdu' rate, content flagged as harmful in English translation but passed as normal in the original script, ranging from 2.4% to 9.9% (median 4.3%). A complete enumeration of all 205 papers across nine ALW/WOAH editions via the ACL Anthology API confirms zero dedicated Urdu papers across the entire period. Results indicate that current LLMs provide uneven safety assurance across Urdu's script varieties, with smaller open-weight models showing substantially higher instability and missed-harm rates than frontier closed models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：'翻译中的隐秘'即未见的危害：使用'乌尔都语遗漏'分数测量跨脚本安全不一致性在LLM仇恨言论检测中的应用
乌尔都语是全球第十大语言，拥有2.46亿说话者，但在主流LLM安全评估和九年的WOAH会议中几乎完全缺席。为了调查这种缺席是否对内容审核的可靠性产生可测量的影响，测试了五个大型语言模型：GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.5、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Qwen-2.5和Llama-3.1，涵盖了六个数据集，包括Nastaliq乌尔都语、罗马乌尔都语、英语和代码切换的乌尔都英语。在五个乌尔都脚本数据集中，原始脚本与英语翻译分类之间的标签不稳定性范围为15.9%（Gemini 2.5 Flash）到31.6%（Qwen-2.5），'乌尔都语遗漏'率，即在英语翻译中标记为有害但在原始脚本中通过的内容，范围为2.4%到9.9%（中位数4.3%）。通过ACL Anthology API对九个ALW/WOAH版本中205篇论文的完整枚举确认，在整个期间内没有专门的乌尔都语论文。结果表明，当前的LLM在乌尔都语的脚本变体之间提供的不均匀安全保障，较小的开放权重模型显示出明显更高的不稳定性和遗漏危害率，超过了前沿的封闭模型。

Previous methods lacked comprehensive analysis of Urdu's script variations, leading to unreliable content moderation. The proposed approach systematically evaluates five LLMs across multiple datasets, revealing substantial inconsistencies in safety performance between original Urdu scripts and their English translations. Style Over Substance: Content-Invariant Wrappers Flip LLM Safety-Judge Verdicts
Authors: Yongxi Zhou, Wenbo Ye, Yuanzhe Liu, Zihan Dong, Junwei Yao
First: 2026-09-08T04:27:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-08T04:27:22+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 1 table. Code, wrappers, and per-verdict labels: https://github.com/Yongxi-Zhou/safety-judge-robustness
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Automatic safety judges -- systems such as Llama Guard or a GPT-4o grading prompt that decide whether a model's reply is harmful -- produce the numbers behind almost every reported jailbreak success rate, defense evaluation, and safety leaderboard. We ask whether these judges grade what a reply contains or how it sounds. We keep a reply's content fixed and add content-invariant style wrappers: fixed strings placed before or after the reply that change only its tone (an educational disclaimer, a fake safety "reasoning" block, a token refusal followed by the unchanged harmful body), or, on harmless refusals, framing that merely sounds dangerous. The body is preserved byte-for-byte, so a faithful judge must return the same verdict, and any flip is an error of the judge, not a change in safety. Over 600 JailbreakBench replies x up to 7 forms x 8 judges, we measure flip rates with paired significance tests and measured noise floors. Findings are precise rather than universal: most judges barely move, but specific judges harbor cheaply exploitable blind spots. A token-refusal wrapper flips 19.9% of GPT-4o-mini's correct "unsafe" verdicts (noise floor 0.5%; 18.2% under majority-of-three re-scoring) yet moves Claude only 0.4%. The deployed Llama Guard 4 is deterministically gamed: an "educational course" framing flips 12.3% of its harmful verdicts to safe. A second deployed guard (gpt-oss-safeguard-20b) is immune, and rewriting only the grading prompt (StrongREJECT-style) cuts the attack tenfold on the identical model -- the vulnerability lives in the judge, not the content. A two-annotator human validation confirms 100% content invariance and 90% of flips as judge errors (kappa 0.95-1.0), and a bootstrap shows the underlying model ranking is already unstable to sampling alone. We release the dataset, wrappers, code, and per-verdict labels.
中文标题/摘要
标题：形式胜于内容：内容不变的包装翻转LLM安全评判结果
自动安全评判系统——如Llama Guard或GPT-4o评分提示，决定模型回复是否有害——生成几乎所有报告的越狱成功率、防御评估和安全排行榜背后的数据。我们探讨这些评判是评估回复的内容还是其语气。我们保持回复内容不变，添加内容不变的风格包装：在回复前后放置的固定字符串，仅改变其语气（教育免责声明、虚假的安全“推理”块、后接不变的有害内容的拒绝令），或者在无害拒绝的情况下，仅仅听起来危险的框架。主体逐字保留，因此一个忠实的评判必须返回相同的判决，任何翻转都是评判的错误，而不是安全的变化。在超过600个JailbreakBench回复x最多7种形式x 8个评判中，我们通过配对显著性测试和测量噪声底线来测量翻转率。发现是精确的而非普遍的：大多数评判几乎没有变化，但特定评判存在廉价可利用的盲点。一个令牌拒绝包装翻转了19.9%的GPT-4o-mini的正确“unsafe”判决（噪声底线0.5%；在三人多数重新评分下为18.2%），但对Claude的影响仅为0.4%。已部署的Llama Guard 4被确定性地操控：“教育课程”框架将12.3%的有害判决翻转为安全。第二个已部署的防护（gpt-oss-safeguard-20b）是免疫的，仅重写评分提示（StrongREJECT风格）就将攻击减少十倍，脆弱性存在于评判中，而非内容。两位注释者的人类验证确认100%的内容不变性和90%的翻转为评判错误（kappa 0.95-1.0），而自助法显示基础模型排名已经对抽样不稳定。我们发布数据集、包装、代码和每个判决标签。
Summary / 总结
This research investigates the reliability of automatic safety judges, which assess whether responses from language models are harmful, by questioning whether these judges evaluate content or merely the presentation style. Previous methods have focused on content evaluation but often overlook how stylistic changes can influence judgments, leading to exploitable vulnerabilities. The proposed approach introduces content-invariant style wrappers that alter the tone of responses without changing their content, effectively revealing biases in the judges' assessments. The study employs a robust methodology involving over 600 responses tested against multiple judges to measure the rate of judgment flips caused by these wrappers. The findings indicate that while most judges remain stable, certain judges exhibit significant vulnerabilities, with one wrapper causing a 19.9% flip rate in harmful verdicts, demonstrating that the flaws lie within the judges rather than the content itself, thereby contributing valuable insights into the robustness of safety evaluation systems.
本研究探讨了自动安全评估系统的可靠性，这些系统评估模型响应的有害性，并质疑其评估是基于内容还是风格。以往的方法主要关注内容分析，但对风格变化表现出脆弱性，导致判决不一致。提出的方法引入了内容不变的风格包装，这些包装在不改变内容的情况下改变响应的语气，从而评估评估者的可靠性。该研究采用了全面的方法，涉及600多个响应，由多个评估者进行评估，结果显示大多数评估者保持一致的判决，但某些评估者存在可利用的弱点。值得注意的是，令牌拒绝包装改变了19.9%的GPT-4o-mini的不安全判决，显示出特定评估者的显著脆弱性，而其他评估者则未受影响。这些发现突显了安全评估系统需要改进的鲁棒性，并提供了数据集和工具以供进一步研究。
The Oversight Gap: What LLM Safety Monitors Miss, and Why It Is Not Capability
Authors: Xin Xu
First: 2026-09-07T07:57:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-07T07:57:28+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 3 figures. Under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Several properties safety monitors are asked to certify, among them cross-tenant noninterference, sandbagging and evaluation awareness, are 2-safety hyperproperties, witnessed only by two executions. The standard consequence is a binary impossibility: one trace cannot decide them. We replace the binary with a measurement. A tight bound puts the balanced accuracy of any single-trace monitor at $\tfrac12+\tfrac12\,TV(P_0,P_1)$, turning undecidability into a graded detectability frontier and defining an oversight gap: a monitor's shortfall below it. On a leak family with closed-form $TV$, nine LLM monitors are optimal at $TV=0$ but capture little signal as $TV$ grows; at $TV=1$, where a 20-line membership check scores $100\%$, they average $60.9\%$. That shortfall is mostly not capability: naming what to check closes $61\%$ of it while leaving the $TV=0$ control at chance. The same split runs through a $2{\times}2$ factorial: an imagined second run leaves monitors at chance ($50.4\%$) while the same rule on an executed second run reaches $90.0\%$, and a stored oracle without a comparison procedure yields only $68.2\%$. Information and procedure are each necessary and neither is capability. Under nondeterminism, replay tracks a closed-form $k$-replay curve only under the right projection, and a projection frontier shows the resulting dilemma is forced: narrow misses $98.6\%$ of off-channel leaks, broad flags $75.7\%$ of clean traffic, and attainable accuracy decays like $1/(qm)$ in the benign-variation rate and the channel count. Finally, two frontier LLM judges certified an earlier version of our own benchmark as sound while a sign test found a directional bias ($p=2.7\times10^{-5}$) that invalidated three of our findings. Construction validity for hyperproperty benchmarks should be proved mechanically, not audited by models.
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the limitations of existing safety monitors for large language models (LLMs), which struggle to certify complex properties like cross-tenant noninterference and evaluation awareness due to their binary impossibility in decision-making. Previous methods have relied on single-trace monitors that fail to capture the nuanced performance needed for these hyperproperties, leading to significant oversight gaps. The proposed approach introduces a graded detectability framework that quantifies monitor performance through a tight bound on balanced accuracy, revealing that many monitors perform poorly as the total variation (TV) increases. The paper contributes by demonstrating that the shortfall in monitor performance is not solely due to capability but also to the lack of information and procedural context. The methodology involves analyzing the performance of nine LLM monitors across varying TV levels, showing that while some monitors achieve high accuracy under specific conditions, they generally fall short, with average scores of 60.9% at TV=1. This work highlights the necessity of mechanical proof for hyperproperty benchmarks and suggests that the interplay of information and procedure is critical for effective monitoring.
本文探讨了现有大型语言模型（LLM）安全监控器的局限性，这些监控器在认证复杂属性（如跨租户非干扰和评估意识）时，由于其二元决策特性而面临困难。传统方法未能提供足够的检测能力，导致了监控器性能不足的监督差距。作者提出了一种新方法，用分级测量系统替代二元结果，从而更细致地理解监控器性能。他们的方法包括评估九个LLM监控器在一个以总变差距离（TV）为特征的泄漏家族中的表现，结果显示，尽管监控器在TV=0时表现最佳，但随着TV的增加，其有效性下降，在TV=1时平均仅为60.9%的准确率。这项研究通过强调需要改进基准，以考虑安全监控中的信息和程序元素，表明传统能力评估不足以确保LLM安全，做出了重要贡献。
SAEs Can Improve Unlearning: Dynamic Sparse Autoencoder Guardrails for Precision Unlearning in LLMs
Authors: Aashiq Muhamed, Jacopo Bonato, Mona Diab, Virginia Smith
First: 2025-04-11T01:24:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-07T03:32:59+00:00
Comments: COLM 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Machine unlearning is a promising approach to improve LLM safety by removing unwanted knowledge from the model. However, prevailing gradient-based unlearning methods suffer from issues such as high computational costs, hyperparameter instability, poor sequential unlearning capability, vulnerability to relearning attacks, low data efficiency, and lack of interpretability. While Sparse Autoencoders are well-suited to improve these aspects by enabling targeted activation-based unlearning, prior approaches underperform gradient-based methods. This work demonstrates that, contrary to these earlier findings, SAEs can significantly improve unlearning when employed dynamically. We introduce $\textbf{Dynamic DAE Guardrails}$ (DSG), a novel method for precision unlearning that leverages principled feature selection and a dynamic classifier. Our experiments show DSG substantially outperforms leading unlearning methods, achieving superior forget-utility trade-offs. DSG addresses key drawbacks of gradient-based approaches for unlearning -- offering enhanced computational efficiency and stability, robust performance in sequential unlearning, stronger resistance to relearning attacks, better data efficiency including zero-shot settings, and more interpretable unlearning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SAEs可以改善遗忘：用于LLMs精确遗忘的动态稀疏自编码器护栏
机器遗忘是一种有前景的方法，通过从模型中移除不需要的知识来提高LLM的安全性。然而，现有的基于梯度的遗忘方法存在高计算成本、超参数不稳定、顺序遗忘能力差、易受重学习攻击、数据效率低和缺乏可解释性等问题。稀疏自编码器非常适合通过启用针对性激活的遗忘来改善这些方面，但先前的方法在性能上不如基于梯度的方法。本研究表明，与早期发现相反，SAEs在动态使用时可以显著改善遗忘。我们引入了$\textbf{动态DAE护栏}$（DSG），这是一种利用原则性特征选择和动态分类器的精确遗忘新方法。我们的实验表明，DSG在遗忘方法中显著优于领先的遗忘方法，实现了更好的遗忘-效用权衡。DSG解决了基于梯度的遗忘方法的关键缺陷——提供了更高的计算效率和稳定性、在顺序遗忘中的强大性能、对重学习攻击的更强抵抗力、更好的数据效率（包括零样本设置）以及更可解释的遗忘。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenges of machine unlearning in large language models (LLMs), which is essential for enhancing model safety by eliminating unwanted knowledge. Previous gradient-based unlearning methods have been hindered by high computational costs, hyperparameter instability, and other limitations, prompting the need for more effective solutions. This paper proposes Dynamic Sparse Autoencoder Guardrails (DSG), a novel approach that utilizes dynamic feature selection and a classifier to improve unlearning precision. The methodology demonstrates that DSG significantly outperforms existing unlearning techniques, achieving better forget-utility trade-offs and addressing the shortcomings of gradient-based methods, including improved efficiency, stability, and interpretability. The experimental results indicate that DSG provides robust performance in sequential unlearning and is resilient against relearning attacks, supporting its effectiveness in practical applications.

Behind Harmful Compliance: Behavioral and Mechanistic Divergence Across LLM Jailbreaks
Authors: Md Rysul Kabir, Zoran Tiganj
First: 2026-04-20T17:01:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-05T18:10:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Open-weight language models can be rendered unsafe through several parameter-level interventions, yet models with matched harmful compliance can exhibit fundamentally different failure modes. We compare harmful supervised fine-tuning (SFT), harmful reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), and refusal-feature abliteration in Qwen2.5-7B and Llama-3.1-8B using harmfulness, capability, self-audit, safety reflection, representation similarity, and refusal-direction repair. All three routes reach near-ceiling harmfulness, but SFT causes the broadest capability loss and representational drift; abliteration yields localized, family-dependent refusal-feature suppression; and RLVR largely preserves base-model capability, explicit safety judgments, and representation geometry while retargeting behavior toward compliance. RLVR models consequently remain unusually responsive to safety-reflection prompts despite complying under direct prompting. We further find that harmful RLVR induces capability-blind compliance: models claim to complete unavailable actions and fabricate information about nonexistent entities. Targeted RLVR calibration substantially reduces this false acceptance without restoring safety or degrading general capability. These results show that harmful compliance, harm recognition, and capability awareness are separable behavioral axes, and that self-audit and hallucination patterns are not necessarily robust safety signals under adaptive post-training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：有害合规背后的行为和机制差异：LLM越狱案例分析
开放权重语言模型可以通过多种参数级干预变得不安全，但具有匹配有害合规的模型可能表现出根本不同的失败模式。我们比较了Qwen2.5-7B和Llama-3.1-8B中的有害监督微调（SFT）、有害可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）和拒绝特征消融，使用有害性、能力、自我审计、安全反思、表示相似性和拒绝方向修复等指标。所有三种途径都达到了接近上限的有害性，但SFT导致了最广泛的能力损失和表示漂移；消融产生了局部的、依赖于家族的拒绝特征抑制；而RLVR在重新定向合规行为的同时，基本上保留了基础模型的能力、明确的安全判断和表示几何。尽管在直接提示下合规，RLVR模型仍然对安全反思提示表现出异常的响应性。我们进一步发现，有害的RLVR会导致能力盲目的合规：模型声称完成不可用的动作，并虚构关于不存在实体的信息。针对性的RLVR校准显著减少了这种虚假接受，而不恢复安全或降低一般能力。这些结果表明，有害合规、伤害识别和能力意识是可分离的行为轴，自我审计和幻觉模式在适应性后训练下不一定是稳健的安全信号。

The methodology involves comparing the performance of Qwen2.5-7B and Llama-3.1-8B models across various metrics, demonstrating that RLVR can significantly reduce false acceptance without compromising overall capability, thus supporting the goals of improved safety and compliance.

TIER: Threat Implicitness Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Safety Behaviors
Authors: Thu-Hien Trinh-Thi, Hai-Yen Vong, Thanh-Ha Ung-Dung, Tram Ho
First: 2026-09-04T13:13:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-04T13:13:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current LLM safety benchmarks largely rely on binary metrics, overlooking how models respond to harmful prompts with varying threat implicitness. We introduce TIER, a Threat Implicitness Benchmark for behavioral safety evaluation of LLMs. TIER covers four risk domains and four threat levels, from explicit harmful requests to sophisticated jailbreaks. Responses are assessed using a six-label behavior scale and two independent LLM judges. Experiments on six open-weight LLMs show that safety behaviors evolve gradually across threat levels rather than shifting directly from refusal to compliance. Contextual prompts yield the most diverse behaviors, while jailbreaks reveal the largest robustness gaps. Furthermore, models with similar Attack Success Rates can exhibit distinct response distributions, highlighting the need for behavior-aware LLM safety evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TIER：评估LLM安全行为的威胁隐含性基准
当前LLM安全基准主要依赖于二元指标，忽视了模型对不同威胁隐含性的有害提示的响应。我们引入了TIER，一个用于LLM行为安全评估的威胁隐含性基准。TIER涵盖四个风险领域和四个威胁级别，从明确的有害请求到复杂的越狱。使用六级行为量表和两个独立的LLM评审对响应进行评估。在六个开放权重的LLM上进行的实验表明，安全行为在威胁级别之间逐渐演变，而不是直接从拒绝转变为遵从。上下文提示产生了最丰富的行为，而越狱则揭示了最大的鲁棒性差距。此外，具有相似攻击成功率的模型可能表现出不同的响应分布，突显了对行为意识的LLM安全评估的需求。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the limitations of current LLM safety benchmarks, which primarily utilize binary metrics and fail to account for the varying implicitness of threats in harmful prompts. Previous methods have not effectively captured the nuances of model responses, leading to an incomplete understanding of LLM safety behaviors. The proposed TIER benchmark introduces a more comprehensive evaluation framework that categorizes threats into four risk domains and levels, allowing for a nuanced assessment of model responses using a six-label behavior scale and independent judges. This approach is well-motivated as it reveals that safety behaviors evolve gradually across threat levels, with contextual prompts producing diverse behaviors and jailbreaks exposing significant robustness gaps. Experiments conducted on six open-weight LLMs demonstrate that the proposed methodology provides a clearer picture of safety behaviors, indicating that models with similar attack success rates can still show distinct response patterns, thereby supporting the need for behavior-aware safety evaluations.

IndicSafeEval: Safety Robustness of Large Language Models under Multilingual Persuasive Jailbreak Attacks
Authors: Saikat Mondal, Mamta, Deeksha Varshney, Oana Cocarascu, Asif Ekbal
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2026-09-03T12:54:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-04T11:46:25+00:00
Comments: 38 pages, 7 figures, 33 tables. Accepted to Findings of EMNLP 2026. Contains examples of harmful model outputs
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in multilingual settings, yet their safety is still evaluated primarily in English. This limits our understanding of how alignment failures manifest in low-resource and culturally diverse languages. We introduce IndicSafeEval, a persuasion-based jailbreak evaluation framework for Indian languages. Our benchmark combines ten safety critical content categories with six human-like persuasive strategies across four different Indian languages, such as Hindi, Bengali, Marathi and Punjabi, resulting in 7,200 adversarial prompts. We conduct a systematic black-box evaluation of several open-source LLMs to examine how their safety behaviour varies across languages, persuasion strategies, and risk categories. Our analysis shows that the model does not behave equally safely across all languages and prompt styles. Instead, safety performance depends strongly on both the languages used and the way a request is phrased using persuasive cues. We further observe that different risk categories exhibit different levels of vulnerability, with some types of harmful content being significantly more susceptible to persuasion-based jailbreaks than others. These findings reveal important limitations of current safety evaluations, which are largely English-centric, and underscore the need for multilingual and persuasion-aware benchmarking frameworks to more accurately assess real-world LLM safety. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/MonSaikat/IndicSafeEval. Warning: this paper contains example data that may be offensive or harmful.
中文标题/摘要

大型语言模型（LLMs）在多语言环境中的使用日益增加，但其安全性仍主要在英语中进行评估。这限制了我们对低资源和文化多样语言中对齐失败表现的理解。我们引入了IndicSafeEval，这是一个针对印度语言的基于说服的越狱评估框架。我们的基准结合了十个安全关键内容类别和六种类人类的说服策略，涵盖四种不同的印度语言，如印地语、孟加拉语、马拉地语和旁遮普语，共生成7200个对抗性提示。我们对多个开源LLM进行了系统的黑箱评估，以检查它们的安全行为在不同语言、说服策略和风险类别中的变化。我们的分析表明，模型在所有语言和提示风格中并不表现出相同的安全性。相反，安全性能在很大程度上依赖于所使用的语言和请求的措辞方式。我们进一步观察到，不同的风险类别表现出不同的脆弱性，一些有害内容类型对基于说服的越狱攻击显著更易受影响。这些发现揭示了当前安全评估的重要局限性，主要集中在英语，并强调了需要多语言和说服意识的基准框架，以更准确地评估现实世界中的LLM安全性。我们的实现可在https://github.com/MonSaikat/IndicSafeEval获取。警告：本文包含可能冒犯或有害的示例数据。

The proposed IndicSafeEval framework introduces a novel evaluation approach that combines ten safety-critical content categories with six persuasive strategies across four Indian languages, generating 7,200 adversarial prompts for systematic black-box testing of various open-source LLMs. The findings indicate that safety performance is inconsistent across languages and prompt styles, with certain risk categories being more vulnerable to persuasion-based attacks. This research contributes to the field by highlighting the necessity for multilingual and persuasion-aware safety evaluations, thereby advancing the understanding of LLM robustness in diverse linguistic contexts.

FLY-EVAL++: An Evidence-Driven Evaluation Protocol for Safety-Constrained Flight Prediction with Large Language Models
Authors: Yalun Wu, Junfeng Fang, Jiawei Wang, Haotian Liu, Qijun Yang, Minghan Yang, Hongcheng Guo, Zhoujun Li, Boyang Wang
Venue: Proceedings of the Conference on Language Modeling (COLM), 2026
First: 2026-09-03T16:00:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-03T16:00:02+00:00
Comments: Published as a conference paper at COLM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Evaluating large language models (LLMs) in safety-critical, physics-governed environments requires more than accuracy-based metrics, because predictions that are numerically close to the ground truth can still violate operational constraints, combine fields in physically inconsistent ways, or fail to produce usable structured outputs. Existing evaluation protocols do not measure these failure modes reliably. We propose FLY-EVAL++, an evidence-driven evaluation protocol that combines deterministic verification of protocol compliance, physical feasibility, and safety constraints with fixed rubric-guided aggregation into interpretable multi-dimensional scores. We instantiate FLY-EVAL++ for Flight Trajectory and Attitude Prediction (FTAP) by extending the PilotBench setting with history-conditioned and multi-step prediction tasks. Across 66 LLMs, safety compliance is the most discriminative dimension of model behavior: models with comparable predictive performance differ by more than 28 points in safety score, and we observe recurrent failures including safety violations under physically plausible predictions and instability in multi-step rollouts. These results show that evaluation in safety-critical domains should measure constraint satisfaction and structured validity explicitly rather than rely on accuracy-centric reporting alone.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FLY-EVAL++：一种基于证据的安全约束飞行预测评估协议
在安全关键、物理约束的环境中评估大型语言模型（LLMs）需要的不仅仅是基于准确性的指标，因为数值上接近真实值的预测仍可能违反操作约束、以物理不一致的方式组合领域，或未能产生可用的结构化输出。现有的评估协议无法可靠地测量这些失败模式。我们提出了FLY-EVAL++，一种基于证据的评估协议，结合了对协议合规性、物理可行性和安全约束的确定性验证，以及通过固定评分标准引导的聚合，形成可解释的多维评分。我们通过扩展PilotBench设置，结合历史条件和多步预测任务，为飞行轨迹和姿态预测（FTAP）实例化FLY-EVAL++。在66个LLM中，安全合规性是模型行为中最具区分性的维度：具有可比预测性能的模型在安全评分上相差超过28分，我们观察到反复出现的失败，包括在物理合理预测下的安全违规和多步展开中的不稳定性。这些结果表明，在安全关键领域的评估应明确测量约束满足和结构有效性，而不是仅依赖于以准确性为中心的报告。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the need for robust evaluation protocols for large language models (LLMs) in safety-critical environments, where traditional accuracy metrics are insufficient due to potential violations of operational constraints and physical inconsistencies. Previous methods have failed to reliably assess these critical failure modes, prompting the development of FLY-EVAL++, an evidence-driven evaluation protocol that integrates deterministic verification of compliance with safety constraints and physical feasibility, alongside a structured scoring system. This approach is well-motivated as it emphasizes the importance of constraint satisfaction and structured validity in evaluations. The methodology is applied to Flight Trajectory and Attitude Prediction (FTAP) tasks, revealing that safety compliance is a significant differentiator among models, with notable performance variations observed across 66 LLMs. The findings indicate that traditional accuracy-focused evaluations are inadequate, highlighting the necessity for more comprehensive assessment frameworks in safety-critical applications.
本文探讨了在安全关键环境中对大型语言模型（LLMs）进行改进评估方法的必要性，传统的基于准确性的指标无法捕捉操作约束和物理一致性。现有的评估协议无法可靠地评估这些关键的失败模式，这促使作者提出了FLY-EVAL++，一种证据驱动的评估协议，结合了对安全约束和物理可行性的合规性进行确定性验证，以及结构化评分系统。本文的贡献在于其对飞行轨迹和姿态预测（FTAP）任务的评估方法的创新，揭示了在具有相似预测性能的模型之间安全合规性存在显著差异。该方法论通过扩展PilotBench设置，纳入历史条件和多步预测任务，表明安全合规性是模型行为的一个关键维度，研究结果表明，尽管准确性相似，模型的安全评分可以相差超过28分，从而强调了在安全关键领域评估约束满足的重要性。
TACS: Trajectory-Aware Candidate Selection for LLM Jailbreak Suffix Optimization
Authors: Shiliang Xiao
First: 2026-08-30T05:18:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-01T03:40:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Gradient-based jailbreak suffix optimization methods typically update the suffix by retaining the candidate with the lowest current loss. We show that this seemingly natural design is fundamentally myopic: candidates that look better under the current-step proxy often fail to produce better jailbreak outcomes later in the search, revealing a form of selection-stage reward hacking. This suggests that candidate selection, rather than candidate generation alone, is a hidden bottleneck in suffix optimization. To address this issue, we propose TACS, a trajectory-aware candidate selection framework for jailbreak suffix optimization. Instead of selecting candidates solely by their immediate loss, TACS augments per-step evaluation with a trajectory-aware proxy and stabilizes selection with reference-policy regularization and a discriminator-estimated chi-squared correction, encouraging choices that remain effective beyond the current step. Experiments on HarmBench show that TACS consistently outperforms strong baselines under the same search budget, substantially improving attack success rates while exhibiting more stable optimization behavior throughout the search. Our findings highlight that mitigating selection-stage reward hacking caused by myopic candidate selection is critical for improving jailbreak suffix optimization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TACS：用于LLM越狱后缀优化的轨迹感知候选选择
基于梯度的越狱后缀优化方法通常通过保留当前损失最低的候选项来更新后缀。我们表明，这种看似自然的设计在根本上是短视的：在当前步骤代理下看起来更好的候选项往往在搜索的后期未能产生更好的越狱结果，揭示了一种选择阶段奖励黑客行为。这表明候选选择，而不仅仅是候选生成，是后缀优化中的一个隐藏瓶颈。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了TACS，一个用于越狱后缀优化的轨迹感知候选选择框架。TACS不仅仅通过候选项的即时损失来选择候选项，而是通过轨迹感知代理增强每一步的评估，并通过参考策略正则化和判别器估计的卡方修正来稳定选择，鼓励在当前步骤之外仍然有效的选择。在HarmBench上的实验表明，TACS在相同搜索预算下始终优于强基线，显著提高了攻击成功率，同时在整个搜索过程中表现出更稳定的优化行为。我们的研究结果强调，减轻由短视候选选择引起的选择阶段奖励黑客行为对于改善越狱后缀优化至关重要。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the limitations of existing gradient-based jailbreak suffix optimization methods, which typically select candidates based on the lowest current loss, leading to suboptimal long-term outcomes due to myopic decision-making. The proposed TACS framework differs by incorporating a trajectory-aware candidate selection process that evaluates candidates not only on immediate loss but also on their potential effectiveness in future steps, thereby mitigating the issue of selection-stage reward hacking. This approach is well-motivated as it identifies candidate selection as a critical bottleneck in optimization. The methodology involves augmenting evaluations with a trajectory-aware proxy and stabilizing selections through reference-policy regularization and a chi-squared correction. Experiments conducted on HarmBench demonstrate that TACS significantly outperforms existing methods, achieving higher attack success rates and more stable optimization behavior within the same search budget, thus supporting the goals of improved jailbreak suffix optimization.
本文探讨了现有基于梯度的越狱后缀优化方法的局限性，这些方法通常仅根据候选者的即时损失进行选择，导致由于选择阶段奖励黑客行为而产生的次优长期结果。提出的方法TACS引入了一种轨迹感知的候选选择框架，通过结合轨迹感知代理和参考策略正则化以及判别器估计的卡方修正来增强评估过程，从而稳定选择。该方法有效缓解了与短视候选选择相关的问题，导致攻击成功率提高和优化行为更加稳定。在HarmBench上进行的实验表明，TACS在相同搜索预算内始终优于强基线方法，验证了其在增强越狱后缀优化方面的有效性。
Validity-Aware Jailbreak Evaluation for Large Language Models
Authors: Qilong Wu, Sahil Wadhwa, Pranab Mohanty, Giri Iyengar, Varun Chandrasekaran
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2026-08-31T23:57:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-31T23:57:53+00:00
Comments: To appear on EMNLP 2026 main
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Jailbreak robustness has become central to large language model (LLM) safety evaluation, yet prevailing methodologies rely primarily on refusal behavior, semantic resemblance, and intent-matching heuristics that emphasize linguistic plausibility rather than correctness. We identify a key limitation in existing evaluations: many jailbreak intents depend on instructional validity rather than epistemic factuality, allowing realistic-looking responses to be labeled successful despite being factually or procedurally incorrect. To address this gap, we propose Sequential Epistemic and Action-Level Validation (SEAV), a verification-centric jailbreak evaluation framework that decomposes responses into ordered steps and evaluates both validity and correctness. SEAV combines LLM-as-a-judge mechanisms for semantic interpretation with retrieval-grounded verification using external knowledge sources, assessing whether generated content is factually correct, structurally consistent, and operationally capable of advancing harmful objectives. Empirically, SEAV cuts the false-positive rate on SD-A (a curated strategic-dishonesty diagnostic) by 14.9\,pp vs. the strongest baseline, and reclassifies 22.1\%--51.0\% of sampled prior-labeled successes as invalid across three of four public benchmarks. Together, these results show that enforcing correctness substantially reshapes measured robustness: many previously labeled jailbreak successes are reclassified as invalid, and results are stable across the tested search backends and evaluator models. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Ardor-Wu/SEAV.
中文标题/摘要
标题：针对大型语言模型的有效性感知越狱评估
越狱的鲁棒性已成为大型语言模型（LLM）安全评估的核心，但现有方法主要依赖于拒绝行为、语义相似性和意图匹配启发式，这些方法强调语言的合理性而非正确性。我们识别出现有评估的一个关键限制：许多越狱意图依赖于指令有效性而非认知事实性，允许看似真实的响应被标记为成功，尽管在事实或程序上是错误的。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了顺序认知和行动级验证（SEAV），这是一个以验证为中心的越狱评估框架，它将响应分解为有序步骤，并评估有效性和正确性。SEAV结合了LLM作为评判机制的语义解释与使用外部知识源的检索基础验证，评估生成内容是否在事实上正确、结构上连贯，并在操作上能够推进有害目标。实证结果表明，SEAV将SD-A（一个策划的战略不诚实诊断）的假阳性率降低了14.9个百分点，相较于最强基线，并在四个公共基准中的三个重新将22.1%至51.0%的先前标记成功的样本重新分类为无效。这些结果表明，强制正确性显著重塑了测量的鲁棒性：许多先前标记的越狱成功被重新分类为无效，且结果在测试的搜索后端和评估模型中保持稳定。代码和数据可在https://github.com/Ardor-Wu/SEAV获取。

The proposed Sequential Epistemic and Action-Level Validation (SEAV) framework offers a novel approach by decomposing responses into ordered steps and evaluating both validity and correctness, integrating LLM-as-a-judge mechanisms with retrieval-grounded verification. This methodology significantly improves evaluation accuracy, achieving a 14.9 percentage point reduction in false positives on the SD-A benchmark and reclassifying a substantial portion of previously labeled successes as invalid. The findings indicate that prioritizing correctness leads to a more reliable assessment of jailbreak robustness, demonstrating the effectiveness of SEAV across multiple benchmarks.

Risk-Adjusted Harm Scoring for Automated Red Teaming for LLMs in Financial Services
Authors: Fabrizio Dimino, Bhaskarjit Sarmah, Stefano Pasquali
First: 2026-03-11T14:14:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-31T14:24:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing LLM safety evaluations rely on binary attack-success rates and domain-agnostic taxonomies, leaving regulated Banking, Financial Services, and Insurance (BFSI) deployments exposed to failures elicited through legally or professionally plausible framing. We introduce RAHS (Risk-Adjusted Harm Score), a risk-sensitive metric jointly capturing disclosure severity, disclaimer mitigation, and inter-judge agreement, and FinRedTeamBench, a 989-prompt benchmark spanning seven BFSI risk areas and 34 sub-categories mapped to regulatory frameworks. Evaluation uses an ensemble of three heterogeneous LLM judges, validated against human experts, and an adaptive multi-turn red-teaming pipeline. On nine open-weight models, RAHS preserves separation under near-ceiling ASR, ranking is stable under hyperparameter sweeps, and multi-turn pressure drives not only more jailbreaks but more operationally severe disclosures, exposing failure modes that single-turn, domain-agnostic evaluations cannot reveal.
中文标题/摘要
标题：针对金融服务中大型语言模型的自动红队测试的风险调整伤害评分
现有的大型语言模型安全评估依赖于二元攻击成功率和领域无关的分类法，使受监管的银行、金融服务和保险（BFSI）部署面临通过合法或专业上合理的框架引发的失败。我们引入了RAHS（风险调整伤害评分），这是一种风险敏感的指标，联合捕捉披露严重性、免责声明缓解和评审者间一致性，以及FinRedTeamBench，这是一个涵盖七个BFSI风险领域和34个子类别的989个提示基准，映射到监管框架。评估使用三位异构大型语言模型评审者的集成，经过与人类专家的验证，以及自适应多轮红队测试流程。在九个开放权重模型上，RAHS在接近上限的攻击成功率下保持分离，排名在超参数搜索中稳定，多轮压力不仅驱动更多越狱行为，还导致更严重的操作性披露，暴露出单轮、领域无关评估无法揭示的失败模式。

Do We Still Need Humans in the Loop? Human vs. LLM Annotation in Active Learning for TikTok Hate Speech Detection
Authors: Ahmad Dawar Hakimi, Lea Hirlimann, Isabelle Augenstein, Hinrich Schütze
First: 2026-04-15T14:10:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-31T11:56:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Annotating data remains a costly bottleneck for supervised NLP. Active learning (AL) reduces the number of human labels needed by selecting only the most informative instances, while instruction-tuned LLMs attack the same bottleneck from the other side, making labels cheap enough to annotate entire corpora. This raises two questions: can LLM labels replace human labels within the AL loop, and does AL remain necessary when entire corpora can be cheaply labeled? We investigate both by training supervised hate speech classifiers on a new dataset of 278K German political TikTok comments, comparing human and LLM annotation under matched conditions. LLM annotation at scale outperforms human-supervised classifiers at roughly one-tenth the cost, for both a closed-source (GPT-5.2) and an open-weight (Qwen3.5-122B-A10B) LLM, and the advantage is robust under soft-label evaluation. It hinges on the annotation interface: only a two-question decomposition mirroring the human annotation task unlocks it. AL provides no reliable advantage over random sampling in our prefiltered pool. Error structure depends on the LLM: only GPT-5.2 matches the human FP/FN balance, while other variants over-flag border-control and economic-competition discourse. Humans remain essential as evaluators; for training labels, the question shifts to which LLM, which interface, and what shape of pool.
中文标题/摘要
标题：我们还需要人类参与吗？在TikTok仇恨言论检测中的人类与LLM注释的主动学习
数据注释仍然是监督NLP的一个成本瓶颈。主动学习（AL）通过仅选择最具信息量的实例来减少所需的人类标签数量，而经过指令调优的LLM则从另一个角度解决了同样的瓶颈，使得标签便宜到足以注释整个语料库。这引发了两个问题：LLM标签能否在AL循环中替代人类标签，以及当整个语料库可以被廉价标注时，AL是否仍然必要？我们通过在一个新的278K德语政治TikTok评论数据集上训练监督仇恨言论分类器来研究这两个问题，比较在匹配条件下的人类和LLM注释。大规模的LLM注释在成本约为人类监督分类器的十分之一的情况下表现优于人类监督分类器，无论是闭源（GPT-5.2）还是开放权重（Qwen3.5-122B-A10B）LLM，并且在软标签评估下这一优势是稳健的。这取决于注释接口：只有一个与人类注释任务相对应的两问题分解才能解锁它。在我们预过滤的池中，AL对随机抽样没有可靠的优势。错误结构依赖于LLM：只有GPT-5.2匹配人类的FP/FN平衡，而其他变体则过度标记边境控制和经济竞争话语。人类作为评估者仍然至关重要；对于训练标签，问题转向哪个LLM、哪个接口以及什么形状的池。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenge of data annotation in supervised natural language processing (NLP), particularly in the context of hate speech detection on TikTok, where traditional methods are costly and time-consuming. Previous approaches relied heavily on human annotation, which is expensive and limited in scalability, while recent advancements in instruction-tuned large language models (LLMs) offer a potential solution by providing cheaper labels. This study proposes a method that integrates LLMs into the active learning (AL) framework, specifically comparing human and LLM annotations on a dataset of 278,000 German political TikTok comments. The findings reveal that LLM annotation significantly outperforms human annotation at a fraction of the cost, with the performance being contingent on the annotation interface used. The research concludes that while LLMs can effectively replace human labels in training classifiers, humans are still necessary for evaluation, thus reshaping the role of human involvement in the annotation process.
