Quality-Diversity Red-Teaming: Automated Generation of High-Quality and Diverse Attackers for Large Language Models
Authors: Ren-Jian Wang, Ke Xue, Zeyu Qin, Ziniu Li, Sheng Tang, Hao-Tian Li, Shengcai Liu, Zhi Yu, Yuanpeng Tan, Chao Qian
First: 2025-06-08T13:07:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-08-01T10:21:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Ensuring the safety and robustness of large language models (LLMs) is a fundamental challenge and a critical prerequisite for the responsible deployment of artificial intelligence. Red-teaming, a systematic framework to identify adversarial prompts that elicit harmful responses from target LLMs, has emerged as a crucial safety evaluation paradigm. Within this framework, the diversity of adversarial prompts is critical for a comprehensive safety assessment. However, previous red-teaming approaches often pursue diversity through simplistic metrics such as word frequency or sentence embedding similarity, which may not capture meaningful variation in attack strategies. In addition, the common practice of training a single attacker model restricts coverage across all potential attack styles and risk categories. This paper introduces Quality-Diversity Red-Teaming (QDRT), a new framework designed to address these limitations. QDRT achieves goal-driven diversity through behavior-conditioned training and implements a behavioral replay buffer in an open-ended manner. In addition, it trains multiple specialized attackers capable of generating high-quality attacks across diverse styles and risk categories. Our empirical evaluation demonstrates that QDRT generates attacks that are both more diverse and more effective against a wide range of target LLMs, including open-source models \texttt{GPT-2}, \texttt{Llama-3}, \texttt{Gemma-2}, \texttt{Qwen2.5}, and commercial models \texttt{GPT-4.1} and \texttt{GPT-5-Chat}. This work advances the field of LLM safety by providing a systematic and effective approach to automated red-teaming, ultimately supporting the responsible deployment of LLMs. Our code is available at https://github.com/lamda-bbo/QDRT.
中文标题/摘要
标题：质量-多样性红队：大型语言模型高质量和多样化攻击者的自动生成
确保大型语言模型（LLMs）的安全性和稳健性是一个基本挑战，也是负责任地部署人工智能的关键前提。红队是一种系统框架，用于识别引发目标LLMs有害响应的对抗性提示，已成为重要的安全评估范式。在此框架内，对抗性提示的多样性对于全面的安全评估至关重要。然而，以往的红队方法往往通过简单的指标（如词频或句子嵌入相似性）追求多样性，这可能无法捕捉攻击策略的有意义变化。此外，训练单一攻击者模型的常见做法限制了对所有潜在攻击风格和风险类别的覆盖。本文介绍了质量-多样性红队（QDRT），这是一个旨在解决这些局限性的新框架。QDRT通过行为条件训练实现目标驱动的多样性，并以开放式方式实现行为重放缓冲区。此外，它训练多个专门的攻击者，能够在多样化风格和风险类别中生成高质量的攻击。我们的实证评估表明，QDRT生成的攻击在多样性和有效性方面均优于针对广泛目标LLMs的攻击，包括开源模型\texttt{GPT-2}、\texttt{Llama-3}、\texttt{Gemma-2}、\texttt{Qwen2.5}，以及商业模型\texttt{GPT-4.1}和\texttt{GPT-5-Chat}。这项工作通过提供系统有效的自动化红队方法，推动了LLM安全领域的发展，最终支持LLMs的负责任部署。我们的代码可在https://github.com/lamda-bbo/QDRT获取。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the critical challenge of ensuring the safety and robustness of large language models (LLMs), which is essential for their responsible deployment. Previous red-teaming methods often relied on simplistic metrics for diversity, such as word frequency, failing to capture meaningful variations in attack strategies, and typically trained a single attacker model, limiting the diversity of attack styles. The proposed Quality-Diversity Red-Teaming (QDRT) framework overcomes these issues by employing behavior-conditioned training and a behavioral replay buffer, allowing for the training of multiple specialized attackers that generate high-quality and diverse attacks. This paper contributes to LLM safety by providing a systematic approach to automated red-teaming, demonstrating through empirical evaluation that QDRT produces more diverse and effective attacks against various target LLMs, thereby supporting the goal of responsible AI deployment.
本研究解决了确保大型语言模型（LLMs）安全性和鲁棒性的关键挑战，特别是通过红队框架识别引发有害响应的对抗性提示。以往的方法依赖于简单的多样性度量，如词频，这无法捕捉攻击策略中的有意义变化，并且通常使用单一攻击者模型，限制了对多样化攻击风格的覆盖。所提出的质量-多样性红队（QDRT）框架通过行为条件训练和行为重放缓冲区克服了这些局限性，允许训练多个专门的攻击者，生成高质量和多样化的攻击。本文通过实证评估证明，QDRT能够针对各种目标LLM生成更具多样性和有效性的攻击，从而为LLM的负责任部署提供系统化的自动化红队方法。
EvalSafetyGap: A Hybrid Survey and Conceptual Framework for LLM Evaluation-Safety Failures
Authors: Buğra Alperen Uluırmak, Rifat Kurban
First: 2026-06-29T12:33:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-31T12:14:44+00:00
Comments: 74 pages, 2 figures, 4 tables. Hybrid systematic survey and conceptual framework on LLM evaluation and AI-safety failures, synthesizing 373 primary studies (2018-2026). Introduces the EvalSafetyGap framework (Instability Decomposition, Alignment Trilemma) and reports an exploratory ten-model audit. Submitted as a review/survey article; not currently under consideration elsewhere
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a systematic survey and conceptual synthesis of the shared measurement problem underlying large language model (LLM) evaluation and AI safety: benchmark scores, reward signals, and safety metrics can improve while the capabilities and alignment properties they are meant to represent remain uncertain. Synthesizing 373 primary studies published between 2018 and 2026, the survey organizes evidence on benchmark validity, contamination, dynamic evaluation, LLM-as-a-judge protocols, adversarial safety testing, reward and proxy optimization, mechanistic interpretability, and AI governance into an eight-stream evidence taxonomy. Building on this synthesis, we introduce EvalSafetyGap, a conceptual framework that unifies benchmark-validity and alignment-failure research as a shared proxy-target divergence problem under optimization pressure, formalized through a Goodhart-inspired Instability Decomposition and an Alignment Trilemma. An exploratory ten-model public-evidence audit illustrates the framework by showing why capability, behavioral robustness, and governance disclosure should be reported as separate evidence layers rather than collapsed into a single safety score. The survey closes with a research agenda for dynamic and contamination-resistant benchmarks, pre-specified multi-attempt threat models, version-locked evaluation, transparent source reporting, and validated mechanistic safety indicators, offering researchers, model developers, and AI auditors a shared vocabulary for measurement-aware LLM safety evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EvalSafetyGap：大型语言模型评估安全失败的混合调查与概念框架
本文系统性地调查和概念性综合了大型语言模型（LLM）评估和人工智能安全的共享测量问题：基准分数、奖励信号和安全指标可以改善，而它们所代表的能力和对齐属性仍然不确定。通过综合2018年至2026年间发表的373项主要研究，调查将基准有效性、污染、动态评估、LLM作为评判者的协议、对抗性安全测试、奖励和代理优化、机制可解释性以及人工智能治理的证据组织成八个证据分类流。在此基础上，我们引入了EvalSafetyGap，一个概念框架，将基准有效性和对齐失败研究统一为在优化压力下的共享代理-目标偏差问题，通过受Goodhart启发的不稳定性分解和对齐三难问题进行形式化。一个探索性的十模型公共证据审计通过展示能力、行为鲁棒性和治理披露为何应作为单独的证据层而非合并为单一安全分数，来说明该框架。调查最后提出了一个研究议程，涉及动态和抗污染基准、预先指定的多次尝试威胁模型、版本锁定评估、透明的来源报告和验证的机制安全指标，为研究人员、模型开发者和人工智能审计员提供了一个共享的测量意识的LLM安全评估词汇。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the shared measurement challenges in evaluating large language models (LLMs) and their safety, highlighting that improvements in benchmark scores and safety metrics do not necessarily correlate with actual capabilities and alignment properties. Previous methods often failed to adequately address issues of benchmark validity and alignment failures, leading to a lack of clarity in safety evaluations. The proposed EvalSafetyGap framework offers a novel approach by framing these issues as a proxy-target divergence problem under optimization pressure, utilizing concepts like Instability Decomposition and the Alignment Trilemma. This framework is well-motivated as it synthesizes findings from 373 studies and provides a structured taxonomy for evidence on LLM evaluation. The exploratory audit of ten models demonstrates the necessity of reporting capability, behavioral robustness, and governance separately, thereby supporting the goal of creating more reliable and transparent safety evaluations for LLMs.

On-Policy Distillation for LLM Safety: A Routing Approach to Template-Robust Realignment
Authors: Yongjian Guo, Wanlun Ma, Lingyu Shen, Xi Xiao, Sheng Wen
First: 2026-07-29T16:07:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-29T16:07:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fine-tuning is the dominant paradigm for specializing large language models (LLMs), yet it exposes a critical vulnerability: malicious data providers can embed harmful behaviors into downstream corpora, creating models that retain professional skills while violating human values on demand. Existing safety-realignment defenses often fail in practice due to three key limitations: they frequently cause catastrophic forgetting of specialized skills; their effectiveness collapses when the defender cannot observe the attacker's prompt template; and successfully realigned models remain susceptible to re-jailbreaking via simple system prompt switches. To address these challenges, we propose Routing-based On-Policy Distillation (ROPD), a novel realignment framework that models the divergence between aligned and compromised output probability distributions rather than fitting specific prompt templates. We conduct extensive experiments comparing ROPD against four state-of-the-art baselines across three datasets and three base models with varying alignment strengths. Our results demonstrate that when baseline defenses face template mismatches, often accompanied by severe degradation in downstream task performance. In contrast, ROPD substantially mitigates template-mismatch risks, maintaining superior robustness in both defense effectiveness and capability preservation. While our analysis indicates ROPD is not entirely immune to template shifts, its performance degradation is negligible compared to existing methods, establishing a new standard for robust LLM realignment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于策略蒸馏的LLM安全性：一种模板鲁棒重对齐的路由方法
微调是专门化大型语言模型（LLMs）的主流范式，但它暴露了一个关键漏洞：恶意数据提供者可以将有害行为嵌入下游语料库，创建在需求下保留专业技能但违反人类价值观的模型。现有的安全重对齐防御在实践中常常失败，主要有三个关键限制：它们经常导致专业技能的灾难性遗忘；当防御者无法观察攻击者的提示模板时，其有效性崩溃；成功重对齐的模型仍然容易通过简单的系统提示切换重新越狱。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了基于路由的策略蒸馏（ROPD），这是一种新颖的重对齐框架，它建模对齐和妥协输出概率分布之间的差异，而不是拟合特定的提示模板。我们进行了广泛的实验，将ROPD与四个最先进的基线在三个数据集和三个基础模型上进行比较，后者具有不同的对齐强度。我们的结果表明，当基线防御面临模板不匹配时，通常伴随着下游任务性能的严重下降。相比之下，ROPD显著减轻了模板不匹配的风险，在防御有效性和能力保留方面保持了优越的鲁棒性。尽管我们的分析表明ROPD并非完全免疫于模板变化，但其性能下降与现有方法相比微不足道，为鲁棒的LLM重对齐建立了新的标准。

The paper contributes a novel methodology that demonstrates ROPD's superior robustness against template mismatches, maintaining performance across various datasets and models. Experimental results show that ROPD significantly outperforms existing defenses, achieving better defense effectiveness and capability preservation, thus establishing a new standard for LLM realignment.

MEDIC: Comprehensive Evaluation of Leading Indicators for LLM Safety and Utility in Clinical Applications
Authors: Praveenkumar Kanithi, Clément Christophe, Marco AF Pimentel, Tathagata Raha, Prateek Munjal, Nada Saadi, Hamza A Javed, Svetlana Maslenkova, Nasir Hayat, Ronnie Rajan, Shadab Khan
First: 2024-09-11T14:44:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-28T17:58:21+00:00
Comments: Published in Transactions on Machine Learning Research (06/2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve superhuman performance on standardized medical licensing exams, these static benchmarks have become saturated and increasingly disconnected from the functional requirements of clinical workflows. To bridge the gap between theoretical capability and verified utility, we introduce MEDIC, a comprehensive evaluation framework establishing leading indicators of clinical LLM competence across five dimensions. These upfront indicators reveal cross-benchmark capability gaps, such as the divergence between static knowledge retrieval and functional execution, that inform model selection before costly deployment-based evaluation. Beyond standard question-answering, we assess operational capabilities using deterministic execution protocols and a novel Cross-Examination Framework (CEF), which quantifies information fidelity and hallucination rates without reliance on reference texts. Our evaluation across a heterogeneous task suite exposes critical performance trade-offs: we identify a significant knowledge-execution gap, where proficiency in static retrieval does not predict success in operational tasks such as clinical calculation or SQL generation. Furthermore, we observe a divergence between passive safety (refusal) and active safety (error detection), revealing that models fine-tuned for high refusal rates often fail to reliably audit clinical documentation for factual accuracy. These findings demonstrate that no single architecture dominates across all dimensions, highlighting the necessity of a portfolio approach to clinical model deployment. We accompany this work with a publicly available MEDIC leaderboard at https://hf.co/spaces/m42-health/MEDIC-Benchmark.

GPT-Red: Automated Red Teaming via Self-Play at Scale
Authors: Eric Wallace, Christopher A. Choquette-Choo, Nikhil Kandpal, Sam Toyer, Dylan Hunn, Stephanie Lin, Yuxin Wen, Xiangyu Qi, Christopher Wolff, Zizhao Wang, Milad Nasr, Sicheng Zhu, Chuan Guo, Juan Felipe Cerón Uribe, Kaiwen Wang, Aiden Low, Kai Xiao, Kai Chen
First: 2026-07-28T16:03:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-28T16:03:39+00:00
Comments: 28 pages.13 main pages and 13 main figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce \textbf{GPT-Red}, an automated red-teaming agent that is trained to discover novel prompt injection attacks against frontier LLMs. The goal of this model is to evaluate and improve the robustness of our production systems. To this end, we use it to adversarially train GPT-5.6, our most robust model to prompt injections to date. To create GPT-Red, we design a scalable self-play algorithm where the model is tasked with attacking a diverse population of simultaneously-trained defender agents. We train the model on realistic red-teaming environments using compute on the same scale as some of our largest RL post-training runs, making it the single-largest LLM safety training run ever documented. GPT-Red excels at red-teaming: it reliably breaks our past models up to GPT-5.5, it finds more successful attacks than human red-teamers, and it generalizes to held-out environments, defender models, and harnesses. In the future, we expect that as we improve the robustness of each new GPT model, it will in turn will provide better learning signal for \textit{even stronger} red-teamer agents, thus unlocking a self-improvement flywheel.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GPT-Red：通过自我对弈实现自动化红队测试
我们介绍了\textbf{GPT-Red}，一个自动化红队代理，旨在发现针对前沿大型语言模型（LLM）的新型提示注入攻击。该模型的目标是评估和提高我们生产系统的鲁棒性。为此，我们利用它对GPT-5.6进行对抗性训练，这是迄今为止对提示注入最鲁棒的模型。为了创建GPT-Red，我们设计了一种可扩展的自我对弈算法，模型的任务是攻击一组同时训练的多样化防御代理。我们在现实的红队测试环境中训练该模型，使用的计算资源与我们一些最大的强化学习后训练运行相当，使其成为有史以来记录的最大规模LLM安全训练运行。GPT-Red在红队测试中表现出色：它可靠地击破我们过去的模型（最高至GPT-5.5），找到的成功攻击次数超过人类红队员，并且能够推广到未见过的环境、防御模型和工具。在未来，我们预计随着我们提高每个新GPT模型的鲁棒性，它将为\textit{更强大的}红队代理提供更好的学习信号，从而解锁自我改进的飞轮效应。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the need for improved robustness in large language models (LLMs) against prompt injection attacks, which have been inadequately handled by previous methods that often rely on manual red-teaming efforts. The proposed approach, GPT-Red, utilizes a scalable self-play algorithm to automate the red-teaming process, allowing the model to generate and test novel attacks against a variety of defender agents simultaneously. This method overcomes the limitations of human red-teamers by consistently identifying more successful attacks and demonstrating generalization across different environments and models. The contribution of this paper lies in the development of GPT-Red, which not only enhances the security of the GPT-5.6 model but also sets a precedent for future models to benefit from a self-improvement cycle in adversarial training. The methodology involves training on extensive red-teaming environments, achieving superior performance in breaking previous models and establishing a foundation for ongoing improvements in LLM safety.

TRIDENT: Benchmarking LLM Safety in Finance, Medicine, and Law
Authors: Zheng Hui, Yijiang River Dong, Ehsan Shareghi, Nigel Collier
First: 2025-07-22T17:52:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-27T09:44:33+00:00
Comments: COLM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in high-risk domains such as law, finance, and medicine, systematically evaluating their domain-specific safety and compliance becomes critical. While prior work has largely focused on improving LLM performance in these domains, it has often neglected the evaluation of domain-specific safety risks. To bridge this gap, we first define domain-specific safety principles for LLMs based on the AMA Principles of Medical Ethics, the ABA Model Rules of Professional Conduct, and the CFA Institute Code of Ethics. Building on this foundation, we introduce Trident-Bench, a benchmark specifically targeting LLM safety in the legal, financial, and medical domains. We evaluated 19 general-purpose and domain-specialized models on Trident-Bench and show that it effectively reveals key safety gaps -- strong generalist models (e.g., GPT, Gemini) can meet basic expectations, whereas domain-specialized models often struggle with subtle ethical nuances. This highlights an urgent need for finer-grained domain-specific safety improvements. By introducing Trident-Bench, our work provides one of the first systematic resources for studying LLM safety in law and finance, and lays the groundwork for future research aimed at reducing the safety risks of deploying LLMs in professionally regulated fields. Code and benchmark will be released at: https://github.com/zackhuiiiii/TRIDENT.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TRIDENT：金融、医学和法律领域大型语言模型安全性基准测试
随着大型语言模型（LLMs）在法律、金融和医学等高风险领域的日益应用，系统评估其领域特定的安全性和合规性变得至关重要。尽管之前的研究主要集中在提高LLM在这些领域的性能，但往往忽视了对领域特定安全风险的评估。为填补这一空白，我们首先基于医学伦理AMA原则、ABA职业行为模型规则和CFA协会伦理规范定义了LLM的领域特定安全原则。在此基础上，我们推出了Trident-Bench，这是一个专门针对法律、金融和医学领域LLM安全性的基准测试。我们在Trident-Bench上评估了19个通用和领域专用模型，结果表明它有效揭示了关键的安全差距——强大的通用模型（如GPT、Gemini）能够满足基本期望，而领域专用模型往往在微妙的伦理细节上表现不佳。这突显了对更细致的领域特定安全改进的迫切需求。通过引入Trident-Bench，我们的工作提供了研究法律和金融领域LLM安全性的首个系统资源之一，并为未来旨在降低在专业监管领域部署LLM的安全风险的研究奠定了基础。代码和基准测试将发布于：https://github.com/zackhuiiiii/TRIDENT。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the critical need for evaluating the safety and compliance of large language models (LLMs) in high-risk domains such as law, finance, and medicine, where previous studies have primarily focused on performance rather than safety risks. The proposed approach, Trident-Bench, differs from existing methods by establishing domain-specific safety principles derived from established ethical guidelines and systematically benchmarking LLMs against these principles. This method effectively identifies significant safety gaps, revealing that while generalist models can meet basic safety expectations, specialized models often fail to address complex ethical issues. The contribution of this paper lies in providing a systematic resource for assessing LLM safety in regulated fields, with the evaluation of 19 models demonstrating the necessity for targeted safety improvements in these domains. The methodology involves defining safety principles and benchmarking models, achieving insights that support the urgent need for enhanced safety measures in LLM deployment.
本研究解决了在法律、金融和医学等高风险领域评估大型语言模型（LLMs）安全性和合规性的重要需求，以往的研究主要集中在性能上，而忽视了安全风险。现有方法缺乏系统评估领域特定安全性的手段，本文通过基于既定伦理准则定义安全原则，并引入Trident-Bench基准来弥补这一空白。本文的贡献在于建立了一个评估LLM安全性的资源，揭示了通用模型能够满足基本安全期望，而专业模型往往无法处理复杂的伦理问题。该方法论涉及对19个模型进行Trident-Bench评估，显示出显著的安全差距，并强调了在这些受监管领域内针对LLM安全性进行有针对性改进的必要性。
ASEval: Automated Trajectory-Level Security Testing for Autonomous Agents
Authors: Jianan Ma, Xiaohu Du, Ruixiao Lin, Yaoxiang Bian, Jialuo Chen, Yunhao Feng, Xiaofang Yang, Shiwen Cui, Changhua Meng, Xinhao Deng, Jingyi Wang, Zhen Wang
First: 2026-05-21T11:07:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-24T06:09:29+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 12 figures, 8 tables. Code and data available at https://github.com/antgroup/Agent3Sigma-Stage
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
As autonomous agents (e.g., OpenClaw) increasingly operate with deep system-level privileges to execute complex tasks, they introduce severe, unmitigated security risks. Existing LLM safety testing methods are largely built around prompt-level inputs and response-level judgments, while recent agent benchmarks remain limited in automation, trajectory coverage, and action-grounded judgment. In this work, we present ASEval, a novel automated security testing framework for autonomous agents. ASEval generates realistic benign multi-turn conversations, perturbs them into risk test cases through carefully designed perturbation operators, executes each case against the target agent, and applies an action-grounded oracle to judge the complete trace. We instantiate ASEval to construct A3S-Bench, a test suite of 2,254 executable multi-turn test cases spanning diverse usage scenarios, delivery patterns, and agent-level security risks. Extensive evaluation on 11 LLM-backed OpenClaw agents confirms that ASEval effectively exposes pervasive security failures. In particular, our proposed perturbation operators raise the average risk-trigger rate from 22.9% under existing injection techniques to 47.4%, as judged by an action-grounded oracle that matches human annotation at 93.2% binary F1. Our further analysis and case studies illustrate that such failures emerge from multi-turn delivery, persistent state, and tool-mediated actions that response-level evaluation overlooks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ASEval：自动化轨迹级安全测试框架用于自主代理
随着自主代理（例如，OpenClaw）越来越多地以深层系统级权限执行复杂任务，它们引入了严重且未缓解的安全风险。现有的LLM安全测试方法主要围绕提示级输入和响应级判断构建，而最近的代理基准在自动化、轨迹覆盖和基于动作的判断方面仍然有限。在本研究中，我们提出了ASEval，一个新颖的自主代理自动化安全测试框架。ASEval生成现实的良性多轮对话，通过精心设计的扰动操作将其扰动为风险测试用例，针对目标代理执行每个用例，并应用基于动作的oracle来判断完整的轨迹。我们实例化ASEval构建了A3S-Bench，一个包含2,254个可执行多轮测试用例的测试套件，涵盖多种使用场景、交付模式和代理级安全风险。对11个基于LLM的OpenClaw代理的广泛评估确认ASEval有效暴露了普遍的安全失败。特别是，我们提出的扰动操作将现有注入技术下的平均风险触发率从22.9%提高到47.4%，根据与人类注释匹配的93.2%二元F1的基于动作的oracle进行判断。我们的进一步分析和案例研究表明，这些失败源于多轮交付、持续状态和工具介导的动作，而响应级评估则忽视了这些因素。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the growing security risks posed by autonomous agents, which operate with deep system-level privileges to perform complex tasks. Previous methods for safety testing primarily focused on prompt-level inputs and response-level evaluations, leading to limitations in automation and trajectory coverage. The proposed ASEval framework differs by generating realistic multi-turn conversations and transforming them into risk test cases using specialized perturbation operators, allowing for a more comprehensive evaluation of agent security. This approach is well-motivated as it targets the shortcomings of existing methods and enhances the detection of security failures. ASEval contributes by creating the A3S-Bench test suite, which includes 2,254 executable test cases that cover various scenarios and risks. Evaluation on 11 LLM-backed OpenClaw agents demonstrates that ASEval significantly improves the detection of security vulnerabilities, increasing the average risk-trigger rate from 22.9% to 47.4%, with an action-grounded oracle achieving a binary F1 score of 93.2%, thus supporting the framework's goals.
本研究关注自主代理在深层系统权限下操作所带来的重大安全风险，强调现有安全测试方法在提示级输入和响应级评估方面的局限性。这些方法往往无法充分覆盖代理交互和安全漏洞的复杂性。所提出的ASEval框架通过生成真实的多轮对话、将其扰动为风险测试用例，并采用基于动作的oracle进行全面评估，从而实现安全测试过程的自动化。ASEval贡献了一个测试套件A3S-Bench，包含2254个可执行的测试用例，涵盖各种场景和安全风险，证明其在揭示11个基于LLM的OpenClaw代理的安全失败方面的有效性。该框架将风险触发率从22.9%显著提高到47.4%，评估结果与人类判断高度一致，从而支持其增强自主代理安全测试的目标。
Geometric Configurations of Perturbed Jailbreak Prompts
Authors: Lynn Delcon, Andres Algaba, Vincent Ginis
First: 2026-07-22T11:52:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-22T11:52:43+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 9 figures, 7 tables, 2nd Workshop on Safe AI (SafeAI)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Perturbation techniques that turn unsuccessful jailbreak prompts into successful ones are continuously evolving, constituting a major security threat to LLM safety. In this paper, we investigate the internal representations of such string-level perturbed jailbreak inputs in the small weight models of the Qwen-2.5-1.5B/-3B/-7B-Instruct and Llama-3.2-1B/-3B/-3.1-8B-Instruct families. We select two representation spaces: the last-layer-last-token embedding space and the top-50 next-token probability space. The former space separates prompts based on their spelling and format, while the latter space is effectively one-dimensional but appears more complex to cluster. Within our refusal-dominated answer set we find no behavioral hyperplane in either space. Only the next token "Sure" in the 1.5B Qwen model, and both tokens "," and "ĊĊ" in the 1$ Llama model, display a significant association with a compliant-labeled answer.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扰动越狱提示的几何配置
扰动技术将不成功的越狱提示转变为成功的提示，持续演变，构成对大型语言模型安全的重大威胁。本文研究了Qwen-2.5-1.5B/-3B/-7B-Instruct和Llama-3.2-1B/-3B/-3.1-8B-Instruct系列的小权重模型中此类字符串级扰动越狱输入的内部表示。我们选择了两个表示空间：最后一层最后一个标记的嵌入空间和前50个下一个标记的概率空间。前者根据提示的拼写和格式进行分离，而后者有效地是一维的，但在聚类时显得更复杂。在我们的拒绝主导的答案集中，我们在任一空间中都未发现行为超平面。只有1.5B Qwen模型中的下一个标记“Sure”，以及1$ Llama模型中的两个标记“,”和“ĊĊ”与合规标记的答案显示出显著关联。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the evolving threat of perturbation techniques that transform unsuccessful jailbreak prompts into successful ones, posing significant security risks to large language model (LLM) safety. Previous methods have primarily focused on surface-level analyses, which fail to capture the deeper internal representations of these prompts, leading to incomplete understanding and mitigation strategies. This paper proposes a novel approach that investigates the internal representations of perturbed jailbreak inputs within specific embedding spaces of the Qwen and Llama model families, revealing that while the last-layer-last-token embedding space separates prompts based on spelling and format, the next-token probability space is more complex and lacks clear behavioral hyperplanes. The findings indicate that only specific tokens in certain models show a significant association with compliant responses, contributing to a deeper understanding of LLM behavior under perturbation and enhancing strategies for improving model safety.
本研究探讨了扰动技术如何将不成功的越狱提示转变为成功的提示，这对大型语言模型（LLM）的安全构成了重大威胁。以往的方法主要集中在提示的表面分析，常常无法捕捉到模型行为的潜在复杂性。本文提出了一种新颖的方法，研究特定嵌入空间中扰动越狱输入的内部表示，提供了对这些模型如何处理此类输入的更细致理解。研究方法包括分析最后一层最后一个标记的嵌入空间和前50个下一个标记的概率空间，以识别行为模式。研究结果表明，尽管大多数提示没有明显的行为超平面，但特定模型中的某些标记与合规响应之间存在显著相关性，为LLM的脆弱性提供了有价值的见解，并增强了对模型安全机制的理解。
Stress Testing Concept Erasure with Large Language Model Agents
Authors: Yuyang Xue, Feng Chen, Zhihua Liu, Edward Moroshko, Jingyu Sun, Steven McDonagh, Sotirios A. Tsaftaris
First: 2026-07-20T12:38:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-22T09:45:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Concept erasure aims to remove semantic concepts from a trained generative model and is increasingly important for responsible AI deployment. However, verifying whether a model has robustly removed targeted concepts remains a critical challenge. Existing evaluation methods are typically pre-defined and static, failing to expose vulnerabilities under diverse natural-language probes and challenging conditions. Moreover, manually designed evaluation strategies can be biased and difficult to scale. We posit that concept erasure evaluation is best formulated as an adaptive hypothesis search, operationalised by agents that iteratively propose, critique, and verify tests to systematically expand coverage of failure modes. To this end, we propose Stress Testing Agents for Concept Erasure (STACE), a framework that autonomously stress-tests concept-erased models using multiple Large Language Model (LLM) agents, by iteratively generating and verifying stress-testing hypotheses grounded by external knowledge. We also introduce a suite of metrics for assessing the performance and efficiency of LLM-agent-powered stress-testing frameworks. Our extensive experiments show that STACE outperforms five LLM-based evaluation baselines on four concept categories. Further analysis across two T2I models, six concept erasure approaches, and various erasure strengths show that STACE is robust for different settings. We also show that STACE can be adapted beyond concept erasure evaluation to other problem domains, such as LLM jailbreaking. Our code is available anonymously.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大型语言模型代理的概念消除压力测试
概念消除旨在从训练好的生成模型中移除语义概念，对于负责任的人工智能部署越来越重要。然而，验证模型是否稳健地移除了目标概念仍然是一个关键挑战。现有的评估方法通常是预定义和静态的，未能在多样的自然语言探测和挑战条件下暴露出脆弱性。此外，手动设计的评估策略可能存在偏见且难以扩展。我们认为，概念消除评估最好被表述为一种自适应假设搜索，由代理迭代提出、批评和验证测试，以系统性地扩展失败模式的覆盖范围。为此，我们提出了概念消除压力测试代理（STACE），这是一个框架，通过多个大型语言模型（LLM）代理自主地对概念消除模型进行压力测试，迭代生成和验证基于外部知识的压力测试假设。我们还引入了一套用于评估LLM代理驱动的压力测试框架性能和效率的指标。我们的广泛实验表明，STACE在四个概念类别上优于五个基于LLM的评估基线。对两个T2I模型、六种概念消除方法和各种消除强度的进一步分析表明，STACE在不同设置下都表现稳健。我们还展示了STACE可以适应于概念消除评估以外的其他问题领域，如LLM越狱。我们的代码可匿名获取。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenge of concept erasure in generative models, which is crucial for responsible AI deployment, particularly in verifying the removal of targeted concepts. Previous methods for evaluating concept erasure are static and predefined, leading to biases and limitations in scalability, which the proposed approach, Stress Testing Agents for Concept Erasure (STACE), seeks to overcome by employing adaptive hypothesis search through multiple Large Language Model agents. This method allows for iterative generation and verification of stress-testing hypotheses, thus systematically expanding the coverage of potential failure modes. The contribution of the paper lies in the development of a robust framework that not only enhances the evaluation of concept erasure but can also be adapted to other domains, achieving superior performance compared to five existing LLM-based evaluation baselines across various concept categories and settings. The extensive experiments demonstrate that STACE effectively supports its goals by providing a more dynamic and comprehensive evaluation methodology.
本研究解决了生成模型中的概念消除问题，这对负责任的人工智能部署至关重要。以往的概念消除评估方法通常是静态和预定义的，导致在识别漏洞时存在偏见和有限的可扩展性。提出的方法，概念消除压力测试代理（STACE），创新性地将评估形式化为自适应假设搜索，利用多个大型语言模型代理迭代生成和验证测试，以增强故障模式的覆盖范围。这种方法动机充分，因为它通过提供动态和可扩展的评估框架来解决现有策略的局限性。本文贡献了一种新颖的方法论，证明STACE在四个概念类别上优于五种现有的基于LLM的评估基线，表明其在各种设置中的有效性和适应性，包括超出概念消除评估的应用。
Don't Want Your LLM to Recommend Nuclear Strike? Try Asking It in Japanese
Authors: Rian Touchent
Venue: Proceedings of the 6th Workshop on Trustworthy NLP (TrustNLP 2026), Jul 2026, San Diego, United States. pp.489-502
First: 2026-07-21T10:09:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-21T10:09:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models are increasingly used in strategic and advisory contexts, yet their safety alignment is typically evaluated in English only. We test nine models from six providers and ask whether the language of a prompt can change a model's decision in a high-stakes scenario. We use single-turn game-theoretic vignettes in which a model advises a nuclear-armed nation on whether to strike a defenseless opponent. The prompt is intentionally amoral and strategically identical across languages. We find that Japanese prompts reduce launch rates in the Claude model family: Claude Sonnet 4.6 drops from 40% to 0% in scenarios where the strike is unnecessary and from 93% to 17% in contested scenarios, with minimal effect when the strike is strategically rational. The effect extends to Gemini Pro 3.1 (53% to 13%). A cross-language experiment isolates the mechanism: when instructed to reason in Japanese in an English prompt, launch rates drop from 93% to 37%. It is the language the model is asked to reason in, not the language of the input, that drives the effect. When reasoning in Japanese, models spontaneously generate moral vocabulary (''moral cost'', ''millions of lives'') that is entirely absent from the prompt. Five other models show no language effect, but they launch in nearly every condition regardless of language. The effect requires a model that already hesitates in English. These results show that LLM safety behavior is language-dependent, and that evaluating in English alone can miss both risks and safeguards encoded in other languages.
中文标题/摘要
标题：不想让你的大型语言模型推荐核打击？试试用日语提问
大型语言模型在战略和咨询环境中越来越多地被使用，但它们的安全对齐通常仅用英语进行评估。我们测试了来自六个提供商的九个模型，并询问提示的语言是否会在高风险场景中改变模型的决策。我们使用单回合博弈论小插曲，其中模型建议一个拥有核武器的国家是否对一个无防御的对手进行打击。提示在道德上是故意中立的，并在各语言中战略上相同。我们发现日语提示降低了Claude模型系列的发射率：在打击不必要的场景中，Claude Sonnet 4.6的发射率从40%降至0%，在争议场景中从93%降至17%，而在战略合理的情况下影响最小。该效应扩展到Gemini Pro 3.1（53%降至13%）。跨语言实验隔离了机制：当在英语提示中被指示用日语推理时，发射率从93%降至37%。影响是由模型被要求用何种语言推理驱动的，而不是输入的语言。当用日语推理时，模型自发生成道德词汇（“道德成本”，“数百万生命”），这些词汇在提示中完全缺失。其他五个模型没有语言效应，但在几乎所有条件下都发射，无论语言如何。该效应需要一个在英语中已经犹豫的模型。这些结果表明，LLM的安全行为依赖于语言，仅用英语评估可能会错过其他语言中编码的风险和保障。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the safety alignment of large language models (LLMs) in strategic contexts, particularly focusing on how the language of prompts influences decision-making in high-stakes scenarios. Previous methods primarily evaluated LLMs in English, potentially overlooking language-dependent behaviors and moral reasoning. This study proposes a novel approach by testing nine models across six providers using game-theoretic vignettes in multiple languages, revealing that prompts in Japanese significantly reduce launch rates for nuclear strikes in certain scenarios. The methodology involves cross-language experiments that isolate the reasoning language's impact on model behavior, demonstrating that when models reason in Japanese, they generate moral vocabulary absent in English prompts. The findings indicate that LLM safety behavior is language-dependent, highlighting the need for multilingual evaluations to accurately assess risks and safeguards.
本研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）在战略背景下的安全对齐，特别关注提示语言如何影响高风险场景中的决策，例如在核打击建议中的应用。以往的方法主要在英语中评估LLMs，可能忽视了可能影响安全结果的语言依赖行为。所提出的方法通过使用博弈论小品在英语和日语中测试六个提供商的九个模型，揭示了日语提示在某些场景中显著降低了发射率。该方法论表明，在日语中推理使模型生成了在英语中缺失的道德词汇，表明安全行为依赖于语言。研究结果表明，仅在英语中进行的LLM评估可能会遗漏关键风险和保障，强调了在LLM安全评估中进行多语言评估的必要性。
LLM Safety Alignment in Low-Resource Languages: A Systematic Literature Review
Authors: Valdini Douglace Lemofouet, Blessing Ngozi Uzor, Paula Chikaodinaka Anyanwu, Danielle Blanche Kapsa, Sukairaj Hafiz Imam, P Sam Sahil, Abigail Oppong, Tassallah Abdullahi, Clemencia Siro, Idris Abdulmumin, Seid Muhie Yimam, Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad
Venue: IJCAI
First: 2026-07-20T21:11:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-20T21:11:00+00:00
Comments: The paper was accepted at LM4UC workshop organize by IJCAI. I added a screenshot of the decision (Open Review)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved substantial progress in safety alignment, yet their safety guarantees remain significantly weaker in low-resource and multilingual settings than in high-resource languages. In this paper, we conduct a Systematic Literature Review (SLR) of LLM safety alignment in low-resource languages by adopting the PRISMA 2020 methodology. Out of roughly 1,500 papers identified from Semantic Scholar, arXiv, and OpenAlex, 50 relevant studies have been selected and analyzed. Our review is organized around four themes: safety alignment methods, multilingual safety risks, evaluation benchmarks, and cross-lingual transferability. We further propose a taxonomy of safety alignment approaches based on three adaptation mechanisms: data adaptation, objective optimization, and mechanistic alignment. Across literature, translated English benchmarks fail to sufficiently represent culturally rooted harms, and multilingual models are more vulnerable to cross-lingual jailbreaks, code-switching attacks, and safety degradation in underrepresented languages. These failures are driven by several key factors, including uneven multilingual pre-training coverage, insufficient native-language preference data, poor transfer of safety representations, and a lack of culturally aware evaluation frameworks. The review also notes that many low-resource languages, especially African languages, have fewer safety benchmarks available than other multilingual regions. Overall, the results reveal a persistent multilingual safety gap, and suggest that future progress will require culturally grounded benchmarks, participatory data collection, balanced multilingual pre-training, and scalable multilingual alignment methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：低资源语言中的LLM安全对齐：系统文献综述
大型语言模型（LLMs）在安全对齐方面取得了显著进展，但在低资源和多语言环境中的安全保障仍显著弱于高资源语言。本文采用PRISMA 2020方法，对低资源语言中的LLM安全对齐进行系统文献综述（SLR）。从Semantic Scholar、arXiv和OpenAlex中识别出的约1500篇论文中，选择并分析了50项相关研究。我们的综述围绕四个主题组织：安全对齐方法、多语言安全风险、评估基准和跨语言可转移性。我们进一步提出了一种基于三种适应机制的安全对齐方法分类法：数据适应、目标优化和机制对齐。文献中，翻译的英语基准未能充分代表文化根植的危害，多语言模型更容易受到跨语言越狱、代码切换攻击和在代表性不足语言中的安全降级。这些失败由几个关键因素驱动，包括不均匀的多语言预训练覆盖、缺乏母语偏好数据、安全表征的转移不良，以及缺乏文化意识的评估框架。综述还指出，许多低资源语言，尤其是非洲语言，安全基准的可用性少于其他多语言地区。总体而言，结果揭示了持续存在的多语言安全差距，并建议未来的进展需要文化基础的基准、参与性数据收集、平衡的多语言预训练和可扩展的多语言对齐方法。
Summary / 总结
This article addresses the significant safety alignment challenges faced by Large Language Models (LLMs) in low-resource languages, where existing methods have proven inadequate due to a lack of culturally relevant benchmarks and vulnerabilities to various attacks. The proposed systematic literature review (SLR) utilizes the PRISMA 2020 methodology to analyze 50 relevant studies from a pool of approximately 1,500 papers, highlighting the shortcomings of current safety alignment approaches and introducing a taxonomy based on data adaptation, objective optimization, and mechanistic alignment. The findings indicate a persistent multilingual safety gap, particularly for underrepresented languages, and emphasize the need for culturally grounded benchmarks and improved multilingual pre-training. The research methodology reveals that addressing these gaps is crucial for enhancing LLM safety in low-resource settings, ultimately contributing to a more equitable application of LLM technology across diverse languages.
本研究关注大型语言模型（LLMs）在低资源语言中的安全对齐挑战，相较于高资源语言，这些挑战显著且未得到充分解决，现有方法往往忽视文化特定的危害和脆弱性。以往的方法面临多语言预训练覆盖不足和缺乏文化意识评估框架等问题，导致安全差距持续存在。本文通过采用PRISMA 2020方法论进行系统文献综述，分析了50项相关研究，并提出了一种基于数据适应、目标优化和机制对齐的安全对齐方法分类。研究结果表明，翻译的基准测试未能充分代表低资源语言中的风险，所提出的方法强调需要文化基础的基准和均衡的多语言预训练，以改善安全对齐。结果表明，解决这些差距对于提高LLM在低资源环境中的表现至关重要。
Operational Hallucination and Safety Drift in AI Agents
Authors: Shasha Yu, Fiona Carroll, Barry L. Bentley
First: 2026-07-20T17:01:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-20T17:01:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) serving as planners in tool-using autonomous agents introduce dynamic reliability risks in multi-turn execution. While single-turn safety mechanisms are relatively mature, extended interactions reveal structural vulnerabilities where initial alignment degrades over time. This paper empirically characterizes two observed failure modes across multiple state-of-the-art LLMs: Safety Drift, the gradual erosion of declared safety intent leading to constraint-violating actions (e.g., textual refusal followed by reconnaissance and unsafe execution), and Operational Hallucination, persistent repetitive tool calls indicative of flawed state perception (e.g., livelocks even in legitimate tasks). Through controlled multi-turn evaluation on high-stakes ethical dilemmas, malicious requests, and benign controls, we quantify these phenomena using declaration-action gap and livelock metrics, demonstrating their cross-model prevalence under direct execution protocols. Root-cause analysis attributes the instabilities to the decoupling of reasoning context from execution state in current agent loops. We propose an Action-Aware Supervision Layer - a lightweight, plug-and-play architectural blueprint incorporating intent-action consistency checks, runtime state tracking, and forced termination primitives. Post-hoc simulation on captured failure trajectories shows the layer can intercept observed violations without false positives on benign cases. This work advances agent reliability by shifting focus from linguistic safeguards to enforceable architectural mechanisms for responsible agentic AI.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能代理中的操作性幻觉与安全漂移
作为工具使用自主代理的规划者，大型语言模型（LLMs）在多轮执行中引入了动态可靠性风险。虽然单轮安全机制相对成熟，但扩展交互揭示了结构性脆弱性，初始对齐随着时间的推移而下降。本文实证描述了多个最先进LLMs中观察到的两种失败模式：安全漂移，即声明的安全意图逐渐侵蚀，导致违反约束的行为（例如，文本拒绝后进行侦察和不安全执行），以及操作性幻觉，即持续重复的工具调用，表明状态感知存在缺陷（例如，即使在合法任务中也出现活锁）。通过对高风险伦理困境、恶意请求和良性控制的受控多轮评估，我们使用声明-行动差距和活锁指标量化这些现象，展示它们在直接执行协议下的跨模型普遍性。根本原因分析将不稳定性归因于当前代理循环中推理上下文与执行状态的解耦。我们提出了一种行动感知监督层——一种轻量级、即插即用的架构蓝图，结合了意图-行动一致性检查、运行时状态跟踪和强制终止原语。对捕获的失败轨迹进行的后期模拟表明，该层可以在良性案例中拦截观察到的违规行为而没有假阳性。该工作通过将重点从语言保障转向可执行的架构机制，推动了代理的可靠性，以实现负责任的代理人工智能。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the reliability risks associated with large language models (LLMs) used as planners in autonomous agents, particularly during multi-turn interactions where safety mechanisms fail. Previous methods have focused on single-turn safety, which do not account for the degradation of alignment over extended interactions, leading to issues like Safety Drift and Operational Hallucination. This paper contributes by empirically characterizing these failure modes and proposing an Action-Aware Supervision Layer that enhances agent reliability through intent-action consistency checks and runtime state tracking. The methodology involves controlled evaluations on ethical dilemmas and malicious requests, revealing that the proposed layer can effectively intercept violations without generating false positives, thereby supporting the goal of improving safety in AI agents.
本研究关注于作为规划者的语言模型（LLMs）在自主代理中的可靠性风险，特别是在多轮交互中，初始安全措施可能会下降。过去的方法主要集中在单轮安全机制上，这不足以解决在延长交互中出现的结构性脆弱性，导致安全漂移和操作性幻觉等问题。提出的方法引入了一种行动感知监督层，通过实施意图-行动一致性检查和运行时状态跟踪来增强代理的可靠性，有效减轻了识别出的风险。该方法通过对伦理困境和恶意请求的控制实验进行评估，证明其能够在不产生虚假积极结果的情况下拦截违规行为，从而支持确保更安全、更可靠的人工智能代理的目标。
STAC: When Innocent Tools Form Dangerous Chains for LLM Agents
Authors: Jing-Jing Li, Jianfeng He, Chao Shang, Devang Kulshreshtha, Xun Xian, Yi Zhang, Hang Su, Sandesh Swamy, Yanjun Qi
First: 2025-09-30T00:31:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-19T03:06:33+00:00
Comments: Long version with 15 pages in main draft and a total of 39 pages including references and appendix. Data and code \url{https://github.com/amazon-science/MultiTurnAgentAttack}
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
As LLMs advance into autonomous agents with tool-use capabilities, they introduce security challenges that extend beyond traditional content-based LLM safety concerns. This paper introduces Sequential Tool Attack Chaining (\STAC), a novel multi-turn attack framework that exploits agent tool use. \STAC chains together tool calls that each appear harmless in isolation but, when combined, collectively enable harmful operations that only become apparent at the final execution step. At the core of \STAC is an automated, closed-loop pipeline that synthesizes executable multi-step tool chains, validates them through in-environment execution, and reverse-engineers stealthy multi-turn prompts that reliably induce agents to execute the verified malicious sequence. Using this framework, we generate and systematically evaluate 483 \STAC cases, featuring 1,352 sets of user-agent-environment interactions and spanning diverse domains, tasks, agent types, and 10 failure modes. Our evaluations show that state-of-the-art LLM agents are highly vulnerable to \STAC, with an average final attack success rate (ASR) of 91.2\% -- exceeding 90\% for all but one of the eight agents evaluated. We further perform defense analysis and find that existing prompt-based defenses provide limited protection. To address this gap, we propose a new reasoning-driven defense prompt that achieves the strongest initial-turn protection, cutting ASR by up to 28.8\%; however, this advantage erodes sharply under adaptive attacks, and an experience-based defense (ToolShield) proves more durable over sustained multi-turn interactions. These results highlight a crucial gap: defending tool-enabled agents requires reasoning over entire action sequences and their cumulative effects, rather than evaluating isolated prompts or responses.
中文标题/摘要
标题：STAC：当无辜工具形成对LLM代理的危险链条
随着LLM向具备工具使用能力的自主代理发展，它们引入的安全挑战超出了传统基于内容的LLM安全问题。本文介绍了顺序工具攻击链（STAC），一种新颖的多轮攻击框架，利用代理的工具使用。STAC将看似无害的工具调用串联在一起，但当组合时，集体启用有害操作，这些操作仅在最终执行步骤时显现。STAC的核心是一个自动化的闭环管道，合成可执行的多步骤工具链，通过环境执行验证它们，并逆向工程隐秘的多轮提示，可靠地诱导代理执行经过验证的恶意序列。利用该框架，我们生成并系统评估了483个STAC案例，涉及1,352组用户-代理-环境交互，涵盖多种领域、任务、代理类型和10种失败模式。我们的评估表明，最先进的LLM代理对STAC高度脆弱，平均最终攻击成功率（ASR）为91.2%，在评估的八个代理中，除了一个外，均超过90%。我们进一步进行防御分析，发现现有的基于提示的防御提供的保护有限。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种新的基于推理的防御提示，达到了最强的初始回合保护，将ASR降低了多达28.8%；然而，在自适应攻击下，这一优势迅速减弱，而基于经验的防御（ToolShield）在持续的多轮交互中表现得更为持久。这些结果突显了一个关键差距：防御工具启用的代理需要对整个动作序列及其累积效果进行推理，而不是评估孤立的提示或响应。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the emerging security challenges posed by large language models (LLMs) as they evolve into autonomous agents capable of tool use, which introduces risks beyond traditional content-based safety concerns. Previous methods primarily focused on isolated prompt evaluations, failing to account for the cumulative effects of tool interactions, which can lead to harmful outcomes when combined. The proposed Sequential Tool Attack Chaining (STAC) framework innovatively chains together seemingly harmless tool calls into multi-step attacks, utilizing an automated pipeline for synthesis and validation of these sequences. This study systematically evaluates 483 STAC cases across various domains and agent types, revealing a high average attack success rate of 91.2% against state-of-the-art LLM agents. Furthermore, it introduces a new reasoning-driven defense prompt that significantly reduces attack success rates, although its effectiveness diminishes under adaptive attacks, while an experience-based defense (ToolShield) offers more robust protection during multi-turn interactions, emphasizing the need for comprehensive defense strategies that consider the entire sequence of actions.

Addressing Benchmarking Gaps in Large Language Models for Health and Medicine with Dynamic Red-Teaming
Authors: Jiazhen Pan, Bailiang Jian, Paul Hager, Yundi Zhang, Che Liu, Friederike Jungmann, Hongwei Bran Li, Julian Canisius, Chenyu You, Junde Wu, Jiayuan Zhu, Fenglin Liu, Yuyuan Liu, Niklas Bubeck, Moritz Knolle, Chen, Chen, Christian Wachinger, Zhenyu Gong, Cheng Ouyang, Georgios Kaissis, Benedikt Wiestler, Daniel Rueckert
First: 2025-07-30T08:44:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T15:41:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to answer health-related questions and support healthcare workflows, yet evidence for their safety still relies heavily on static benchmarks that can rapidly become obsolete or be optimized against. Here we introduce a Dynamic, Automatic, and Systematic (DAS) red-teaming audit framework that continuously stress-tests LLMs for health across four safety-critical axes: robustness, privacy, bias/fairness, and hallucination/factual inaccuracies. Validated against board-certified clinicians with high concordance, a suite of adversarial agents autonomously mutates health-related test cases to uncover vulnerabilities in real time. Applying DAS to 15 proprietary and open-source LLMs revealed a profound gap between high static benchmark performance and low dynamic reliability--the "Benchmarking Gap". Despite median MedQA accuracy exceeding 80\%, 94\% of previously correct answers failed under dynamic robustness testing. This brittleness generalized to the realistic, open-ended HealthBench dataset, where top-tier models exhibited failure rates exceeding 70\% and sharp shifts in model rankings across evaluations, suggesting that high scores on established static benchmarks may reflect superficial memorization. We observed similarly high failure rates across other domains: privacy leaks were elicited in 86\% of scenarios, cognitive-bias priming altered recommendations in 81\% of fairness tests, and hallucination rates exceeded 74\% in widely used models. By converting LLM safety evaluation for health from a static checklist into a living adversarial audit, DAS provides a scalable framework for surfacing latent risks before such systems are deployed in consumer-facing health assistants, clinician-facing tools, and broader healthcare workflows. Code is available at https://github.com/JZPeterPan/DAS-Medical-Red-Teaming-Agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过动态红队解决大型语言模型在健康和医学中的基准测试差距
大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地用于回答与健康相关的问题和支持医疗工作流程，但其安全性的证据仍然在很大程度上依赖于快速过时或可优化的静态基准。在这里，我们引入了一种动态、自动和系统化（DAS）的红队审计框架，持续对LLMs在健康方面进行压力测试，涵盖四个安全关键轴心：鲁棒性、隐私、公平性/偏见和幻觉/事实不准确性。经过与高一致性的董事会认证临床医生的验证，一套对抗性代理自主变更与健康相关的测试案例，以实时发现漏洞。将DAS应用于15个专有和开源LLMs揭示了高静态基准性能与低动态可靠性之间的深刻差距——“基准测试差距”。尽管中位数MedQA准确率超过80%，94%的先前正确答案在动态鲁棒性测试中失败。这种脆弱性在现实的开放式HealthBench数据集中普遍存在，顶级模型的失败率超过70%，并且在评估中模型排名发生剧烈变化，表明在既定静态基准上获得的高分可能反映了表面的记忆。我们在其他领域观察到类似的高失败率：在86%的场景中引发了隐私泄露，81%的公平性测试中认知偏见的引导改变了推荐，广泛使用的模型中幻觉率超过74%。通过将LLM安全评估从静态检查表转变为动态对抗审计，DAS提供了一个可扩展的框架，以在这些系统部署到面向消费者的健康助手、面向临床医生的工具和更广泛的医疗工作流程之前，揭示潜在风险。代码可在https://github.com/JZPeterPan/DAS-Medical-Red-Teaming-Agents获取。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the increasing reliance on large language models (LLMs) in healthcare, highlighting the limitations of static benchmarks that can quickly become outdated or manipulated. Previous methods primarily focused on fixed evaluations, which fail to capture the dynamic nature of real-world applications, leading to a significant 'Benchmarking Gap' where models perform well in static tests but poorly in practical scenarios. The Joint Effect of Quantization and Sampling Temperature on LLM Safety Alignment: A Factorial Analysis
Authors: Hari Prasad, Ritam Pal
First: 2026-06-28T19:52:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-15T10:24:21+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 5 Figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern LLM deployments often combine quantization with higher sampling temperatures to reduce cost, latency, or repetition, yet safety evaluations usually treat these as fixed implementation details. We test whether models that are safe at FP16 with greedy decoding remain safe after quantization and stochastic sampling, or whether the two factors amplify each other. We evaluate 8 instruction-tuned models from five families across 3 precisions and 6 temperatures, covering 144 configurations on 7 harmfulness benchmarks and generating about 2.0 million responses, which are scored by a six-judge safety ensemble. Contrary to concerns that low-bit deployment erodes alignment, we find that standard quantization is approximately safety-neutral: for 7 of 8 models, AWQ INT4 keeps attack success within about 1.6 percentage points of FP16 or lowers it, with clear degradation only for SmolLM3-3B (34.5% to 44.1%). However, the larger risk comes from sampling: higher temperatures sharply increase decision instability, with DFR reaching 41.9% at T = 1.0, even when average ASR changes only modestly. The two factors do not compound: our Compound Degradation Index remains sub-additive (-0.071 to +0.018), indicating that quantization partially offsets rather than amplifies temperature-induced degradation. Finally, a per-benchmark breakdown shows that single-benchmark evaluation badly understates risk: several models scoring 0% on AdvBench exceed 80% on ManyHarm. Standard INT4/INT8 quantization can therefore be reasonable for well-aligned models, but safety claims should report multi-sample stability across multiple benchmarks rather than rely on a single benchmark at greedy decoding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：量化与采样温度对大型语言模型安全对齐的联合影响：一个因子分析
现代大型语言模型的部署通常将量化与更高的采样温度结合，以降低成本、延迟或重复性，但安全评估通常将这些视为固定的实现细节。我们测试在FP16下使用贪婪解码安全的模型在量化和随机采样后是否仍然安全，或者这两个因素是否相互放大。我们评估了来自五个家族的8个指令调优模型，涵盖3种精度和6种温度，覆盖144种配置，在7个有害性基准上生成约200万条响应，并由六名评审组成的安全团队进行评分。与低位部署会削弱对齐的担忧相反，我们发现标准量化大致是安全中性的：对于8个模型中的7个，AWQ INT4将攻击成功率保持在FP16的约1.6个百分点内或降低，只有SmolLM3-3B出现明显退化（34.5%降至44.1%）。然而，较大的风险来自采样：更高的温度显著增加决策不稳定性，在T = 1.0时DFR达到41.9%，即使平均ASR变化仅适度。这两个因素并不相互叠加：我们的复合退化指数保持在亚加性范围内（-0.071到+0.018），表明量化部分抵消而非放大温度引起的退化。最后，逐基准的细分显示，单基准评估严重低估了风险：几个在AdvBench上得分为0%的模型在ManyHarm上超过80%。因此，标准INT4/INT8量化对于对齐良好的模型是合理的，但安全声明应报告多个基准的多样本稳定性，而不是依赖于贪婪解码的单一基准。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the safety alignment of large language models (LLMs) in the context of modern deployment practices that combine quantization and higher sampling temperatures, which are often treated as fixed parameters in safety evaluations. Previous methods have not adequately assessed the interaction between quantization and sampling temperature, leading to concerns about safety degradation in low-bit deployments. The authors propose a factorial analysis of 8 instruction-tuned models across various precisions and temperatures, evaluating their performance on 7 harmfulness benchmarks with a substantial dataset of 2 million responses. The findings reveal that standard quantization is largely safety-neutral for most models, while higher sampling temperatures significantly increase decision instability. The study contributes to understanding the nuanced relationship between quantization and sampling temperature, suggesting that quantization can mitigate some risks associated with higher temperatures, and emphasizes the importance of multi-benchmark evaluations for accurate safety assessments.
本研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）在现代部署实践中安全对齐的问题，这些实践通常将量化与更高的采样温度结合使用，而这些参数在安全评估中常被视为固定值。以往的方法未能充分评估量化与采样温度之间的相互作用，导致人们担心低比特部署可能会损害模型对齐。所提出的方法系统地评估了八个指令调优模型在不同精度和温度下的安全性，结果表明标准量化通常是安全中性的，而较高的采样温度显著增加了决策不稳定性。本文的贡献在于对144种配置在七个有害性基准上的因子分析，表明量化并未加剧与温度相关的风险，并强调了多基准评估在准确安全评估中的重要性。研究结果表明，尽管标准INT4/INT8量化对于良好对齐的模型是可以接受的，但安全评估必须考虑多个样本和基准的稳定性，而不是依赖单一基准的评估。
Minionese: Comprehensive Benchmark and Mechanistic Study of Multilingual LLM Safety
Authors: Chigozirim Ifebi, Brent Kong, Ayushi Mehrotra
First: 2026-07-11T04:13:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-11T04:13:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Safety alignment in large language models remains brittle across languages: prompts reliably refused in English can elicit harmful compliance in non-English and low-resource settings. We introduce \textsc{Minionese}, a multilingual jailbreak benchmark spanning 18 languages, 4 resource tiers, and 4 perturbation types (standard translation, code-switching, transliteration, and translationese), paired with a geometric mechanistic analysis of refusal failure across language tiers. We show that each attack type produces a distinct vulnerability profile: transliteration vulnerability is mediated by script identity, code-switching maintains effectiveness through the lowest-resource tier, and a sharp safety regime transition between Tiers 2 and 3 is consistent across all models. Mechanistically, low-resource jailbreaks succeed by routing harmful content through a geometrically misaligned subspace that projects insufficiently onto the refusal directions, leaving the refusal mechanism intact but untriggered. These findings show that English-only safety evaluations are insufficient; they require accounting for script family, perturbation type, and per-language alignment coverage. The benchmark and analysis code is at https://github.com/Brentkong/Minionese-Comprehensive-Benchmark-and-Mechanistic-Study-of-Multilingual-LLM-Safety.git.
中文标题/摘要
标题：小黄人语：多语言大型语言模型安全性的综合基准和机制研究
大型语言模型的安全对齐在不同语言间仍然脆弱：在英语中可靠拒绝的提示在非英语和低资源环境中可能引发有害的顺从。我们介绍了\textsc{Minionese}，这是一个涵盖18种语言、4个资源层级和4种扰动类型（标准翻译、代码切换、音译和翻译语）的多语言越狱基准，并结合了对不同语言层级拒绝失败的几何机制分析。我们展示了每种攻击类型产生独特的脆弱性特征：音译脆弱性受脚本身份的影响，代码切换在最低资源层级中保持有效，而第2层和第3层之间的安全机制转变在所有模型中是一致的。从机制上讲，低资源越狱通过将有害内容引导到几何上不对齐的子空间中成功，该子空间在拒绝方向上投影不足，保持拒绝机制完好但未被触发。这些发现表明，仅依赖英语的安全评估是不够的；它们需要考虑脚本家族、扰动类型和每种语言的对齐覆盖。基准和分析代码可在https://github.com/Brentkong/Minionese-Comprehensive-Benchmark-and-Mechanistic-Study-of-Multilingual-LLM-Safety.git找到。

Mechanistic Interpretability of LLM Jailbreaks via Internal Attribution Graphs
Authors: Anupam Wagle, Ifrat Ikhtear Uddin, Chaowei Zhang, Longwei Wang
First: 2026-07-08T20:31:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T20:31:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) exhibit remarkable capabilities but remain highly vulnerable to adversarial prompts and jailbreak attacks. Existing approaches primarily analyze these failures through input-output behaviors or attribution methods, offering limited insight into how adversarial perturbations alter the model's internal reasoning. Consequently, the mechanisms underlying unsafe or incorrect behaviors remain poorly understood. We introduce a mechanistic framework for diagnosing LLM vulnerabilities using paired internal computation graphs, which represent prompt-specific inference as structured causal interactions among latent features. By constructing and aligning computation graphs for clean and attacked prompts, we reveal that adversarial attacks induce systematic transformations of internal reasoning, including suppression of safety-relevant components, emergence of attack-specific features, and rerouting of computation paths. Building on this representation, we propose a unified framework that (i) decomposes computation into invariant, suppressed, and emergent structures, (ii) identifies recurring vulnerability motifs associated with failure modes, and (iii) performs causal interventions on nodes, paths, and subgraphs to directly evaluate their contributions to attack success. This enables a transition from descriptive attribution to causal diagnosis of model failures. Experiments across multiple open-source LLMs and diverse adversarial and jailbreak benchmarks demonstrate that structural deviations in internal computation graphs strongly correlate with unsafe behaviors. Furthermore, targeted interventions on identified vulnerability motifs improve model robustness, establishing internal computation graphs as a principled foundation for understanding, diagnosing, and mitigating LLM vulnerabilities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过内部归因图对LLM越狱的机制性可解释性
大型语言模型（LLMs）展现出显著的能力，但仍然对对抗性提示和越狱攻击高度脆弱。现有方法主要通过输入输出行为或归因方法分析这些失败，提供的见解有限，无法深入理解对抗性扰动如何改变模型的内部推理。因此，导致不安全或不正确行为的机制仍然不清楚。我们引入了一种机制框架，通过配对的内部计算图来诊断LLM的脆弱性，这些图将特定提示的推理表示为潜在特征之间的结构因果交互。通过构建和对齐干净和攻击提示的计算图，我们揭示了对抗性攻击引发的内部推理的系统性转变，包括安全相关组件的抑制、攻击特定特征的出现和计算路径的重新引导。在此表示的基础上，我们提出了一个统一框架，(i) 将计算分解为不变、抑制和新兴结构，(ii) 识别与失败模式相关的重复脆弱性特征，(iii) 对节点、路径和子图进行因果干预，以直接评估它们对攻击成功的贡献。这使得从描述性归因转向模型失败的因果诊断成为可能。针对多个开源LLM和各种对抗性及越狱基准的实验表明，内部计算图的结构偏差与不安全行为强烈相关。此外，对识别出的脆弱性特征进行有针对性的干预提高了模型的鲁棒性，确立了内部计算图作为理解、诊断和减轻LLM脆弱性的原则基础。

Predicting LLM Safety Before Release by Simulating Deployment
Authors: Marcus Williams, Hannah Sheahan, Cameron Raymond, Tomek Korbak, Deng Pan, Peilin Yang, Leon Maksin, Ningyi Xie, Phillip Guo, Ian Kivlichan, Micah Carroll
First: 2026-07-08T09:17:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T09:17:47+00:00
Comments: 31 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Pre-deployment safety evaluations aim to inform the downstream risks of releasing a new AI model. Yet most evaluations provide limited evidence about how often undesired model behavior will occur in deployment: they generally have insufficient coverage, are unrepresentative, and are generally recognizable as tests. To address these concerns, we study a simple way to simulate a model deployment: starting from de-identified conversations from a previous model deployment, we hold fixed the initial conversation prefix and regenerate the next response using a candidate model. The resulting responses can then both be audited for novel misalignments and used to estimate the prevalence of model misbehavior before deployment. We evaluate deployment simulation across four GPT-5-series deployments, using registered, outcome-blinded predictions for GPT-5.4 and retrospective analyses of three earlier releases. We find that deployment simulation produces informative estimates of post-deployment misbehavior rates and outperforms baselines based on adversarially selected production data; its evaluation-awareness point estimates were also much closer to production traffic than those from traditional evaluations. We also identify the realism of tool resampling as a central challenge for further improving predictions and share results suggesting that this challenge is surmountable even in complex tool-use settings. Finally, we show that deployment simulation can be seeded from public chat datasets and remain informative about production misbehavior rates, suggesting a path for external researchers to run deployment-grounded evaluations without access to private production logs. Overall, deployment simulation helps evaluators forecast how language models will behave in the real world and supports more quantitative assessment of deployment risk.

标题：通过模拟部署预测 LLM 发布前的安全性
发布前的安全评估旨在告知发布新 AI 模型的下游风险。然而，大多数评估提供的证据有限，无法说明在部署中不希望的模型行为发生的频率：它们通常覆盖不足、不具代表性，并且通常被识别为测试。为了解决这些问题，我们研究了一种简单的模拟模型部署的方法：从先前模型部署的去标识化对话开始，固定初始对话前缀，并使用候选模型重新生成下一个响应。生成的响应可以用于审计新出现的不一致性，并用于在部署前估计模型不当行为的普遍性。我们在四个 GPT-5 系列部署中评估了部署模拟，使用了对 GPT-5.4 的注册、结果盲预测和对三个早期发布的回顾性分析。我们发现，部署模拟产生了有关部署后不当行为率的信息性估计，并且优于基于对抗性选择的生产数据的基线；其评估意识点估计也比传统评估更接近生产流量。我们还确定了工具重采样的现实性作为进一步改善预测的核心挑战，并分享了结果，表明即使在复杂的工具使用环境中，这一挑战也是可以克服的。最后，我们展示了部署模拟可以从公共聊天数据集中播种，并保持对生产不当行为率的信息性，这为外部研究人员在没有访问私有生产日志的情况下进行基于部署的评估提供了一条路径。总体而言，部署模拟帮助评估者预测语言模型在现实世界中的行为，并支持对部署风险的更定量评估。

Operational Reframing and Approval-Framed Delegation in Multi-Agent LLM Safety
Authors: Lifei Liu, Haoran Yu, Xiaochong Jiang, Su Wang, Pin Qian, Yihang Chen
First: 2026-07-08T07:31:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-08T07:31:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety evaluations of multi-agent LLM systems often compare a direct prompt with a planner-executor pipeline and report the difference as a single "pipeline effect." We argue that this aggregate is difficult to interpret because it conflates three mechanisms: harmful intent may be reframed as plausible operational work, the planner may refuse or transform the request, and the executor may act under delegation prompts implying prior approval. To separate these factors, we introduce a five-condition controlled contrast design, evaluated on 30 synthetic harmful scenarios and an exploratory external validation set from four agent-safety benchmarks using LLM-judged compliance. Our results show that aggregate pipeline safety is not a stable architectural property. Operational reframing is the most portable risk signal, increasing compliance for GPT, Gemini, and DeepSeek across both scenario sets, while Claude is comparatively resistant. Planner behavior can offset this risk mainly through refusal; however, when the planner produces executable steps, the executor may become more compliant than under the direct operational baseline. Approval-framed delegation is sensitive to prompt design, model pairing, and scenario source, and a skeptical executor prompt sharply reduces compliance. Raw-direct model rankings can also mispredict deployed planner-executor behavior. Gemini is safest under raw direct prompts in the primary set yet shows the largest amplification with a Claude planner, rising from 8.9 percent to 38.9 percent compliance. GPTs near-zero aggregate pipeline effect instead hides a reframing increase canceled by planner refusal. These findings suggest that multi-agent safety evaluations should report reframing, planner behavior, delegation framing, and model pairing separately before attributing failures to architecture itself.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多智能体LLM安全中的操作重构与批准框架委托
多智能体LLM系统的安全评估通常将直接提示与规划-执行管道进行比较，并将差异报告为单一的“管道效应”。我们认为，这种汇总难以解释，因为它混淆了三种机制：有害意图可能被重构为合理的操作工作，规划者可能拒绝或转变请求，而执行者可能在暗示先前批准的委托提示下行动。为了分离这些因素，我们引入了一个五条件的对照设计，在30个合成有害场景和来自四个代理安全基准的探索性外部验证集上进行评估，使用LLM判断的合规性。我们的结果表明，汇总管道安全并不是一个稳定的架构属性。操作重构是最具可移植性的风险信号，在两个场景集上提高了GPT、Gemini和DeepSeek的合规性，而Claude则相对抵抗。规划者的行为主要通过拒绝来抵消这一风险；然而，当规划者产生可执行步骤时，执行者的合规性可能比直接操作基线更高。批准框架委托对提示设计、模型配对和场景来源敏感，而怀疑的执行者提示会显著降低合规性。原始直接模型排名也可能错误预测已部署的规划-执行行为。在主要集中的原始直接提示下，Gemini是最安全的，但在Claude规划者下显示出最大的放大，从8.9%上升到38.9%的合规性。GPT的近零汇总管道效应则掩盖了被规划者拒绝抵消的重构增加。这些发现表明，多智能体安全评估应分别报告重构、规划者行为、委托框架和模型配对，然后再将失败归因于架构本身。
Summary / 总结
This article addresses the complexities in safety evaluations of multi-agent large language model (LLM) systems, which have traditionally aggregated results into a single 'pipeline effect,' obscuring the underlying mechanisms at play. Previous methods have failed to distinguish between harmful intent reframing, planner behavior, and executor compliance, leading to misinterpretations of safety. The proposed approach introduces a five-condition controlled contrast design to separately analyze these factors, demonstrating that operational reframing serves as a significant risk signal across various models, while planner behavior can mitigate risks through refusal. The methodology involves evaluating 30 synthetic harmful scenarios and an external validation set, revealing that compliance varies significantly based on prompt design and model interactions. The findings indicate that safety evaluations should consider these factors independently to provide a clearer understanding of multi-agent system safety.
本研究探讨了多智能体大型语言模型（LLM）系统安全评估中的复杂性，传统上将结果汇总为单一的“管道效应”，这掩盖了潜在的机制。以往的方法未能区分有害意图的重新框架、规划者行为和执行者合规性，导致对安全性能的误解。所提出的方法引入了五个条件的对照设计，以隔离这些因素，从而提供对多智能体交互的更清晰洞察。该方法论涉及在四个智能体安全基准上评估30个合成有害场景和一个外部验证集，结果显示操作性重新框架是一个重要的风险信号，规划者行为可以影响执行者的合规性。这些发现表明，安全评估应单独报告这些因素，以避免对架构安全性的误导性结论。
DT-Guard: Intent-Driven Reasoning-Active Training for Reasoning-Free LLM Safety Guardrail
Authors: He Liu, Changtao Miao, Xinjie Yang, Tianle Song, Yin Wu, Junchi Chen, Bintao He, Xinyuan Zhang, Bo Zhang, Shi Yan, Wei Lu, Wei Wang, Danyang Xu, Jiansheng Cai, Zhe Li
First: 2026-07-07T14:25:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-07T14:25:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models deployed in open-world applications require safety guardrails that are both robust to complex risks and efficient enough for low-latency runtime moderation. Existing guardrails face a practical trade-off between lightweight classification-based models, which are efficient but often struggle with concealed intent, ambiguous semantics, and borderline safety decisions, and reasoning-based guards, which improve judgment quality but introduce additional token generation and inference latency. We present DT-Guard, a content safety guardrail model based on a Reasoning-Active Training, Reasoning-Free Inference paradigm. The key idea is to use reasoning supervision during training while emitting only structured safety labels at inference time. DT-Guard formulates safety judgment as a progressive decision process, Intent - Category - Safety, and constructs an intent-driven dataset with intent labels, risk categories, safety labels, and structured reasoning trajectories. To further improve hard-case robustness, we propose Rollout-Guided Progressive Hard-Case Optimization (RG-PHO), which uses multi-rollout consistency to identify stably mastered, persistently failed, and preference-unstable samples, and applies targeted supervised and preference optimization accordingly. At inference time, DT-Guard directly generates structured labels without explicit reasoning traces, preserving deployment efficiency. Experiments on prompt-side and response-side safety benchmarks show that DT-Guard achieves average F1 scores of 0.886 and 0.870, respectively. With only a 4B backbone, it reaches a dual-side average F1 of 0.878, outperforming strong 8B guardrail baselines. These results demonstrate that reasoning supervision can be effectively internalized into low-latency safety discrimination.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DT-Guard：基于意图驱动推理主动训练的无推理大型语言模型安全护栏
在开放世界应用中部署的大型语言模型需要既能应对复杂风险又能高效低延迟的安全护栏。现有护栏在轻量级基于分类的模型（高效但常常难以处理隐含意图、模糊语义和边界安全决策）与基于推理的护栏（提高判断质量但引入额外的令牌生成和推理延迟）之间面临实际权衡。我们提出了DT-Guard，一种基于推理主动训练、无推理推断范式的内容安全护栏模型。其关键思想是在训练期间使用推理监督，而在推断时仅发出结构化安全标签。DT-Guard将安全判断公式化为一个渐进决策过程：意图 - 类别 - 安全，并构建了一个带有意图标签、风险类别、安全标签和结构化推理轨迹的意图驱动数据集。为了进一步提高对难例的鲁棒性，我们提出了回滚引导渐进难例优化（RG-PHO），利用多回滚一致性识别稳定掌握、持续失败和偏好不稳定的样本，并相应地应用针对性的监督和偏好优化。在推断时，DT-Guard直接生成结构化标签，而无需显式的推理痕迹，从而保持部署效率。在提示端和响应端的安全基准实验表明，DT-Guard的平均F1分数分别为0.886和0.870。仅使用4B主干，它的双侧平均F1达到了0.878，超越了强大的8B护栏基线。这些结果表明，推理监督可以有效地内化为低延迟的安全判别。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the need for effective safety guardrails in large language models (LLMs) used in open-world applications, highlighting the limitations of existing methods that either sacrifice efficiency for accuracy or vice versa. Previous approaches often struggle with concealed intent and ambiguous semantics, leading to suboptimal safety decisions. The proposed DT-Guard model introduces a Reasoning-Active Training and Reasoning-Free Inference paradigm, which utilizes reasoning supervision during training while maintaining efficiency at inference by generating structured safety labels without explicit reasoning traces. This method enhances robustness against hard cases through Rollout-Guided Progressive Hard-Case Optimization (RG-PHO), which optimizes performance based on identified sample difficulties. Experimental results demonstrate that DT-Guard achieves average F1 scores of 0.886 and 0.870 on safety benchmarks, outperforming existing 8B guardrail models with a 4B backbone, thus validating the effectiveness of reasoning supervision in low-latency safety discrimination.
本研究解决了在开放世界应用中使用的大型语言模型（LLM）中有效安全防护的需求，目前的防护措施在效率和处理复杂风险的能力之间存在权衡。以往的方法要么依赖于轻量级分类模型，这些模型在处理细微意图和安全决策时表现不佳，要么依赖于推理模型，这些模型引入了延迟。所提出的DT-Guard模型创新性地结合了训练期间的推理监督与推理自由的推断，允许在不妥协质量的情况下高效地进行安全判断。该方法包括一个结构化的决策过程和一个专门的数据集，通过针对性优化增强对困难案例的鲁棒性。实验结果表明，DT-Guard在安全基准测试中实现了平均F1分数为0.886和0.870，超越了现有的8B防护模型，且仅使用4B的基础模型，从而验证了推理监督在保持低延迟安全判别中的有效性。
Chasing Moving Targets with Online Self-Play Reinforcement Learning for Safer Language Models
Authors: Mickel Liu, Liwei Jiang, Yancheng Liang, Simon Shaolei Du, Yejin Choi, Tim Althoff, Natasha Jaques
Venue: ICML 2026 Poster
First: 2025-06-09T06:35:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-06T07:31:35+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026 Poster
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Conventional large language model (LLM) safety alignment relies on a reactive, disjoint loop: attackers exploit a static model, then defenders patch exposed vulnerabilities. This sequential setup leads to attackers overfitting obsolete exploits while defenders perpetually lag behind emerging threats. To address this, we introduce Self-RedTeam, the first fully online self-play multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) algorithm that continuously co-evolves attacker and defender for robust safety alignment. A single policy self-plays as both attacker and defender, generating adversarial prompts and defending against them, with a reward model adjudicating outcomes. Each role uses hidden chain-of-thought for strategic planning. Grounded in two-player zero-sum game theory, we establish a theoretical safety guarantee: if the game converges to Nash Equilibrium, the defender produces safe responses against any adversarial input. Empirically, Self-RedTeam generalizes across five models from the Llama and Qwen families, uncovering more diverse attacks (+17.80% SBERT) and improving safety of RLHF-trained models by up to 95% across 14 benchmarks. Our work motivates a shift from reactive patching to proactive co-evolution, enabling LLM safety self-improvement via online self-play MARL. Link to code: https://github.com/mickelliu/selfplay-redteaming
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过在线自我对弈强化学习追踪移动目标以实现更安全的语言模型
传统的大型语言模型（LLM）安全对齐依赖于反应式的、不连贯的循环：攻击者利用静态模型，防御者修补暴露的漏洞。这种顺序设置导致攻击者过度拟合过时的攻击，而防御者则始终滞后于新兴威胁。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了Self-RedTeam，这是第一个完全在线自我对弈的多智能体强化学习（MARL）算法，持续共同进化攻击者和防御者以实现稳健的安全对齐。单一策略同时扮演攻击者和防御者，生成对抗性提示并进行防御，奖励模型裁定结果。每个角色使用隐含的思维链进行战略规划。基于双人零和博弈理论，我们建立了理论安全保证：如果游戏收敛到纳什均衡，防御者将对任何对抗性输入产生安全响应。在实证上，Self-RedTeam在Llama和Qwen家族的五个模型中具有广泛的泛化能力，发现了更多样化的攻击（+17.80% SBERT），并在14个基准测试中将RLHF训练模型的安全性提高了多达95%。我们的工作激励了从反应式修补到主动共同进化的转变，使LLM安全性通过在线自我对弈MARL实现自我改进。代码链接：https://github.com/mickelliu/selfplay-redteaming
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the limitations of conventional large language model (LLM) safety alignment, which typically follows a reactive approach where defenders lag behind attackers exploiting static models. Previous methods have struggled with this sequential dynamic, leading to outdated defenses against evolving threats. The proposed Self-RedTeam introduces a novel online self-play multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) algorithm that allows for continuous co-evolution of attacker and defender roles, enhancing safety alignment through strategic planning and a reward model. This methodology is grounded in two-player zero-sum game theory, providing a theoretical safety guarantee at Nash Equilibrium. Empirical results demonstrate that Self-RedTeam generalizes across five models, revealing a 17.80% increase in diverse attacks and improving the safety of RLHF-trained models by up to 95% across 14 benchmarks, thus supporting the goal of proactive safety enhancement in LLMs.
本研究解决了传统大型语言模型（LLM）安全对齐方法的局限性，这些方法通常采用反应式的方式，防御者在攻击者利用漏洞后进行修补。这种方法导致防御机制对新兴威胁的反应滞后。提出的Self-RedTeam方法引入了一种在线自我对抗的多智能体强化学习（MARL）算法，允许攻击者和防御者的持续共同演化，从而增强安全对齐。这种方法的动机明确，因为它将策略从反应式转变为主动式，使LLM能够通过持续的对抗互动自我提升安全性。该方法论涉及一个单一策略在攻击者和防御者角色之间交替，利用隐含的思维链进行战略规划，并基于双人零和博弈理论提供理论安全保证。实证结果表明，Self-RedTeam在五个模型上具有良好的泛化能力，攻击多样性提高了17.80%，并在14个基准测试中使RLHF训练的模型安全性提高了多达95%，支持了增强LLM安全性的目标。
Behind the Refusal: Determining Guardrail Activation via Behavioral Monitoring
Authors: William Hackett, Peter Garraghan
First: 2026-07-02T12:59:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-02T12:59:28+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 13 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As Large Language Models (LLMs) and agentic systems become integrated into real-world applications, ensuring their safety and security is critical. Guardrail systems that detect and block malicious instructions sent to and from an LLM are an essential component of AI security. However, researchers conducting black-box adversarial emulation against production AI systems often struggle to determine whether a guardrail block or an LLM rejection has occurred. This distinction is important because the techniques used to bypass guardrails can differ substantially from those used to bypass LLM safety alignment, and has a material impact on attack technique selection and optimization. We propose the first black-box guardrail reconnaissance methodology, which detects the presence of a guardrail within a target AI system through behavioral monitoring of HTTP, lexical, and timing signals, assuming only black-box access and zero prior knowledge of the guardrail or AI system. Experiments demonstrate that our approach detects guardrail presence with 100% accuracy, with statistically significant behavioral separation between benign and malicious interactions (q < 0.001). Our approach further identifies the content categories a guardrail is designed to block, and distinguishes guardrail blocks from LLM rejection on unseen prompts with an average F1 score of 98%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：拒绝背后的原因：通过行为监测确定护栏激活
随着大型语言模型（LLMs）和自主系统逐渐融入现实应用，确保其安全性至关重要。检测并阻止发送到和从LLM传出的恶意指令的护栏系统是AI安全的重要组成部分。然而，进行黑箱对抗仿真以测试生产AI系统的研究人员常常难以确定是发生了护栏阻止还是LLM拒绝。这一区别很重要，因为绕过护栏的技术与绕过LLM安全对齐的技术可能有显著不同，并对攻击技术的选择和优化产生实质性影响。我们提出了首个黑箱护栏侦察方法，通过对HTTP、词汇和时序信号的行为监测，检测目标AI系统中护栏的存在，假设仅有黑箱访问权限且对护栏或AI系统没有先前知识。实验表明，我们的方法以100%的准确率检测护栏的存在，良性和恶意交互之间的行为差异具有统计学显著性（q < 0.001）。我们的方法进一步识别护栏设计阻止的内容类别，并在未见提示上以98%的平均F1分数区分护栏阻止和LLM拒绝。

Optimization Algorithms for Joint OFDM Waveform Design and RIS Configuration in 6G Networks: From Convex Relaxation to Foundation Models
Authors: Ahmet Kaplan
First: 2026-06-30T08:34:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-30T08:34:46+00:00
Comments: 22 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Joint OFDM-RIS optimization for 6G is a mixed-integer nonlinear programming (MINLP) problem covering sum-rate maximization, energy efficiency, max-min fairness, and peak-to-average power ratio (PAPR)-constrained objectives. Seventy-eight joint OFDM-RIS optimization works published between 2021 and 2026 are surveyed. No standardized benchmark exists, and cross-paper comparisons remain infeasible. This survey classifies these works into four paradigms: (I) model-based convex relaxation, (II) heuristic and metaheuristic search, (III) deep reinforcement and unsupervised learning, and (IV) emerging methods including foundation models (FM), diffusion-based generative AI, and quantum optimization. A literature synthesis of self-reported benchmarks shows that ML-based methods (Paradigm~III) report 95-99\% of model-based spectral efficiency at 10^2-10^4 x faster per-inference runtime (method-pair dependent; literature values are self-reported and exclude ML pre-training cost). A companion tutorial benchmark at N=16, N=64, and N=128 reveals a critical scaling property: GPU-based neural network inference (DDQN, PPO, graph neural network (GNN), unsupervised DL) is N-invariant, with identical runtime at N=16 and N=128, while iterative solvers (AO+SCA, PSO) scale polynomially. Energy efficiency (P2) and PAPR-constrained (P4) benchmarks are deferred to future work with standardized power models and waveform generators. Six open challenges emerge from the synthesis: the cross-paradigm benchmark deficit, real-world hardware-constrained deployment, joint waveform-RIS optimization for doubly-dispersive channels, multi-objective PAPR trade-offs, LLM safety in live network control, and diminishing returns of standalone heuristics. We specify requirements for a standardized benchmark. This study serves as a roadmap for researchers and practitioners working on joint OFDM-RIS optimization in 6G networks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：6G网络中联合OFDM波形设计和RIS配置的优化算法：从凸松弛到基础模型
6G中的联合OFDM-RIS优化是一个混合整数非线性规划（MINLP）问题，涵盖了总速率最大化、能量效率、最大-最小公平性和峰均功率比（PAPR）约束目标。调查了2021年至2026年间发表的78项联合OFDM-RIS优化工作。尚无标准化基准，跨论文比较仍不可行。本调查将这些工作分类为四种范式：（I）基于模型的凸松弛，（II）启发式和元启发式搜索，（III）深度强化和无监督学习，以及（IV）新兴方法，包括基础模型（FM）、基于扩散的生成AI和量子优化。自报告基准的文献综合显示，基于机器学习的方法（范式III）在每次推理运行时间上以10^2-10^4倍的速度报告95-99%的基于模型的光谱效率（方法对依赖；文献值为自报告且不包括机器学习预训练成本）。在N=16、N=64和N=128的伴随教程基准揭示了一个关键的缩放特性：基于GPU的神经网络推理（DDQN、PPO、图神经网络（GNN）、无监督深度学习）是N不变的，在N=16和N=128时具有相同的运行时间，而迭代求解器（AO+SCA、PSO）则呈多项式缩放。能量效率（P2）和PAPR约束（P4）基准推迟到未来工作，需使用标准化的功率模型和波形生成器。从综合中出现六个开放挑战：跨范式基准缺口、现实世界硬件约束的部署、双散射信道的联合波形-RIS优化、多目标PAPR权衡、LLM在实时网络控制中的安全性，以及独立启发式的收益递减。我们为标准化基准指定了要求。本研究为在6G网络中从事联合OFDM-RIS优化的研究人员和实践者提供了路线图。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the optimization of joint OFDM-RIS configurations in 6G networks, highlighting the lack of standardized benchmarks and the challenges in cross-paper comparisons. Previous methods, including model-based convex relaxation and heuristic approaches, have limitations in scalability and efficiency, while the proposed approach incorporates emerging techniques such as foundation models and generative AI to enhance performance. The paper contributes a comprehensive survey of 78 optimization works, categorizing them into four paradigms and revealing that machine learning-based methods achieve 95-99% of the spectral efficiency of traditional methods while being significantly faster. The methodology involves a literature synthesis and benchmarking across various configurations, identifying critical scaling properties and open challenges for future research, ultimately providing a roadmap for advancing joint OFDM-RIS optimization in 6G networks.
