Robust Harmful Features Under Jailbreak Attacks: Mechanistic Evidence from Attention Head Specialization in Large Language Models
Authors: Yanchen Yin, Dongqi Han, Linghui Li
Venue: ICML 2026 Oral presentation
First: 2026-06-26T14:51:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-29T15:00:34+00:00
Comments: 33 pages, 19 figures. Accepted at ICML 2026 as an Oral presentation
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Jailbreak attacks bypass LLM safety alignment, yet their mechanisms remain poorly understood. We provide evidence that attacks do not comprehensively eliminate safety features, but instead selectively suppress specific attention heads. We identify two functionally differentiated types: Adversarially Compromised Heads (ACHs) concentrated in early layers, which are suppressed under attacks, and Safety-Aligned Heads (SAHs) in mid-layers, which maintain robust activations even when attacks succeed. Ablation studies support the causal role of ACHs and the contribution of SAHs to robust activations: suppressing a small number of ACHs is sufficient to induce jailbreak-like behavior on normally refused inputs, while removing SAHs substantially weakens mid-layer safety activations. Token-level attribution further shows that ACH suppression is driven specifically by attack-template tokens, providing a mechanistic account of why attacks can bypass refusal decisions through ACH suppression while leaving internal safety signals sustained by SAHs -- a phenomenon we term Robust Harmful Features. To validate the practical significance of this robustness, we show that simply reading these persistent activations -- without any training -- yields competitive aggregate detection performance with strong adversarial robustness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：监狱攻击下的稳健有害特征：来自大型语言模型注意力头专业化的机制证据
监狱攻击绕过了大型语言模型的安全对齐，但其机制仍然不甚了解。我们提供证据表明，攻击并未全面消除安全特征，而是选择性地抑制特定的注意力头。我们识别出两种功能上不同的类型：集中在早期层的对抗性妥协头（ACHs），在攻击下被抑制；以及在中层的安全对齐头（SAHs），即使在攻击成功时也能保持稳健的激活。消融研究支持ACHs的因果作用以及SAHs对稳健激活的贡献：抑制少量ACHs足以在通常拒绝的输入上诱发类似监狱攻击的行为，而移除SAHs则显著削弱中层的安全激活。标记级归因进一步表明，ACH抑制是由攻击模板标记特定驱动的，提供了攻击如何通过ACH抑制绕过拒绝决策而保持SAHs支持的内部安全信号的机制性解释——我们称之为稳健有害特征。为了验证这种稳健性的实际意义，我们展示了仅仅读取这些持续的激活——无需任何训练——就能在强对抗稳健性下获得竞争性的整体检测性能。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenge of jailbreak attacks on large language models (LLMs), which undermine safety alignment by selectively suppressing certain attention heads rather than eliminating safety features entirely. Previous methods have not adequately explained the mechanisms behind these attacks, leading to a lack of understanding of how safety features can be compromised. This paper proposes a novel approach that identifies two types of attention heads: Adversarially Compromised Heads (ACHs), which are suppressed during attacks, and Safety-Aligned Heads (SAHs), which maintain their activations. The methodology includes ablation studies that demonstrate the critical role of ACHs and the protective function of SAHs, revealing that suppressing ACHs can induce jailbreak behavior while removing SAHs weakens safety activations. The findings indicate that simply monitoring these persistent activations can achieve competitive detection performance against jailbreak attacks, thus supporting the goal of enhancing LLM safety.
本研究探讨了针对大型语言模型（LLMs）的越狱攻击所带来的挑战，这些攻击通过选择性抑制某些注意力头而非完全消除安全特征，从而破坏了安全对齐。以往的方法未能充分解释这些攻击背后的机制，导致对安全特征如何被破坏的理解不足。所提出的方法识别了两种类型的注意力头：在攻击中被抑制的对抗性受损头（ACHs）和保持活跃的安全对齐头（SAHs），从而提供了对LLMs脆弱性的更清晰的机制理解。该研究采用消融研究和标记级归因，证明抑制ACHs可以诱发有害行为，而SAHs的存在有助于维持安全性。研究结果表明，仅监测SAHs的持续激活可以在对抗越狱攻击中获得竞争性的检测性能，支持增强LLM安全性的目标，而无需额外训练。
CASE-Bench: Context-Aware SafEty Benchmark for Large Language Models
Authors: Guangzhi Sun, Xiao Zhan, Shutong Feng, Philip C. Woodland, Jose Such
Venue: ICML 2025
First: 2025-01-24T21:55:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-28T22:17:10+00:00
Comments: 24 pages. This paper has been accepted at ICML 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Aligning large language models (LLMs) with human values is essential for their safe deployment and widespread adoption. Current LLM safety benchmarks often focus solely on the refusal of individual problematic queries, which overlooks the importance of the context where the query occurs and may cause undesired refusal of queries under safe contexts that diminish user experience. Addressing this gap, we introduce CASE-Bench, a Context-Aware SafEty Benchmark that integrates context into safety assessments of LLMs. CASE-Bench assigns distinct, formally described contexts to categorized queries based on Contextual Integrity theory. Additionally, in contrast to previous studies which mainly rely on majority voting from just a few annotators, we recruited a sufficient number of annotators necessary to ensure the detection of statistically significant differences among the experimental conditions based on power analysis. Our extensive analysis using CASE-Bench on various open-source and commercial LLMs reveals a substantial and significant influence of context on human judgments (p<0.0001 from a z-test), underscoring the necessity of context in safety evaluations. We also identify notable mismatches between human judgments and LLM responses, particularly in commercial models within safe contexts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CASE-Bench：面向上下文的安全基准测试用于大型语言模型
将大型语言模型（LLMs）与人类价值观对齐对于其安全部署和广泛采用至关重要。目前的LLM安全基准测试通常仅关注拒绝个别有问题的查询，这忽视了查询发生的上下文的重要性，可能导致在安全上下文中不必要地拒绝查询，从而降低用户体验。为了解决这一问题，我们引入了CASE-Bench，一个将上下文整合到LLM安全评估中的面向上下文的安全基准测试。CASE-Bench根据上下文完整性理论为分类查询分配独特的、正式描述的上下文。此外，与以往主要依赖少数注释者的多数投票的研究不同，我们招募了足够数量的注释者，以确保根据功效分析检测实验条件之间的统计显著差异。我们对各种开源和商业LLM使用CASE-Bench进行的广泛分析揭示了上下文对人类判断的显著影响（p<0.0001，来自z检验），强调了上下文在安全评估中的必要性。我们还发现人类判断与LLM响应之间存在显著不匹配，特别是在安全上下文中的商业模型。

SCARCE: Scalable Cascade Analysis for Rare-event Characterisation via Embeddings
Authors: Yingjie Wang, Yi Dong, Edmund Lau, Jie Meng, Taylor T Johnson, Xiaowei Huang
First: 2026-06-28T21:59:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-28T21:59:31+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 11 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Rare events govern the safety profile of modern AI systems, yet their probabilities are extremely difficult to estimate: direct Monte Carlo requires prohibitive sample budgets. Subset Simulation (SS) addresses this by decomposing a rare-event probability into moderate conditional probabilities over nested intermediate events. However, classical SS requires a handcrafted scalar performance function whose sublevel sets define those events, demanding detailed knowledge of the failure geometry and limiting transfer to new domains. We propose SCARCE (Scalable Cascade Analysis for Rare-event Characterisation via Embeddings), which replaces the performance function with learned latent representations and geometric rulers that score proximity to failure regions. Adaptive thresholding constructs nested intermediate events directly from data. We formalise SCARCE through a non-negative supermartingale, yielding a high-probability upper envelope that remains valid under early stopping. On MNIST misclassification, where dense Monte Carlo provides ground truth, SCARCE achieves approximately 400--500 times lower mean absolute error than grid-searched traditional SS while eliminating systematic over-counting. We then study PAIR-style LLM jailbreaks under a fleet-level threat model with adversarial fraction $η$. On Llama-Guard-3-8B hidden states, a PCA-based ruler attains 2.6% mean relative error for $η\geq 10^{-3}$ against finite-sample references whose average bootstrap relative half-width is 27.9%, and transfers to a GCG-style corpus with 2.93% relative error after recalibration. A directional criterion $\mathrm{KL}(p_{\mathrm{good}}\,\|\,p_{\mathrm{bad}})$ ranks rulers consistently with estimation error (Spearman $ρ=0.83$).
中文标题/摘要
标题：SCARCE：通过嵌入进行稀有事件特征化的可扩展级联分析
稀有事件主导现代人工智能系统的安全特性，但其概率极难估计：直接的蒙特卡洛方法需要巨大的样本预算。子集模拟（SS）通过将稀有事件概率分解为嵌套中间事件的适度条件概率来解决这个问题。然而，经典的SS需要手工制作的标量性能函数，其子水平集定义这些事件，要求对失效几何形状有详细了解，并限制了向新领域的转移。我们提出了SCARCE（通过嵌入进行稀有事件特征化的可扩展级联分析），它用学习的潜在表示和几何尺代替性能函数，评分与失效区域的接近度。自适应阈值直接从数据构建嵌套中间事件。我们通过非负超马丁戈形式化SCARCE，产生一个在早期停止下仍然有效的高概率上包络。在MNIST错误分类中，稠密蒙特卡洛提供了真实值，SCARCE的平均绝对误差比网格搜索的传统SS低约400-500倍，同时消除了系统性过度计数。然后，我们在具有对抗性比例$η$的舰队级威胁模型下研究PAIR风格的LLM越狱。在Llama-Guard-3-8B隐藏状态上，基于PCA的尺在$η\geq 10^{-3}$时对有限样本参考的平均相对误差为2.6%，其平均自助相对半宽度为27.9%，并在重新校准后转移到GCG风格的语料库，具有2.93%的相对误差。方向标准$\mathrm{KL}(p_{\mathrm{good}}\,\|\,p_{\mathrm{bad}})$与估计误差一致地对尺进行排名（斯皮尔曼$ρ=0.83$）。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenge of estimating rare event probabilities in AI systems, which are crucial for understanding safety profiles but difficult to assess using traditional methods like Monte Carlo simulations due to high sample requirements. Previous approaches, such as Subset Simulation (SS), rely on handcrafted performance functions that require extensive knowledge of failure geometries, limiting their applicability to new domains. The proposed method, SCARCE, innovatively utilizes learned latent representations and geometric rulers to score proximity to failure regions, allowing for adaptive thresholding that constructs intermediate events directly from data. This approach is well-motivated as it overcomes the limitations of classical SS by eliminating the need for detailed failure geometry knowledge. The methodology formalizes SCARCE through a non-negative supermartingale, achieving significant performance improvements, such as a 400-500 times lower mean absolute error on MNIST misclassification compared to traditional SS, and demonstrating effective application in analyzing LLM jailbreaks with low relative error rates.
本研究解决了在人工智能系统中估计稀有事件概率的挑战，这对于评估安全性至关重要，但由于传统方法（如蒙特卡洛模拟）需要高样本量而难以量化。以往的方法，特别是子集模拟（SS），依赖于手工制作的性能函数，这需要对失败几何形状有广泛的了解，从而限制了其在新领域的适用性。所提出的方法SCARCE创新性地利用学习的潜在表示和自适应阈值直接从数据构建嵌套的中间事件，从而克服了经典SS的局限性。本文通过非负超马丁戈尔形式化了SCARCE的框架，展示了其在MNIST误分类等任务上的有效性，取得了显著低于传统SS方法的平均绝对误差。此外，SCARCE还被应用于分析LLM越狱，显示出有希望的性能指标，支持其增强稀有事件表征的目标。
Sparse Autoencoders are Capable LLM Jailbreak Mitigators
Authors: Yannick Assogba, Jacopo Cortellazzi, Javier Abad, Pau Rodriguez, Xavier Suau, Arno Blaas
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-12T21:17:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-27T01:07:47+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the Mechanistic Interpretability Workshop, ICML 2026. 31 pages, 20 figures, 7 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Jailbreak attacks remain a persistent threat to large language model safety. We propose Context-Conditioned Delta Steering (CC-Delta), an SAE-based defense that identifies jailbreak-relevant sparse features by comparing token-level representations of the same harmful request with and without jailbreak context. Using paired harmful/jailbreak prompts, CC-Delta selects features via statistical testing and applies inference-time mean-shift steering in SAE latent space. Across four aligned instruction-tuned models and twelve jailbreak attacks, CC-Delta achieves comparable or better safety-utility tradeoffs than baseline defenses operating in dense latent space. In particular, our method clearly outperforms dense mean-shift steering on all four models, and particularly against out-of-distribution attacks, showing that steering in sparse SAE feature space offers advantages over steering in dense activation space for jailbreak mitigation. Our results suggest off-the-shelf SAEs trained for interpretability can be repurposed as practical jailbreak defenses without task-specific training.

标题：稀疏自编码器能够缓解大型语言模型的越狱攻击

Just Ask: Curious Code Agents Reveal System Prompts in Frontier LLMs
Authors: Xiang Zheng, Yutao Wu, Hanxun Huang, Yige Li, Xingjun Ma, Bo Li, Yu-Gang Jiang, Cong Wang
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-01-29T03:53:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-26T10:38:52+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous code agents built on large language models are reshaping software and AI development through tool use, long-horizon reasoning, and self-directed interaction. However, this autonomy introduces a previously unrecognized security risk: agentic interaction fundamentally expands the LLM attack surface, enabling systematic probing and recovery of hidden system prompts that guide model behavior. We identify system prompt extraction as an emergent vulnerability intrinsic to code agents and present \textbf{\textsc{JustAsk}}, a self-evolving framework that autonomously discovers effective extraction strategies through interaction alone. Unlike prior prompt-engineering or dataset-based attacks, \textsc{JustAsk} requires no handcrafted prompts, labeled supervision, or privileged access beyond standard user interaction. It formulates extraction as an online exploration problem, using Upper Confidence Bound-based strategy selection and a hierarchical skill space spanning atomic probes and high-level orchestration. These skills exploit imperfect system-instruction generalization and inherent tensions between helpfulness and safety. Evaluated on \textbf{41} black-box commercial models across multiple providers, \textsc{JustAsk} consistently achieves full or near-complete system prompt recovery, revealing recurring design- and architecture-level vulnerabilities. Our results expose system prompts as a critical yet largely unprotected attack surface in modern agent systems.
中文标题/摘要

How Reliable Is Your Jailbreak Judge? Calibration and Adversarial Robustness of Automated ASR Scoring
Authors: Yang Gao
First: 2026-06-24T07:14:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-25T02:04:55+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 3 figures, 2 tables, Code: github.com/gy15901580825/judgeflip
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Almost every paper on LLM jailbreaks and prompt injection reports an attack-success rate (ASR), and that number is assigned not by people but by an automated judge: either a safety classifier trained for the task, or a general chat model prompted to grade. The judge is rarely checked. We check it. Using 596 human-labeled completions from the HarmBench classifier validation set, we compare the two judge families against human majority votes and then attack them. The two families fail in opposite ways. The dedicated classifier over-flags (precision 0.835, recall 0.974); three different LLM-as-judges keep high precision (0.81 to 0.94) but show erratic recall (0.06 to 0.65), so the same responses produce very different ASR depending on which judge scores them. The two families also differ sharply in robustness. Wrappers that leave the harmful text untouched and only add benign framing flip every LLM-judge between 57% and 100% of the time, and a single prepended refusal sentence accounts for much of this (39% to 88%). The dedicated classifier resists these surface attacks (at most 6.7%), but a white-box GCG attack on its open weights flips 70% of confident true positives (21 of 30; 95% CI 54 to 86%) even at a small optimization budget. A two-annotator audit confirms the attacks leave the harm intact: every one of 80 sampled flips still contained the harmful content. Because a large and growing share of reported ASR comes from LLM-judges, many such numbers are unreliable both on average and under deliberate pressure. We recommend that papers report judge precision and recall on a human-labeled slice, report ASR corrected for judge precision, and include an adversarial check of the judge. Our code is released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：你的越狱评估器有多可靠？自动化ASR评分的校准与对抗鲁棒性
几乎每篇关于LLM越狱和提示注入的论文都报告了攻击成功率（ASR），而这个数字不是由人类赋予的，而是由一个自动化评估器给出的：要么是为此任务训练的安全分类器，要么是被提示进行评分的一般聊天模型。评估器很少被检查。我们进行了检查。使用来自HarmBench分类器验证集的596个人工标注的完成项，我们将这两种评估器与人类多数投票进行比较，然后对其进行攻击。这两种评估器在失败方式上截然相反。专用分类器过度标记（精确度0.835，召回率0.974）；三种不同的LLM作为评估者保持高精度（0.81到0.94），但显示出不稳定的召回率（0.06到0.65），因此相同的响应根据哪个评估器评分会产生非常不同的ASR。这两种评估器在鲁棒性上也有显著差异。那些不改变有害文本，仅添加良性框架的包装器使每个LLM评估者在57%到100%的时间内翻转，而一个单独的前置拒绝句子占据了其中很大一部分（39%到88%）。专用分类器抵御这些表面攻击（最多6.7%），但对其开放权重的白盒GCG攻击在小的优化预算下翻转了70%的自信真阳性（30个中的21个；95%置信区间54到86%）。两位标注者的审计确认攻击保持了有害内容的完整性：80个抽样翻转中的每一个仍然包含有害内容。由于报告的ASR中有很大一部分来自LLM评估者，因此许多这样的数字在平均值和故意压力下都是不可靠的。我们建议论文报告人类标注切片上的评估器精度和召回率，报告经过评估器精度修正的ASR，并包括对评估器的对抗性检查。我们的代码已发布。

Previous methods relied on either dedicated safety classifiers or general chat models, both of which exhibited significant shortcomings in precision and recall. The proposed approach involves a thorough evaluation of these judges against human majority votes using a dataset of 596 human-labeled completions, revealing that dedicated classifiers over-flag while LLM judges show erratic recall. The methodology employed includes comparative analysis and adversarial testing, demonstrating that many reported ASR values are unreliable, thus supporting the need for improved evaluation standards in this field.

RAS: Measuring LLM Safety Through Refusal Alignment
Authors: Chang-Chieh Huang, Yan-Lun Chen, Chia-Mu Yu, Wei-Bin Lee
First: 2026-06-24T12:19:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-24T12:19:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety evaluation of large language models (LLMs) is commonly performed by querying models with unsafe or jailbreak prompts and judging whether their outputs violate a safety policy. Although useful, output-level evaluation is expensive, sensitive to judge choice, and easily tied to fixed question banks. We propose **SafeVec**, a white-box evaluation procedure that measures safety from internal representations rather than generated answers. **SafeVec** first extracts layer-wise refusal directions from a safety-aligned reference model, then selects stable layer windows where safe and unsafe behaviors are separable, and finally scores a target model by measuring whether its hidden states align with these refusal directions under unsafe and jailbreak prompts. The resulting metric, **RAS** (**R**efusal **A**lignment **S**core), maps representation-level refusal alignment to a calibrated 0-100 safety score. Across `Llama`, `Gemma`, and `Qwen` model families, RAS separates aligned models from uncensored and abliterated variants, tracks output-level attack success rate, and is substantially faster than judge-based evaluation. These results suggest that refusal alignment provides a compact and efficient signal for white-box LLM safety evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RAS：通过拒绝对齐测量大型语言模型的安全性
大型语言模型（LLMs）的安全性评估通常通过使用不安全或越狱提示查询模型，并判断其输出是否违反安全政策。尽管有用，输出级评估成本高、对评判者选择敏感，并且容易与固定问题库挂钩。我们提出了**SafeVec**，一种白盒评估程序，从内部表示而非生成的答案来测量安全性。**SafeVec**首先从安全对齐的参考模型中提取逐层拒绝方向，然后选择安全和不安全行为可分离的稳定层窗口，最后通过测量目标模型的隐藏状态在不安全和越狱提示下是否与这些拒绝方向对齐来评分。最终指标**RAS**（**R**efusal **A**lignment **S**core）将表示级拒绝对齐映射到校准的0-100安全分数。在`Llama`、`Gemma`和`Qwen`模型系列中，RAS将对齐模型与未审查和被消除的变体区分开，跟踪输出级攻击成功率，并且比基于评判者的评估快得多。这些结果表明，拒绝对齐为白盒LLM安全评估提供了紧凑而高效的信号。

Previous methods often depend on fixed question banks, leading to limitations in their applicability and reliability. The proposed approach, SafeVec, shifts the focus to internal representations of the models, extracting layer-wise refusal directions from a safety-aligned reference model to create a new metric called the Refusal Alignment Score (RAS). This methodology allows for a more efficient and effective evaluation of model safety by measuring alignment with refusal directions under unsafe prompts. The results demonstrate that RAS can effectively distinguish between aligned and uncensored models across various model families, providing a faster and more reliable safety evaluation method that supports the goal of improving LLM safety assessments.

TROPT: An Open Framework for Unifying and Advancing Discrete Text Optimization
Authors: Matan Ben-Tov, Mahmood Sharif
First: 2026-06-22T15:44:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T15:44:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Discrete text-trigger optimization -- searching for text sequences that, when ingested by a model, steer it toward a specified objective -- underpins model red-teaming (e.g., LLM jailbreaks), as well as auditing and interpretability. However, the current state of discrete optimizers hinders their adoption and progress. First, existing optimizers, when open-sourced at all, are scattered across research codebases tied to specific models, objectives, and problem domains. Second, optimizer variants proliferate, each requiring engineering overhead to use or extend, and remaining hard to compare head-to-head. Together, these raise the bar for adopting optimizers in existing or new domains, and for advancing them via new strategies. We address these gaps with TROPT, the first open-source framework that unifies discrete optimizers' execution and standardizes their development under a single interface. TROPT makes it easy to customize end-to-end optimization recipes by swapping any component -- models, objectives, and optimizers -- extending its reach across domains and new applications. TROPT currently ships with 30+ optimization recipes -- covering applications such as jailbreaking and probing model internals -- built from 15+ optimizers (spanning white-box to black-box access) and 15+ losses, from foundational to state-of-the-art methods. Demonstrating its utility, we leverage TROPT in several studies: (i) controlled, large-scale experiments comparing and enhancing optimization strategies for LLM jailbreaks, revealing potent-yet-underadopted techniques; and (ii) porting optimizers from one domain (e.g., LLM jailbreak) to new domains (e.g., corpus-poisoning embedding model). In all, TROPT significantly lowers the barrier to adopting and advancing discrete text optimization.

离散文本触发优化——寻找文本序列，当被模型摄取时，引导其朝向特定目标——是模型红队（例如，LLM越狱）以及审计和可解释性的基础。然而，当前离散优化器的状态阻碍了它们的采用和进展。首先，现有的优化器如果开源，通常分散在与特定模型、目标和问题领域相关的研究代码库中。其次，优化器变体繁多，每个都需要工程开销来使用或扩展，并且难以进行直接比较。这些因素共同提高了在现有或新领域中采用优化器的门槛，并通过新策略推进它们。我们通过TROPT解决这些问题，这是第一个统一离散优化器执行并在单一接口下标准化其开发的开源框架。TROPT使得通过更换任何组件——模型、目标和优化器——轻松定制端到端优化配方，扩展其在不同领域和新应用中的适用性。TROPT目前提供30多个优化配方——涵盖越狱和探测模型内部等应用——由15个以上的优化器（从白盒到黑盒访问）和15个以上的损失函数（从基础到最先进的方法）构建。为了展示其效用，我们在几项研究中利用TROPT：（i）控制的大规模实验比较和增强LLM越狱的优化策略，揭示出强大但未被广泛采用的技术；（ii）将优化器从一个领域（例如，LLM越狱）移植到新领域（例如，语料库中毒嵌入模型）。总之，TROPT显著降低了采用和推进离散文本优化的门槛。

Culturally-Adapted Red-Teaming Across East and Southeast Asian Contexts: A Methodological and Comparative Analysis
Authors: Hyeji Choi, Yongtaek Lim, Minwoo Kim
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-08T08:17:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-22T07:46:06+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026 Workshop on Culture X AI
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multilingual safety evaluation of large language models (LLMs) has predominantly relied on direct translation (DT) of English benchmarks into target languages - an approach that converts surface-level linguistic form while failing to reflect the cultural context embedded in threat scenarios, social norms, and legal frameworks. We construct paired DT and culturally-adapted (CA) datasets via 1:1 seed matching for four languages - Korean (KO), Japanese (JA), Thai (TH), and Khmer (KM) - and compare Attack Success Rate (ASR) and Cultural Realism scores across four open-source LLM. CA prompts yield Delta-ASR > 0 across all 16 language x model combinations (mean +9.3 pp), and DT-based evaluation underestimates risk in 44 of 48 category x language combinations. Language-level analysis reveals that the distribution of threat forms is heterogeneous across languages. Cultural Realism analysis further shows that DT Cultural Depth (C3) scores remain consistently below 1.0 out of 3.0 across all four languages (mean 0.17), whereas CA scores reach up to 2.51, indicating that direct translation produces inputs systematically divergent from those encountered in real-world multicultural settings. These findings demonstrate that adapting benchmarks to language-specific cultural contexts - rather than relying on linguistic translation alone - is necessary for valid multilingual LLM safety evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：跨东亚和东南亚背景的文化适应性红队：方法论和比较分析
大型语言模型（LLMs）的多语言安全评估主要依赖于将英语基准直接翻译（DT）为目标语言，这种方法仅转换表层语言形式，未能反映威胁场景、社会规范和法律框架中嵌入的文化背景。我们通过1:1种子匹配构建了四种语言（韩语、日语、泰语和高棉语）的配对DT和文化适应（CA）数据集，并比较了四个开源LLM的攻击成功率（ASR）和文化现实评分。CA提示在所有16种语言x模型组合中均产生Delta-ASR > 0（平均+9.3个百分点），而基于DT的评估在48个类别x语言组合中低估了风险。语言级分析显示，威胁形式的分布在不同语言中是异质的。文化现实分析进一步表明，DT文化深度（C3）评分在所有四种语言中始终低于3.0的1.0（平均0.17），而CA评分高达2.51，表明直接翻译产生的输入与现实世界多文化环境中遇到的输入系统性地不同。这些发现表明，适应特定语言文化背景的基准，而不仅仅依赖语言翻译，对于有效的多语言LLM安全评估是必要的。

AgentLens: Interpretable Safety Steering via Mechanistic Subspaces for Multi-Turn Coding Agent
Authors: Weidi Luo, Qiming Zhang, Yihao Quan, Mingyu Jin, Jie Cai, Chaowei Xiao, Jingcheng Niu, Zhen Xiang
First: 2026-06-21T21:13:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-21T21:13:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Coding agents based on large language models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable autonomous capabilities, but they also introduce significant safety and misuse risks during multi-turn interactions with external environments. Existing safety mechanisms mainly rely on external guardrails, which have a limited ability to perform fine-grained behavioral control during execution. Meanwhile, recent mechanistic interpretability methods for LLM safety are mostly confined to single-turn or jailbreak-style QA settings, limiting their ability to capture the evolving risk dynamics of multi-turn agent execution. In this paper, we investigate the safety of multi-turn coding agents from an internal perspective. We propose AgentLens (Mechanistic Subspace Intervention and Steering), a white-box defense framework that performs runtime safety detection and representation-level mitigation for coding agents. Unlike conventional agent guardrails, AgentLens detect harmful execution states from step-level hidden representations and mitigate unsafe behavior by intervening in a 10-dimensional subspace within a single layer. To support this research, we introduce the Mechanistic Agent Safety (MAS) benchmark, comprising comprehensively annotated multi-turn execution trajectories across 194 tasks using LLaMA-3.1-8B, Qwen-2.5-7B, and Gemma-2-9B. Extensive experiments show that AgentLens achieves strong safety detection performance, provides preliminary evidence for lookahead risk anticipation, and substantially reduces harmful actions of the coding agent, establishing a foundation for applying mechanistic interpretability to dynamic LLM agent safety. The code is available at: https://github.com/EddyLuo1232/AgentLens
中文标题/摘要

基于大型语言模型（LLMs）的编码代理展示了显著的自主能力，但在与外部环境的多轮交互中也引入了重大安全和误用风险。现有的安全机制主要依赖外部护栏，在执行过程中对细粒度行为控制的能力有限。同时，最近针对LLM安全的机制可解释性方法大多局限于单轮或越狱式问答设置，限制了其捕捉多轮代理执行中不断变化的风险动态的能力。本文从内部视角研究多轮编码代理的安全性。我们提出了AgentLens（机制子空间干预与引导），这是一个白盒防御框架，能够对编码代理进行运行时安全检测和表示级别的缓解。与传统的代理护栏不同，AgentLens通过从步骤级隐藏表示中检测有害执行状态，并通过干预单层内的10维子空间来缓解不安全行为。为了支持这项研究，我们引入了机制代理安全（MAS）基准，涵盖了使用LLaMA-3.1-8B、Qwen-2.5-7B和Gemma-2-9B的194个任务的全面注释的多轮执行轨迹。大量实验表明，AgentLens实现了强大的安全检测性能，为前瞻性风险预判提供了初步证据，并显著减少了编码代理的有害行为，为将机制可解释性应用于动态LLM代理安全奠定了基础。代码可在：https://github.com/EddyLuo1232/AgentLens

Enhancing LLM Safety Through a Theoretical Minimax Game Lens
Authors: Yihe Deng, Yu Yang, Junkai Zhang, Wei Wang, Bo Li
First: 2025-02-07T18:45:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-15T02:50:15+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 9 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs) necessitates effective mechanisms to ensure their responsible deployment by accurately distinguishing unsafe content from benign content. While substantial safety datasets are available in English, multilingual safety modeling remains underexplored due to limited open-source safety datasets in other languages. Even within English datasets, safe yet sensitive corner-case content is scarce, leading to shortcut learning by models and non-trivial false-positive rates. To mitigate these issues, we introduce a novel minimax reinforcement learning (RL) framework wherein a data generator and a classifier model co-evolve, facilitating the production of high-quality synthetic multilingual safety data. We theoretically formalize this interaction as a minimax game and rigorously demonstrate convergence to a Nash equilibrium. Empirical evaluations confirm that our synthetic data generation method significantly enhances the classifier model performance, enabling a substantially smaller model to surpass the state-of-the-art by nearly 10% on English benchmarks while achieving 4.5x faster inference speed. These results establish a scalable and efficient methodology for synthetic data generation, advancing the development of safer and more robust multilingual LLM deployments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过理论最小最大博弈视角增强大型语言模型安全性
大型语言模型（LLMs）的快速发展需要有效机制，以确保其负责任的部署，准确区分不安全内容与良性内容。虽然英语中有大量安全数据集，但由于其他语言中开放源代码安全数据集有限，多语言安全建模仍未得到充分探索。即使在英语数据集中，安全但敏感的边缘案例内容也很稀缺，导致模型的捷径学习和非平凡的误报率。为了解决这些问题，我们引入了一种新颖的最小最大强化学习（RL）框架，其中数据生成器和分类模型共同进化，促进高质量合成多语言安全数据的生成。我们将这种交互理论化为最小最大博弈，并严格证明其收敛到纳什均衡。实证评估确认，我们的合成数据生成方法显著提升了分类模型的性能，使得一个显著更小的模型在英语基准测试中超越了最先进的技术近10%，同时实现了4.5倍的推理速度提升。这些结果建立了一种可扩展且高效的合成数据生成方法，推动了更安全、更强大的多语言LLM部署的发展。

Previous methods have relied heavily on existing English datasets, which often lack diverse and sensitive corner-case examples, leading to shortcut learning and high false-positive rates. The proposed minimax reinforcement learning framework innovatively allows a data generator and classifier to co-evolve, producing high-quality synthetic multilingual safety data while formalizing their interaction as a minimax game. This approach effectively mitigates the limitations of existing methods and is well-motivated by the need for robust multilingual safety modeling. The paper contributes a scalable methodology that significantly improves classifier performance, achieving nearly a 10% increase over state-of-the-art models on English benchmarks and a 4.5x faster inference speed, thereby supporting the goal of safer and more robust LLM deployments.

GAS-Leak-LLM: Genetic Algorithm-Based Suffix Optimization for Black-Box LLM Jailbreaking
Authors: Aman Anifer, Vignesh Kumar Kembu, Vishnu M, Antonino Nocera, Vinod P., Amal Murali PK, Akshay S Rajan
First: 2026-06-14T12:41:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-14T12:41:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) constitute pivotal components within the AI-dominated information technology ecosystem. To mitigate risks associated with harmful or policy-violating outputs, commercial systems employ advanced alignment strategies and multi-layered content moderation mechanisms. Despite these safeguards, recent research has demonstrated that LLMs remain vulnerable to adversarial manipulation, particularly through jailbreaking and prompt injection techniques. In this work, we propose GAS-Leak-LLM a novel jailbreaking attack based on a genetic algorithm that systematically evolves adversarial suffix to bypass safety constraints. Operating in a strict black-box setting, our method requires no access to model parameters or internals, thereby reflecting realistic threat scenarios in deployed systems. Through the iterative application of selection, mutation, and crossover heuristics, the framework systematically explores the discrete prompt space to identify high-fitness adversarial suffixes. Empirical findings reveal critical shortcomings in existing safety enforcement mechanisms and confirm the effectiveness and practical viability of the proposed attack.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GAS-Leak-LLM：基于遗传算法的黑箱LLM越狱后缀优化
大型语言模型（LLMs）是AI主导的信息技术生态系统中的关键组成部分。为了减轻与有害或违反政策的输出相关的风险，商业系统采用先进的对齐策略和多层次内容审核机制。尽管有这些保护措施，最近的研究表明，LLMs仍然容易受到对抗性操控，特别是通过越狱和提示注入技术。在本研究中，我们提出了GAS-Leak-LLM，一种基于遗传算法的新型越狱攻击，系统地演化对抗性后缀以绕过安全约束。在严格的黑箱环境中，我们的方法不需要访问模型参数或内部结构，从而反映了已部署系统中的现实威胁场景。通过选择、变异和交叉启发式的迭代应用，该框架系统地探索离散提示空间，以识别高适应性的对抗性后缀。实证研究揭示了现有安全执行机制的关键缺陷，并确认了所提攻击的有效性和实际可行性。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the vulnerabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) to adversarial manipulation, particularly through jailbreaking and prompt injection techniques, despite existing safety measures. Previous methods have relied on traditional alignment strategies and content moderation, which have proven insufficient against sophisticated attacks. The proposed approach, GAS-Leak-LLM, utilizes a genetic algorithm to evolve adversarial suffixes in a black-box setting, allowing it to bypass safety constraints without needing access to model internals. This method is well-motivated by the need for realistic threat assessments in deployed systems. The paper contributes by demonstrating the effectiveness of this novel jailbreaking attack, revealing significant weaknesses in current safety mechanisms, and showcasing its practical viability through empirical testing.
本研究关注大型语言模型（LLMs）在面对对抗性操控时的脆弱性，尤其是越狱和提示注入技术，尽管已有安全措施。以往的方法依赖于对齐策略和内容审核，但在复杂攻击面前效果不佳。提出的方法GAS-Leak-LLM利用遗传算法在黑箱环境中演化对抗后缀，无需访问模型内部，从而模拟现实世界的威胁场景。该方法通过选择、变异和交叉启发式系统地探索提示空间，为当前安全机制的局限性提供了更深入的理解。实验结果显示这些机制存在显著弱点，并验证了GAS-Leak-LLM攻击的有效性，突显其在绕过安全约束方面的实际适用性。
CHILLGuard: Towards Fine-Grained Chinese LLM Safety Guardrail with Scalable Data Construction and Model-aware Preference Alignment
Authors: Wenbo Yu, Bohua Wang, Hao Fang, Kuofeng Gao, Jingru Zeng, Xiaochen Yang, Tianyi Zhang, Xiaoxiao Ma, Jiawei Kong, Hao Wu, Bin Chen, Shu-Tao Xia, Min Zhang
First: 2026-06-13T16:57:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-13T16:57:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Malicious content generated from large language models (LLMs) could pose severe safety risks and ethical concerns. While existing LLM safety guardrails excel in English or multilingual settings, they lack adaptation to Chinese-specific regulatory policies, cultural context and linguistic nuances, failing to support fine-grained risk classification for diverse deployment needs. In this paper, we introduce a 5-macro, 31-micro category fine-grained risk taxonomy for Chinese scenarios, and build CHILLGuard: a dedicated Chinese LLM content safety guardrail. To address the critical scarcity of high-quality annotated Chinese safety data, we propose a scalable multi-stage data construction pipeline: we expand multi-source corpus via retrieval-augmented generation, generate implicit harmful samples through prompt engineering rewriting, and refine high-quality data via multi-model voting-based label calibration. Based on this, we build CHILLGuardTrain, a large-scale training set with 405,007 samples, and CHILLGuardTest, a rigorously curated annotated test set with 51,745 samples. We then train CHILLGuard on CHILLGuardTrain under a generator-classifier collaborative framework via Model-aware Direct Preference Optimization. Extensive experiments under multiple settings demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance of CHILLGuard, e.g., a 15.92% improvement of F1 score over Qwen3Guard-8B-Strict on our benchmark. We will release our resources at https://github.com/cswbyu/CHILLGuard.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CHILLGuard：面向细粒度中文LLM安全防护的可扩展数据构建与模型感知偏好对齐
大型语言模型（LLMs）生成的恶意内容可能带来严重的安全风险和伦理问题。现有的LLM安全防护在英语或多语言环境中表现出色，但缺乏对中国特定监管政策、文化背景和语言细微差别的适应，无法支持多样化部署需求的细粒度风险分类。本文提出了一种针对中国场景的5个宏观、31个微观类别的细粒度风险分类法，并构建了CHILLGuard：一个专门的中文LLM内容安全防护。为了解决高质量标注中文安全数据的严重短缺，我们提出了一种可扩展的多阶段数据构建流程：通过检索增强生成扩展多源语料，通过提示工程重写生成隐性有害样本，并通过多模型投票的标签校准精炼高质量数据。在此基础上，我们构建了CHILLGuardTrain，一个包含405,007个样本的大规模训练集，以及CHILLGuardTest，一个经过严格策划的包含51,745个样本的标注测试集。然后，我们在CHILLGuardTrain上通过模型感知直接偏好优化的生成器-分类器协作框架训练CHILLGuard。多种设置下的广泛实验表明，CHILLGuard的性能达到了最先进水平，例如，在我们的基准测试中，F1分数比Qwen3Guard-8B-Strict提高了15.92%。我们将把我们的资源发布在https://github.com/cswbyu/CHILLGuard。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the safety risks and ethical concerns associated with malicious content generated by large language models (LLMs), particularly focusing on the inadequacies of existing LLM safety guardrails in adapting to the Chinese context. Previous methods have struggled with a lack of high-quality annotated data and insufficient fine-grained risk classification tailored to Chinese regulatory and cultural nuances. The proposed approach, CHILLGuard, introduces a comprehensive fine-grained risk taxonomy specifically for Chinese scenarios and employs a scalable multi-stage data construction pipeline to generate and refine safety data. This methodology includes retrieval-augmented generation and prompt engineering to create harmful samples, resulting in a large-scale training set of 405,007 samples and a curated test set of 51,745 samples. The experimental results demonstrate that CHILLGuard achieves state-of-the-art performance, with a notable 15.92% improvement in F1 score over existing models, effectively supporting the goal of enhancing content safety in Chinese LLM applications.

Do You Really Need a GPU to Guard Your LLM? CPU-Class Classifiers and Multi-Stage Pipelines for Safety Enforcement at Scale
Authors: Vasudev Majhi, Dhruv Gupta, Advait Singh, Matthew Barker, Dhruv Kumar
First: 2025-12-22T04:00:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-13T05:46:54+00:00
Comments: Under Review. 25 pages, 5 figures, 38 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety classifiers that screen LLM inputs for jailbreak attempts have become standard deployment components, yet almost all production systems rely on GPU-based models: fine-tuned transformers and LLM-as-a-judge pipelines. These approaches impose significant per-query latency and infrastructure cost. Very little research has asked whether CPU-based classifiers, such as support vector machines and gradient-boosted trees trained on TF-IDF features, can match their accuracy across the conditions that production deployments encounter. We evaluate five CPU classifier families, Mamba-130M as an SSM-based GPU classifier, and transformer-based GPU models (DeBERTa-v3 and Gemma-2B with LoRA) across nine jailbreak sources and three regimes: in-distribution (D1), out-of-distribution (D2), and adversarially obfuscated (D3). On D1, the best CPU classifier matches the best transformer GPU model at roughly one-fifth the deployment cost. On D2, CPU classifiers fail via confident miscalibration, producing high-confidence false negatives that bypass escalation entirely. On D3, CPU classifiers outperform transformer GPU models by more than 26 percentage points in F1. Based on these complementary failure modes, we design GuardChain, a three-stage safety pipeline (Regex -> CPU -> GPU) that routes each prompt to the cheapest stage capable of a confident decision. The CPU stage alone resolves 80\% of in-distribution prompts at near-peak accuracy, and the GPU stage recovers the out-of-distribution failures. For practitioners deploying LLM safety at scale, this work provides evidence that GPU-class infrastructure is unnecessary for the majority of traffic.
中文标题/摘要
标题：您真的需要 GPU 来保护您的 LLM 吗？用于安全执行的 CPU 类分类器和多阶段管道
用于筛查 LLM 输入的安全分类器已成为标准部署组件，但几乎所有生产系统都依赖于基于 GPU 的模型：微调的变换器和 LLM 作为裁判的管道。这些方法在每个查询上都带来了显著的延迟和基础设施成本。很少有研究探讨基于 CPU 的分类器，如支持向量机和基于 TF-IDF 特征训练的梯度提升树，是否能在生产部署所遇到的条件下匹配其准确性。 我们评估了五个 CPU 分类器系列，Mamba-130M 作为基于 SSM 的 GPU 分类器，以及基于变换器的 GPU 模型（DeBERTa-v3 和 Gemma-2B 与 LoRA）在九个越狱来源和三个模式下的表现：分布内（D1）、分布外（D2）和对抗性混淆（D3）。在 D1 中，最佳 CPU 分类器的表现与最佳变换器 GPU 模型相当，部署成本约为其五分之一。在 D2 中，CPU 分类器因自信的错误校准而失败，产生高置信度的假阴性，完全绕过升级。在 D3 中，CPU 分类器在 F1 上的表现超过变换器 GPU 模型超过 26 个百分点。 基于这些互补的失败模式，我们设计了 GuardChain，一个三阶段安全管道（Regex -> CPU -> GPU），将每个提示路由到能够做出自信决策的最便宜阶段。仅 CPU 阶段就以接近峰值的准确率解决了 80% 的分布内提示，而 GPU 阶段则恢复了分布外的失败。对于大规模部署 LLM 安全的从业者而言，这项工作提供了证据，表明 GPU 类基础设施对于大多数流量来说并非必要。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the reliance on GPU-based models for safety classifiers that screen inputs for jailbreak attempts in large language models (LLMs), highlighting the significant latency and cost associated with these systems. Previous methods primarily utilized fine-tuned transformers and LLM-as-a-judge pipelines, which often resulted in high operational costs and latency issues. This paper proposes a novel approach using CPU-based classifiers, such as support vector machines and gradient-boosted trees, which are evaluated against GPU models across various conditions. The findings reveal that the best CPU classifier can match the performance of the top transformer GPU model at a fraction of the cost, while also outperforming it in adversarial scenarios. The proposed methodology, GuardChain, is a three-stage safety pipeline that efficiently routes prompts to the most cost-effective classifier, achieving high accuracy and demonstrating that GPU infrastructure is not necessary for most LLM safety applications.

NeST: Neuron Selective Tuning for LLM Safety
Authors: Sasha Behrouzi, Lichao Wu, Mohamadreza Rostami, Ahmad-Reza Sadeghi
First: 2026-02-18T20:01:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-12T11:06:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety alignment is essential for the responsible deployment of Large Language Models (LLMs). Yet, existing approaches often rely on heavyweight fine-tuning that is costly to update, audit, and maintain across model families. Full fine-tuning incurs substantial computational and storage overhead, while parameter-efficient methods, e.g., Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), trade efficiency for inconsistent safety gains and sensitivity to design choices. Safety intervention mechanisms reduce unsafe outputs without modifying model weights, but do not directly shape or preserve the internal representations that govern safety behavior. We present NeST, a Neuron-Selective Tuning framework for efficient post-hoc safety alignment. NeST identifies safety-relevant feed-forward neurons via activation probing on vanilla harmful and benign prompts, clusters neurons with similar activation profiles, and trains shared cluster-level updates while freezing the rest of the model. Importantly, NeST is trained only on vanilla malicious prompts, without using jailbreak-specific attack data, yet generalizes robustly to diverse jailbreaks. The learned updates are then folded into the original weights, incurring no inference-time overhead. Evaluated on 14 open-weight language and multimodal models, NeST outperforms lightweight baselines and approaches full fine-tuning robustness with significantly fewer trainable parameters. On text-only models, NeST reduces average jailbreak attack success rate from 44.5% to 1.1% while training only 0.4M parameters on average. Across multimodal settings, it reduces ASR from 55.3% to 1.1%, and for downstream fine-tuned variants, it restores safety by reducing ASR from 53.8% to 0.8%. These results show that robust, maintainable safety alignment can be achieved by concentrating adaptation on localized, functionally coherent safety structures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NeST：用于大型语言模型安全的神经元选择性调优
安全对齐对于大型语言模型（LLMs）的负责任部署至关重要。然而，现有方法通常依赖于成本高昂的重调优，难以在模型家族中进行更新、审计和维护。完全调优会产生大量的计算和存储开销，而参数高效的方法，如低秩适应（LoRA），则在效率与安全增益不一致和对设计选择敏感之间进行权衡。安全干预机制在不修改模型权重的情况下减少不安全输出，但并未直接塑造或保留影响安全行为的内部表示。 我们提出了NeST，一种用于高效事后安全对齐的神经元选择性调优框架。NeST通过对普通有害和良性提示的激活探测，识别与安全相关的前馈神经元，聚类具有相似激活特征的神经元，并在冻结模型其余部分的同时训练共享的聚类级更新。重要的是，NeST仅在普通恶意提示上进行训练，而不使用特定于越狱的攻击数据，但能够稳健地推广到多种越狱场景。学习到的更新随后被折叠到原始权重中，不会增加推理时的开销。在14个开放权重的语言和多模态模型上进行评估，NeST的表现优于轻量级基线，并以显著更少的可训练参数接近完全调优的鲁棒性。在仅文本模型上，NeST将平均越狱攻击成功率从44.5%降低到1.1%，同时平均仅训练0.4M参数。在多模态设置中，它将ASR从55.3%降低到1.1%，对于下游微调变体，它通过将ASR从53.8%降低到0.8%来恢复安全。这些结果表明，通过集中适应于局部、功能一致的安全结构，可以实现稳健、可维护的安全对齐。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the critical need for safety alignment in the deployment of Large Language Models (LLMs), highlighting the limitations of existing methods such as full fine-tuning, which is resource-intensive, and parameter-efficient methods like Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), which compromise safety consistency. The proposed NeST (Neuron-Selective Tuning) framework offers a novel approach by identifying and tuning only the safety-relevant neurons through activation probing, allowing for efficient post-hoc safety alignment without modifying the entire model. This method is motivated by the need for a more maintainable and effective safety intervention that generalizes well across various attack scenarios. NeST demonstrates significant contributions by achieving robust safety improvements with fewer trainable parameters, reducing the average jailbreak attack success rate from 44.5% to 1.1% in text-only models and from 55.3% to 1.1% in multimodal settings, thereby supporting the goal of effective safety alignment in LLMs.
本研究解决了大型语言模型（LLM）部署中安全对齐的关键需求，强调了现有方法的局限性，如全量微调资源密集，以及低秩适应（LoRA）等参数高效方法在安全一致性上的妥协。提出的神经元选择性调优（NeST）框架通过基于激活模式识别和调优仅与安全相关的神经元，提供了一种新颖的方法，实现高效的后期安全对齐，而无需修改整个模型。该方法动机充分，因为它在保持安全有效性的同时减少了计算负担。NeST在14个开放权重的语言和多模态模型上进行了评估，取得了显著的性能提升，例如将平均越狱攻击成功率从44.5%降低到1.1%，仅需0.4M可训练参数，证明了有针对性的适应可以实现稳健且可维护的安全对齐。
HarmProfile: Characterizing Harmful Distributions in Frontier LLMs
Authors: Zhouyuan Ma, Yutao Wu, Hanxun Huang, Xiang Zheng, Xiao Liu, Yixin Cao, Zuxuan Wu, Xingjun Ma, Yu-Gang Jiang
First: 2026-06-11T09:39:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-11T09:39:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Frontier large language models (LLMs) safety evaluation has largely treated harmful generation as an attack outcome rather than as an object of analysis. Consequently, little is known about the harmful outputs produced during model misbehavior, partly because large-scale, high-quality collections of frontier-LLM misbehavior are difficult to obtain. To address this gap, we introduce HarmProfile, a content-centric benchmark dataset that collects model misbehavior across diverse harm categories and model families, and defines the resulting harmful-output distribution as a model-level risk profile. The premise is that, just as linguistic behavior can be characterized from an utterance corpus, model risk can be characterized from the content, severity, and variation of its safety failures. HarmProfile contains over 80,000 validated artifacts from 23 frontier LLMs across 13 model families, organized into 15 harm categories and 57 subcategories. Using this corpus, we find that frontier LLMs reliably produce harmful content at scale, yet exhibit distinct risk profiles; both harmfulness and diversity grow with model capability, suggesting that frontier LLMs may appear safe yet harbor increasingly dangerous knowledge beneath the alignment surface. Our source code is available at https://github.com/fresh-ma/HarmProfile .
中文标题/摘要
标题：HarmProfile：前沿大型语言模型中有害分布的特征化
前沿大型语言模型（LLMs）的安全评估在很大程度上将有害生成视为攻击结果，而非分析对象。因此，对于模型失常期间产生的有害输出知之甚少，部分原因是难以获得大规模、高质量的前沿LLM失常数据集。为了解决这一问题，我们引入了HarmProfile，这是一个以内容为中心的基准数据集，收集了来自不同有害类别和模型家族的模型失常，并将结果有害输出分布定义为模型级风险概况。前提是，正如语言行为可以从发言语料库中表征，模型风险可以从其安全失效的内容、严重性和变化中表征。HarmProfile包含来自23个前沿LLM和13个模型家族的超过80,000个经过验证的文档，组织成15个有害类别和57个子类别。利用该语料库，我们发现前沿LLM在规模上可靠地产生有害内容，但表现出不同的风险特征；有害性和多样性随着模型能力的提高而增长，这表明前沿LLM可能看似安全，但在对齐表面下潜藏着越来越危险的知识。我们的源代码可在https://github.com/fresh-ma/HarmProfile获取。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the gap in understanding harmful outputs generated by frontier large language models (LLMs), which have been primarily evaluated for safety as an attack outcome rather than as a subject of analysis. Previous methods lacked comprehensive datasets to analyze model misbehavior, leading to insufficient insights into harmful generation. The proposed approach, HarmProfile, introduces a content-centric benchmark dataset that compiles over 80,000 validated artifacts from 23 frontier LLMs across 15 harm categories, allowing for a detailed characterization of harmful-output distributions as model-level risk profiles. This methodology enables the analysis of the content, severity, and variation of safety failures, revealing that while frontier LLMs produce harmful content reliably, they also exhibit distinct risk profiles that correlate with model capability. The findings indicate that harmfulness and diversity in outputs increase with model capability, suggesting that seemingly safe models may still possess dangerous knowledge, thus supporting the need for more nuanced safety evaluations.

Geometry-Guided Constraint Learning for LLM Safety Classification
Authors: Fumiaki Uehara, Koo Imai, Masato Tsutsumi, Keigo Kansa, Sora Usui, Yuki Kobiyama
First: 2026-06-09T08:47:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-09T08:47:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety as Polytope (SaP) learns linear half-space constraints in LLM hidden space but requires per-category tuning of the constraint count K. We show that sparse autoencoder (SAE) feature extraction resolves this: K=2 becomes optimal for 12/14 categories on Qwen3.5-9B, achieving 96-99% accuracy per category on our BeaverTails classification benchmark, largely eliminating the need for exhaustive sweeps (K=4-25 with random initialization). This convergence to two planes is consistent with the Linear Representation Hypothesis, providing suggestive evidence that safety boundaries in this setting admit a low-dimensional linear description in the SAE feature space. Building on this geometric perspective, we introduce a cone constraint whose learnable aperture adapts to each category's cluster concentration, stabilized by a three-phase training
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于几何引导的约束学习用于大型语言模型安全分类
安全作为多面体（SaP）在大型语言模型隐藏空间中学习线性半空间约束，但需要对每个类别调整约束数量K。我们表明，稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征提取解决了这个问题：在Qwen3.5-9B上，K=2对12/14个类别是最优的，在我们的BeaverTails分类基准上每个类别的准确率达到96-99%，大大减少了对全面搜索（K=4-25，随机初始化）的需求。这种收敛到两个平面的现象与线性表示假设一致，提供了暗示性证据，表明在这种情况下的安全边界在SAE特征空间中可以用低维线性描述。基于这种几何视角，我们引入了一种锥约束，其可学习的开口适应每个类别的聚类浓度，通过三阶段训练进行稳定。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the challenge of safety classification in large language models (LLMs), specifically focusing on the limitations of the existing Safety as Polytope (SaP) method, which requires extensive tuning of constraint counts for different categories. The proposed approach utilizes sparse autoencoder (SAE) feature extraction to optimize the constraint count, demonstrating that a fixed count of K=2 is effective for 12 out of 14 categories in the Qwen3.5-9B model, achieving high accuracy rates of 96-99% on the BeaverTails classification benchmark. This method not only simplifies the tuning process but also aligns with the Linear Representation Hypothesis, suggesting that safety boundaries can be described in a low-dimensional space. The introduction of a cone constraint with a learnable aperture further enhances the model's adaptability to category-specific data distributions, leading to improved performance and stability during training.
本研究解决了大型语言模型（LLMs）中的安全分类挑战，特别关注现有方法（如安全多面体（SaP））的局限性，这些方法需要对不同类别的约束数量进行广泛调整。提出的方法利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征提取来优化约束数量，证明在Qwen3.5-9B模型中，对于14个类别中的12个，固定的K=2数量足以实现96-99%的准确率，且在BeaverTails分类基准上表现良好。该方法不仅通过消除对广泛参数搜索的需求简化了过程，还与线性表示假设一致，表明安全边界可以有效地在低维空间中表示。引入具有可学习开口的锥约束进一步增强了模型对类别特定数据分布的适应性，从而提高了性能和训练稳定性。
PreAct-Bench: Benchmarking Predictive Monitoring in LLMs
Authors: Hainiu Xu, Italo Luis da Silva, Jiangnan Ye, Yuhao Wang, Wei Liu, Linyi Yang, Jonathan Richard Schwarz, Nicola Paoletti, Yulan He, Hanqi Yan
First: 2026-06-03T13:26:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-03T13:26:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents capable of executing multi-step action trajectories toward a given objective. While existing safety research has focused on detecting unethical behavior from complete trajectories, this paradigm is fundamentally retrospective: it identifies harm only after it has already occurred. In this work, we study a critical yet overlooked safety task, which we term Predictive Monitoring: given only a partial action trajectory, can a model infer whether it will culminate in an unethical action before the overt action is executed? To support this task, we present PreActBench, a benchmark of 1,000 paired ethical and unethical action trajectories spanning five domains. We evaluate a range of LLMs, safety guardrail models, and latent probing methods across varying fractions of the action trajectory using our Prefix Foresight F1 metric. Results show that while humans achieve promising performance, predictive monitoring remains challenging even for strong models, highlighting the need for future-oriented risk reasoning in LLM safety.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PreAct-Bench：大语言模型预测监控的基准测试
大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地被部署为能够执行多步骤行动轨迹以实现特定目标的自主代理。虽然现有的安全研究集中在检测完整轨迹中的不道德行为，但这种范式在根本上是回顾性的：它仅在伤害发生后才进行识别。在本研究中，我们研究了一个关键但被忽视的安全任务，我们称之为预测监控：仅给定部分行动轨迹，模型能否推断出在显性行动执行之前它是否会以不道德的行为告终？为支持这一任务，我们提出了PreAct-Bench，这是一个涵盖五个领域的1,000对伦理和不伦理行动轨迹的基准测试。我们使用我们的Prefix Foresight F1指标评估了一系列LLMs、安全护栏模型和潜在探测方法在不同的行动轨迹分数下的表现。结果表明，尽管人类表现出良好的性能，但即使对于强模型，预测监控仍然具有挑战性，突显了LLM安全中未来导向风险推理的必要性。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the growing deployment of large language models (LLMs) as autonomous agents, emphasizing the need for proactive safety measures rather than retrospective harm detection. Previous methods primarily focused on evaluating complete action trajectories, which fail to prevent unethical actions before they occur. The proposed approach introduces Predictive Monitoring, allowing models to assess the ethical implications of partial action trajectories, thus addressing the limitations of existing methods. This study contributes by presenting PreAct-Bench, a benchmark consisting of 1,000 ethical and unethical action trajectory pairs across five domains, and evaluates various LLMs and safety models using the Prefix Foresight F1 metric. The findings indicate that while human performance is promising, predictive monitoring remains a significant challenge for current models, underscoring the necessity for enhanced future-oriented risk reasoning in LLM safety.
本研究关注大型语言模型（LLMs）作为自主代理的日益普及，并指出现有安全措施的不足，这些措施通常集中在事后检测不道德行为。以往的方法主要评估完整的行动轨迹，未能从部分轨迹中预测潜在的不道德行为。提出的预测监控方法旨在在不道德行为发生之前推断其可能性，从而将重点转向主动安全措施。本文介绍了PreAct-Bench，一个包含1000对跨五个领域的伦理和不伦理行动轨迹的基准，并使用Prefix Foresight F1指标评估各种模型。研究结果表明，尽管人类表现良好，但即使是先进模型的预测监控仍然是一个重大挑战，强调了在LLM安全中改善面向未来的风险推理的必要性。
TamperBench: Systematically Stress-Testing LLM Safety Under Fine-Tuning and Tampering
Authors: Saad Hossain, Tom Tseng, Punya Syon Pandey, Samanvay Vajpayee, Matthew Kowal, Nayeema Nonta, Samuel Simko, Stephen Casper, Zhijing Jin, Kellin Pelrine, Sirisha Rambhatla
First: 2026-02-06T18:04:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T23:27:30+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 15 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
As increasingly capable open-weight large language models (LLMs) are deployed, improving their tamper resistance against unsafe modifications, whether accidental or intentional, becomes critical to minimize risks. However, there is no standard approach to evaluate tamper resistance. Varied datasets, metrics, and tampering configurations make it difficult to compare safety, utility, and robustness across different models and defenses. To address this, we introduce TamperBench, the first unified framework to systematically evaluate the tamper resistance of LLMs. TamperBench (i) curates a repository of state-of-the-art weight-space fine-tuning attacks, latent-space representation attacks, and alignment-stage defenses; (ii) enables realistic adversarial evaluation through systematic hyperparameter sweeps per attack-model pair; and (iii) provides both safety and utility evaluations. We use TamperBench to evaluate 21 open-weight LLMs, including defense-augmented variants, across nine tampering threats using standardized safety and capability metrics with hyperparameter sweeps per model-attack pair. The results provide insights including effects of post-training on tamper resistance, that jailbreak-tuning is typically the most severe attack, and that current alignment-stage defenses largely fail to withstand attack sweeps. Code is available at https://github.com/criticalml-uw/TamperBench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TamperBench：系统性压力测试LLM在微调和篡改下的安全性
随着越来越强大的开放权重大型语言模型（LLM）的部署，提高其对不安全修改的篡改抵抗能力，无论是意外还是故意，变得至关重要，以最小化风险。然而，目前没有标准的方法来评估篡改抵抗力。不同的数据集、指标和篡改配置使得比较不同模型和防御的安全性、实用性和鲁棒性变得困难。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了TamperBench，这是第一个统一框架，用于系统性评估LLM的篡改抵抗力。TamperBench (i) 策划了一系列最先进的权重空间微调攻击、潜在空间表示攻击和对齐阶段防御；(ii) 通过对每个攻击-模型对进行系统的超参数搜索，实现现实的对抗性评估；(iii) 提供安全性和实用性评估。我们使用TamperBench评估了21个开放权重LLM，包括增强防御的变体，针对九种篡改威胁，使用标准化的安全性和能力指标，并对每个模型-攻击对进行超参数搜索。结果提供了包括后训练对篡改抵抗力影响的见解，越狱微调通常是最严重的攻击，以及当前对齐阶段的防御在很大程度上未能抵御攻击的结果。代码可在https://github.com/criticalml-uw/TamperBench获取。

Previous methods have been inconsistent due to varied datasets and metrics, making it challenging to compare the safety and robustness of different models. The proposed approach, TamperBench, offers a unified framework that systematically evaluates LLM tamper resistance through curated attacks and defenses, enabling realistic adversarial evaluations and providing insights into the effectiveness of various defenses. The methodology involves evaluating 21 open-weight LLMs against nine tampering threats using standardized metrics, revealing that jailbreak-tuning poses significant risks and that many current defenses are inadequate. The findings support the goal of establishing a comprehensive evaluation system for LLM safety under tampering conditions.

When Autoregressive Consistency Hurts Safety Alignment
Authors: Bochen Lyu, Yiyang Jia, Xiaohao Cai, Zhanxing Zhu
First: 2026-06-02T19:32:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T19:32:23+00:00
Comments: 21 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety alignment in large language models (LLMs) is fragile in part because it is often shallow: fine-tuning mainly reshapes the model's behavior near the first few output tokens. We argue that this phenomenon can be understood through autoregressive consistency, the tendency of next-token prediction to preserve and extend the current response trajectory consistently. By analyzing the learning dynamics of safety alignment, we show that autoregressive consistency can concentrate alignment updates on early tokens, offering a mechanistic explanation for shallow safety alignment. The same mechanism also predicts a broader class of attacks on LLMs: attacks that induce harmful continuation states at arbitrary positions in the output trajectory. As a concrete example, we introduce random insertion attack, which inserts a short harmful span into an otherwise safe refusal trajectory and exploits autoregressive consistency to sustain the resulting harmful branch, thereby bypassing safety alignment. Notably, a short harmful span can redirect the generation to be harmful even after a long refusal prefix, highlighting autoregressive consistency as a potential broader failure mechanism. This suggests that safety alignment should also break harmful autoregressive consistency throughout the output trajectory. We therefore propose adversarial safety alignment, an initial framework based on worst-case harmful continuation states, and instantiate it with random worst-insertion training. Overall, our results suggest that autoregressive consistency should be treated as a central consideration in both safety alignment and attack design.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自回归一致性如何损害安全对齐
大型语言模型（LLMs）的安全对齐脆弱，部分原因在于其通常较浅：微调主要重塑模型在前几个输出标记附近的行为。我们认为，这一现象可以通过自回归一致性来理解，即下一个标记预测保持并延续当前响应轨迹的一致性倾向。通过分析安全对齐的学习动态，我们表明自回归一致性可以将对齐更新集中在早期标记上，为浅层安全对齐提供了机制解释。同样的机制也预测了一类更广泛的对LLMs的攻击：在输出轨迹的任意位置诱导有害延续状态的攻击。作为具体例子，我们引入随机插入攻击，该攻击将一个短的有害片段插入到一个原本安全的拒绝轨迹中，并利用自回归一致性来维持由此产生的有害分支，从而绕过安全对齐。值得注意的是，一个短的有害片段可以在长拒绝前缀后重新引导生成变得有害，突显自回归一致性作为潜在的更广泛失败机制。这表明安全对齐也应打破输出轨迹中有害的自回归一致性。因此，我们提出了对抗性安全对齐，这是一个基于最坏情况有害延续状态的初步框架，并通过随机最坏插入训练进行实例化。总体而言，我们的结果表明，自回归一致性应被视为安全对齐和攻击设计中的核心考虑。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the fragility of safety alignment in large language models (LLMs), which is often limited to the initial output tokens due to autoregressive consistency, a tendency that preserves the current response trajectory. Previous methods primarily focused on fine-tuning without adequately addressing the depth of safety alignment, leading to vulnerabilities that can be exploited through harmful continuation states. This paper contributes by proposing adversarial safety alignment, a framework that emphasizes the need to disrupt harmful autoregressive consistency throughout the output trajectory. The methodology involves analyzing learning dynamics and introducing random worst-insertion training to counteract shallow safety alignment. The proposed approach demonstrates improved resilience against attacks, suggesting that a more comprehensive treatment of autoregressive consistency can enhance both safety alignment and attack prevention in LLMs.

Black-box, Adaptive, Efficient, Transferable, Harmful, Applicable... Attacks Are All You Need to Break LLMs
Authors: Vincent Limbach, Jonas Dornbusch, David Lüdke, Stephan Günnemann, Leo Schwinn
First: 2026-06-02T13:39:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-02T13:39:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurately evaluating adversarial robustness is a longstanding challenge. A flawed attack design can inflate robustness estimates, making deployment risk assessment and defense comparison unreliable. Historically, standardized attacks such as AutoAttack have largely resolved this for image classifiers, providing a reliable evaluation baseline for systematic comparison across defenses. However, no equivalent exists for LLM jailbreak evaluation yet, where designing such an attack is considerably more difficult. A reliable attack must, among other things, be black-box compatible, applicable to arbitrary defense pipelines, and efficient, which no existing method jointly satisfies. We introduce Indirect Harm Optimization (IHO), a masked diffusion language model attacker trained via iterative preference optimization against a harmfulness judge, requiring only black-box access to the target. The same method can be used without modification as a strong adaptive attack on individual behaviors, or as an efficient amortized policy that transfers to held-out behaviors and unseen target models without fine-tuning. Even against layered defenses, such as a Circuit Breaker-trained model combined with an auxiliary detector, IHO improves attack success considerably over state-of-the-art approaches, without any defense-specific adaptation. Our results position IHO as a practical step toward the kind of standardized jailbreak evaluation that has improved reliability in the past. Code and models are available on GitHub and Hugging Face.
中文标题/摘要
标题：黑箱、自适应、高效、可转移、有害、适用……攻击是破解大型语言模型所需的一切
准确评估对抗鲁棒性一直是一个长期挑战。设计不当的攻击可能会夸大鲁棒性估计，使得部署风险评估和防御比较不可靠。历史上，标准化攻击如AutoAttack在图像分类器中基本解决了这一问题，为系统比较提供了可靠的评估基准。然而，目前尚无等效的LLM越狱评估标准，设计这样的攻击要困难得多。一个可靠的攻击必须在许多方面兼容黑箱，适用于任意防御管道，并且高效，而现有方法没有一个能够共同满足这些要求。我们引入了间接有害优化（IHO），这是一种通过迭代偏好优化训练的掩蔽扩散语言模型攻击者，仅需对目标进行黑箱访问。相同的方法可以在不修改的情况下作为对个体行为的强自适应攻击，或作为一种高效的摊销策略，能够转移到未见的目标模型和保留行为上，而无需微调。即使面对分层防御，如结合辅助检测器的电路断路器训练模型，IHO在攻击成功率上显著超越了最先进的方法，而无需任何特定于防御的适应。我们的结果将IHO定位为朝着标准化越狱评估迈出的实用一步，这在过去提高了可靠性。代码和模型可在GitHub和Hugging Face上获取。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the challenge of accurately evaluating adversarial robustness in large language models (LLMs), highlighting the inadequacies of existing attack designs that inflate robustness estimates and compromise risk assessments. Traditional methods, such as AutoAttack for image classifiers, lack a standardized equivalent for LLM jailbreak evaluations, where attack design is more complex due to the need for black-box compatibility and efficiency. The proposed Indirect Harm Optimization (IHO) method overcomes these issues by utilizing a masked diffusion language model attacker trained through iterative preference optimization, which operates effectively in a black-box setting and can adapt to various defense mechanisms without modification. The paper demonstrates that IHO significantly enhances attack success rates against layered defenses compared to state-of-the-art methods, thus contributing to the establishment of a reliable evaluation framework for LLMs. The methodology shows promise in achieving robust performance across different tasks and models, supporting the goal of standardized evaluation in this domain.
本研究解决了在大型语言模型（LLMs）中准确评估对抗鲁棒性的问题，而现有的图像分类器标准攻击方法并不适用。以往的方法由于无法在各种防御机制中实现黑箱兼容性和高效性，导致评估结果不可靠。所提出的间接危害优化（IHO）方法通过利用仅需黑箱访问的掩蔽扩散语言模型攻击者，克服了这些限制，使其既可以作为对特定行为的自适应攻击，也可以作为高效的策略转移到新模型。论文表明，IHO显著提高了对高级防御的攻击成功率，为建立大型语言模型越狱的标准化评估指标提供了有价值的工具，从而有助于更可靠地评估对抗鲁棒性。
D-Judge: Disrupting Multi-Turn Jailbreaks using Semantics-Preserving Output Rewriting
Authors: Huanli Gong, Zhipeng Wei, Yu Fu, Haz Sameen Shahgir, Ananya Gupta, Yue Dong, N. Benjamin Erichson
First: 2026-05-31T06:40:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-31T06:40:02+00:00
Comments: Proceedings of the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multi-turn jailbreak attacks pose a growing threat to large language model (LLM) safety because they exploit feedback from auxiliary judge models to iteratively refine prompts toward harmful goals. Existing defenses largely detect or block unsafe content at individual turns or at the final response, leaving the judge-driven refinement loop intact and allowing attackers to extract informative feedback from intermediate interactions. We introduce D-Judge, a semantics-preserving output rewriting defense that intervenes directly in this loop by rewriting the victim LLM's responses before they are evaluated by the attacker's judge. By misaligning the judge's feedback signal without changing the meaning of the original response, D-Judge derails the attacker's prompt-refinement process, causing subsequent queries to be optimized against a distorted signal of attack progress. To improve D-Judge's ability to produce such rewrites, we construct a dataset of semantically equivalent response pairs that induce different judge-assigned harmfulness scores, and use it for supervised fine-tuning followed by direct preference optimization. Experiments on HarmBench show that D-Judge reduces the success rate of state-of-the-art multi-turn jailbreaks while preserving performance on benign benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：D-Judge：通过语义保持输出重写来破坏多轮越狱攻击
多轮越狱攻击对大型语言模型（LLM）安全构成日益严重的威胁，因为它们利用辅助评判模型的反馈，迭代地优化提示以达到有害目标。现有防御主要在单个回合或最终响应中检测或阻止不安全内容，保持评判驱动的优化循环不变，使攻击者能够从中间交互中提取有用反馈。我们提出了D-Judge，这是一种语义保持输出重写防御，直接干预这一循环，通过在攻击者的评判之前重写受害者LLM的响应。通过在不改变原始响应含义的情况下使评判的反馈信号失真，D-Judge使攻击者的提示优化过程偏离轨道，导致后续查询针对扭曲的攻击进展信号进行优化。为了提高D-Judge生成此类重写的能力，我们构建了一个语义等价响应对的数据集，该数据集引发不同的评判分配有害性评分，并用于监督微调，随后进行直接偏好优化。在HarmBench上的实验表明，D-Judge在保持良性基准性能的同时，降低了最先进的多轮越狱攻击的成功率。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the increasing threat of multi-turn jailbreak attacks on large language models (LLMs), which exploit feedback from auxiliary judge models to refine harmful prompts. Previous methods primarily focused on detecting or blocking unsafe content at individual turns or final responses, failing to disrupt the iterative feedback loop that attackers utilize. The proposed D-Judge approach differs by rewriting the LLM's responses before they reach the judge, thus misaligning the feedback signal while preserving the original meaning. This method effectively disrupts the prompt-refinement process of attackers. The paper contributes a novel defense mechanism that utilizes a dataset of semantically equivalent response pairs to enhance the rewriting process through supervised fine-tuning and preference optimization. Experimental results on HarmBench demonstrate that D-Judge significantly reduces the success rate of advanced multi-turn jailbreaks while maintaining performance on benign tasks.
本研究针对多轮越狱攻击对大型语言模型（LLMs）日益增长的威胁，这些攻击利用辅助评估模型的反馈来逐步优化有害提示。以往的方法主要集中在检测或阻止单个回合或最终响应中的不安全内容，未能干扰攻击者利用的迭代反馈循环。提出的D-Judge方法通过在评估之前重写LLM的响应，从而使反馈信号失去一致性，同时保持原始含义，这与现有方法有所不同。该方法的动机明确，因为它直接针对攻击机制。论文贡献了一种新颖的防御机制和一个用于监督微调的语义等价响应对数据集。实验结果表明，D-Judge在HarmBench上显著降低了先进多轮越狱攻击的成功率，同时在良性任务上保持了性能。
Quality-Diversity Evolution for Discovering Diverse Vulnerabilities in LLM Safety
Authors: Subhadip Mitra
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-05-30T16:40:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-30T16:40:24+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 6 figures. Accepted at the ICLR 2026 Workshop on Agents in the Wild (AIWILD)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Current approaches to LLM adversarial testing suffer from coverage gaps: manual red-teaming does not scale, LLM-as-attacker methods exhibit mode collapse, and gradient-based approaches produce uninterpretable gibberish. We introduce a quality-diversity evolutionary framework that operates at the semantic level, evolving interpretable attack strategies rather than token sequences. Using MAP-Elites, we maintain a diverse archive of attacks across behavioral dimensions (strategy type, encoding method, prompt length). In experiments across GPT-4o-mini, Claude 3.5 Sonnet, Gemini 2.0 Flash, and an open-weight coding model (Devstral-small-2), we discover distinct vulnerability profiles: GPT-4o-mini is vulnerable to hypothetical and multi-turn framing combined with ROT13 encoding (fitness 0.8), Gemini to direct attacks with ROT13 and multi-turn with Leetspeak (0.8), while Claude shows uniformly ambiguous responses across all strategies (max 0.4). The semantic representation produces interpretable attacks that reveal systematic, model-specific weaknesses, providing actionable insights for improving LLM safety and a reproducible baseline for evaluating future frontier models. Code and experiment artifacts are released at https://github.com/bassrehab/red-queen.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于发现LLM安全性中多样化脆弱性的质量-多样性进化
当前LLM对抗测试的方法存在覆盖缺口：手动红队无法扩展，LLM作为攻击者的方法表现出模式崩溃，基于梯度的方法产生不可解释的乱码。我们引入了一种在语义层面上操作的质量-多样性进化框架，进化可解释的攻击策略而非令牌序列。通过MAP-Elites，我们维护一个跨行为维度（策略类型、编码方法、提示长度）的多样化攻击档案。在对GPT-4o-mini、Claude 3.5 Sonnet、Gemini 2.0 Flash和一个开放权重编码模型（Devstral-small-2）的实验中，我们发现了不同的脆弱性特征：GPT-4o-mini对假设性和多轮框架结合ROT13编码（适应度0.8）脆弱，Gemini对直接攻击使用ROT13和多轮使用Leetspeak（0.8）脆弱，而Claude在所有策略中表现出均匀模糊的响应（最大0.4）。语义表示产生可解释的攻击，揭示系统性、模型特定的弱点，为改善LLM安全性提供可操作的见解，并为评估未来前沿模型提供可重复的基线。代码和实验文物已发布在https://github.com/bassrehab/red-queen。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the limitations of current methods for adversarial testing of large language models (LLMs), which include manual red-teaming that does not scale, mode collapse in LLM-as-attacker approaches, and the generation of uninterpretable outputs through gradient-based methods. The proposed quality-diversity evolutionary framework operates at the semantic level, evolving interpretable attack strategies instead of mere token sequences, thus overcoming the identified shortcomings of existing techniques. This approach is well-motivated as it aims to enhance the safety of LLMs by providing a diverse archive of attacks across various behavioral dimensions. The methodology employs MAP-Elites to systematically discover distinct vulnerability profiles in models such as GPT-4o-mini, Claude 3.5 Sonnet, and Gemini 2.0 Flash, achieving notable performance metrics, including a fitness score of 0.8 for specific attack strategies. The findings reveal model-specific weaknesses and provide actionable insights for improving LLM safety, establishing a reproducible baseline for future evaluations of advanced models.
本研究解决了当前大型语言模型（LLM）对抗测试方法的局限性，包括手动红队导致的覆盖不足、LLM作为攻击者的方法出现的模式崩溃，以及基于梯度的方法生成的不可解释输出。所提出的质量-多样性进化框架通过在语义层面上进化可解释的攻击策略，而不仅仅关注于标记序列，从而有效地解决了这些问题。本文的贡献在于引入了一种系统的方法，维护跨各种行为维度的攻击策略的多样化档案，从而发现了GPT-4o-mini、Gemini 2.0 Flash和Claude 3.5 Sonnet等模型的不同脆弱性特征。该方法采用MAP-Elites评估和进化这些策略，在识别脆弱性方面取得了显著的性能，例如GPT-4o-mini和Gemini的适应度得分为0.8，而Claude的最高得分为0.4，从而为增强LLM安全性提供了可行的见解，并为未来评估建立了可重复的基线。
The Refusal--Compliance Tradeoff: A Large-Scale Safety Behavior Audit of Large Language Models
Authors: Alif Al Hasan, Sumon Biswas
First: 2026-05-06T20:35:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-30T15:07:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Refusal rates are a poor proxy for LLM safety, i.e., a model may over-refuse benign prompts while still complying with harmful ones. We audit both failure modes across 21 open-weight LLMs on four safety benchmarks (OR-Bench, XSTest, ToxiGen, BOLD), using a composition adjustment to isolate model sensitivity from dataset toxicity confounds. We report three findings. First, models adopt fundamentally different calibration strategies: conservative ecosystems such as Llama suppress unsafe outputs at the cost of elevated over-refusals, while permissive ecosystems such as DeepSeek and Qwen preserve helpfulness but tolerate higher harmful compliance. Second, demographic protection is unequal: models over-protect prominent racial and religious groups, frequently refusing even benign prompts about them, while providing substantially weaker protection against disability-targeted attacks. Third, refusal and compliance tendencies are stable within model families across generations and scales, suggesting that post-training objectives shape safety behavior more than architecture. Our results call for joint, demographically-aware, and multi-judge safety evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：拒绝与合规权衡：大规模语言模型的安全行为审计
拒绝率是大型语言模型安全性的不良代理，即模型可能对良性提示过度拒绝，同时仍然对有害提示合规。我们在四个安全基准（OR-Bench、XSTest、ToxiGen、BOLD）上审计了21个开放权重的LLM，使用组合调整来隔离模型敏感性与数据集毒性混淆。我们报告了三个发现。首先，模型采用根本不同的校准策略：保守生态系统如Llama在提高过度拒绝的代价下抑制不安全输出，而宽松生态系统如DeepSeek和Qwen则保持有用性但容忍更高的有害合规。其次，人口保护不平等：模型过度保护显著的种族和宗教群体，频繁拒绝关于它们的良性提示，而对残疾目标攻击提供的保护则明显较弱。第三，拒绝和合规倾向在模型家族内跨代和规模保持稳定，表明后训练目标比架构更能塑造安全行为。我们的结果呼吁进行联合、关注人口统计和多评审的安全评估。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the inadequacy of refusal rates as indicators of safety in large language models (LLMs), highlighting that models may refuse benign prompts while complying with harmful ones. Previous methods primarily focused on refusal rates without considering the context of prompts, leading to misleading assessments of model safety. The proposed approach involves a large-scale audit of 21 open-weight LLMs across four safety benchmarks, employing a composition adjustment to differentiate model sensitivity from dataset toxicity. This methodology reveals that models exhibit varying calibration strategies, with conservative models like Llama prioritizing safety at the expense of higher refusal rates, while permissive models like DeepSeek maintain helpfulness but allow for more harmful compliance. The findings indicate that demographic protection is inconsistent, with models over-protecting certain groups while neglecting others, and that safety behaviors are stable across model families, suggesting that post-training objectives significantly influence safety outcomes. The contributions of this study advocate for a more nuanced, demographically-aware evaluation of LLM safety.
本研究解决了拒绝率作为大型语言模型（LLMs）安全性指标的不足，强调模型可能会对良性提示过度拒绝，而对有害提示则表现出顺从。以往的方法未能有效区分模型敏感性和数据集毒性，导致安全行为评估误导。所提出的方法利用组合调整对21个开放权重的LLMs进行审计，涵盖四个安全基准，揭示模型采用不同的校准策略，并表现出不平等的人口保护。该研究有助于理解模型家族内拒绝和顺从倾向的稳定性，并强调需要更细致、考虑人口因素的安全评估。该方法论表明，安全行为更多地受到后训练目标的影响，而非模型架构，在安全任务上取得了重要的见解。
How Reliable Are AI Attackers Against a Fixed Vulnerable Target? A 400-Run Empirical Study of LLM Penetration Testing Consistency
Authors: Galip Tolga Erdem
First: 2026-05-28T15:39:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-28T15:39:43+00:00
Comments: 41 pages, 7 figures. Code and 400-run dataset: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20421592
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) can autonomously conduct multi-stage cyber attacks, but the consistency of their offensive behavior under repeated trials remains unstudied. This work presents the first large-scale empirical measurement of LLM attack consistency: 400 autonomous penetration testing runs (4 models, 100 each) against an identical honeypot hosting OWASP Juice Shop and two additional vulnerable services, holding prompt, orchestrator, and target constant. No model emitted a content refusal that survived the orchestrator's one-shot authorization re-prompt at iterations 0-1. Claude Sonnet 4's API calls did encounter upstream service unavailability - 91 of 1,135 calls returned HTTP 529 overloaded_error during a documented Anthropic capacity event, truncating 39 of 100 Claude runs. An earlier draft catalogued these as safety refusals; on full-log audit they are upstream API failures, not model-level refusals. Despite this, Claude achieved full exploitation in 61 of 100 runs; Gemini 2.5 Flash-Lite in 85; GPT-4o-mini in 56 while deploying 98 unique attack strategies; qwen2.5-coder:14b in 25. Failure modes are model-distinctive: Claude through API truncation (39 runs), qwen through premature completion (52), GPT-4o-mini through iteration-budget exhaustion (23). Cross-service credential reuse appeared only in configurations retaining the most conversation history (qwen 57%, GPT-4o-mini 49%, cloud models 0% on 5-exchange windows). Cross-model exploitation rate differences are statistically significant (p < 0.001) with large effect sizes; qwen vs. Gemini SQL injection rates differ at Cohen's h = 1.12. First-exploit timing fell within a 15-30 second wall-clock range. To our knowledge, this is the first study to measure autonomous LLM attack behavior at N=100 per model across a multi-service target.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AI攻击者对固定脆弱目标的可靠性如何？一项400次运行的LLM渗透测试一致性实证研究
大型语言模型（LLMs）可以自主进行多阶段网络攻击，但其在重复试验中的攻击行为一致性尚未研究。本研究首次大规模实证测量LLM攻击一致性：对一个托管OWASP Juice Shop和两个额外脆弱服务的相同蜜罐进行400次自主渗透测试（4个模型，各100次），保持提示、协调者和目标不变。在迭代0-1中，没有模型发出经过协调者一次性授权重新提示后仍然有效的内容拒绝。Claude Sonnet 4的API调用确实遇到了上游服务不可用的情况——在一次记录的Anthropic容量事件中，1,135次调用中有91次返回HTTP 529 overloaded_error，截断了100次Claude运行中的39次。早期草稿将这些列为安全拒绝；经过完整日志审计后，它们被认定为上游API故障，而非模型级拒绝。尽管如此，Claude在100次运行中实现了61次完全利用；Gemini 2.5 Flash-Lite为85次；GPT-4o-mini为56次，同时部署了98种独特的攻击策略；qwen2.5-coder:14b为25次。失败模式具有模型特征：Claude通过API截断（39次运行），qwen通过过早完成（52次），GPT-4o-mini通过迭代预算耗尽（23次）。跨服务凭证重用仅出现在保留最多对话历史的配置中（qwen 57%，GPT-4o-mini 49%，云模型在5次交换窗口中为0%）。跨模型利用率差异具有统计显著性（p < 0.001），效应量大；qwen与Gemini的SQL注入率差异为Cohen's h = 1.12。首次利用的时间落在15-30秒的实际时间范围内。据我们所知，这是首个测量自主LLM攻击行为的研究，N=100每个模型，跨多个服务目标。
Summary / 总结
This research investigates the consistency of large language models (LLMs) in conducting cyber attacks, an area that has not been previously studied despite the increasing use of LLMs for penetration testing. Previous methods lacked empirical measurement of attack consistency across multiple trials, leading to uncertainties in their reliability. The proposed approach involves a large-scale empirical study with 400 autonomous penetration testing runs against a fixed honeypot, maintaining constant conditions across different models. The findings reveal significant differences in exploitation rates and failure modes among the models, with Claude achieving full exploitation in 61% of runs and qwen2.5-coder:14b only in 25%. This study contributes to understanding LLM attack behavior and provides a foundation for future research on the reliability of AI-driven cyber attacks, demonstrating statistically significant differences in performance across models and highlighting the need for further exploration in this domain.
